# Onkelos Exodus

תרגום אונקלוס על שמות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus)

התרגום המרכזי לתורה שהתקבל בתלמוד כתרגום סמכותי; בתקופת התלמוד נקרא בבית הכנסת לצד קריאת התורה, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the Torah accepted in the Talmud as authoritative; read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Onkelos/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sifsei Chachomim Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 (Segments: 1210, License: CC-BY-NC)

Version (En): Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 [with Onkelos translation] (Segments: 1210)


## Chapter 1

###### Onkelos Exodus 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:1)


וְאִלֵין שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דְעַלוּ לְמִצְרָיִם עִם יַעֲקֹב גְבַר וֶאֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵהּ עַלוּ:


These are the names of the B’nei Yisrael who came to Egypt. Each man with his household who came with Yaakov.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:2)


רְאוּבֵן שִׁמְעוֹן לֵוִי וִיהוּדָה:


Reuvein, Shimon, Leivi and Yehudah.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:3)


יִשָּׂשׂכָר זְבוּלֻן וּבִנְיָמִין:


Yissachar, Zevulun, and Binyamin.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:4)


דָן וְנַפְתָּלִי גָד וְאָשֵׁר:


Don, Naftali, Gad and Asher.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:5)


וַהֲוָה כָּל נַפְשָׁתָא נַפְקֵי יַרְכָּא דְיַעֲקֹב שַׁבְעִין נַפְשָׁן וְיוֹסֵף **דַהֲוָה** בְּמִצְרָיִם:


All the souls that emanated from the loins of Yaakov, [numbered] seventy souls, [including] Yoseif who was [**already**] in Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:6)


וּמִית יוֹסֵף וְכָל אַחוֹהִי וְכֹל דָרָא הַהוּא:


Yoseif died. All his brothers and all that generation [also died.]


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:7)


וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל נְפִישׁוּ וְאִתְיַלָדוּ וּסְגִיאוּ וּתְקִיפוּ לַחֲדָא לַחֲדָא וְאִתְמְלִיאַת אַרְעָא מִנְהוֹן:


The B’nei Yisrael were fruitful and prolific, and their population multiplied. They were exceedingly mighty, and the land was filled with them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:8)


וְקָם מַלְכָּא חַדְתָּא עַל מִצְרָיִם דְלָא **מְקַיֵם גְזֵרַת** יוֹסֵף:


A new king came into power over Egypt, who did not know [**fulfill**] Yoseif[**’s decree**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:9)


וַאֲמַר לְעַמֵהּ הָא עַמָא בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל סָגָן וְתַקִיפִין מִנָנָא:


He said to his people, “Behold, the people of the B’nei Yisrael are becoming too numerous and strong for us.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:10)


הָבוּ נִתְחַכַּם **לְהוֹן** דִלְמָא יִסְג**וּן** וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יְעַרְעִנָנָא קְרָב וְיִתּוֹסְפ**וּן** אַף **אִנוּן** עַל סַנְאָנָא וִיגִיחוּן בָּנָא **קְרָב וְיִסְקוּן** מִן אַרְעָא:


Come let us deal wisely with him [**them**]. Lest he [**they**] increase so much, that, if there is war, he [**they**] will join our enemies and fight [**a war**] against us, driving us [**and they will go up**] from the land.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:11)


וּמַנִיאוּ **עֲלֵיהוֹן** שִׁלְטוֹנִין **מַבְאִישִׁין** בְּדִיל לְעַנוֹאֵ**הוֹן בְּפָלְחָנְהוֹן** וּבְנוֹ קִרְוֵי בֵית אוֹצָרָא לְפַרְעֹה יָת פִּיתוֹם וְיָת רַעַמְסֵס:


They appointed conscription [**cruel**] officers over him [**them**] to oppress him [**them**] with their burdens [**work**]. He [B’nei Yisrael] built supply cities for Pharaoh, Pisom and Ramseis.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:12)


וּכְמָא דִמְעַנַן **לְהוֹן** כֵּן סָגָן וְכֵן **תַּקְפִין** וַעֲקַת לְמִצְרָאֵי מִן קֳדָם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


But the more [the Egyptians] oppressed him [**them**], the more [the B’nei Yisrael] increased and spread [**became strong**]. [The Egyptians] came to loathe the B’nei Yisrael.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:13)


וְאַפְלָחוּ מִצְרָאֵי יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּקַשְׁיוּ:


The Egyptians enslaved the B’nei Yisrael, with body-breaking labor.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:14)


וְאַמְרָרוּ יָת חַיֵיהוֹן בְּפָלְחָנָא קָשְׁיָא בְּטִינָא וּבְלִבְנִין וּבְכָל פָּלְחָנָא בְּחַקְלָא יָת כָּל פָּלְחָנְהוֹן דְאַפְלָחוּ בְהוֹן בְּקַשְׁיוּ:


They made their lives bitter with harsh labor involving mortar and bricks, and all kinds of work in the fields. All the work they made them do [was intended] to break them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:15)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְחַיָתָא יְהוּדַיָתָא דְשׁוּם חָדָא שִׁפְרָה וְשּׁוּם תִּנְיֵתָא פּוּעָה:


The king of Egypt spoke to the Hebrew midwives. The name of one of them was Shifrah, and the name of the other was Puah.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:16)


וַאֲמַר כַּד תֶּהֶוְיָן מוֹלְדָן יָת יְהוּדַיָתָא וְתֶחְזְיָן עַל מַתְבְּרָא אִם בַּר הוּא תִּקְטְלַן יָתֵהּ וְאִם בְּרַתָּא הִיא ותְקַיְמִנַהּ:


He said, “When you deliver Hebrew women, you must look at the birthstool. If it is a boy, kill him; but if it is a girl, let her live.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:17)


וּדְחִילָא חַיָתָא מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְלָא עֲבָדָא כְּמָא דְמַלִיל עִמְהֶן מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרָיִם וְקַיָמָא יָת בְּנַיָא:


The midwives [however] feared [**from before**] Elohim and did not do as the Egyptian king had told them, and they kept the infant boys alive.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:18)


וּקְרָא מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְחַיָתָא וַאֲמַר לְהֶן מָה דֵין עֲבַדְתּוּן יָת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין וְקַיֵמְתּוּן יָת בְּנַיָא:


The king of Egypt called for the midwives and said to them, “Why did you do this? You let the infant boys live.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:19)


וַאֲמָרָא חַיָתָא לְפַרְעֹה אֲרֵי לָא כִנְשַׁיָא מִצְרְיָתָא יְהוּדַיָתָא אֲרֵי חֲכִּימָן אִנוּן עַד לָא עַלַת לְוָתְהֶן חַיָתָא וִילִידָן:


The midwives said to Pharaoh, “The Hebrew women are not like the Egyptians. They know how to deliver. Even before a midwife gets to them, they have already given birth.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:20)


וְאוֹטֵיב יְיָ לְחַיָתָא וּסְגִי עַמָא וּתְקִיפוּ לַחֲדָא:


Elohim was good to the midwives, and the people increased and were very mighty.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:21)


וַהֲוָה כַּד דְחִילָא חַיָתָא מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וַעֲבַד לְהוֹן בָּתִּין:


Because the midwives feared [**from before**] God, He gave them houses.


###### Onkelos Exodus 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/1:22)


וּפַקִיד פַּרְעֹה לְכָל עַמֵהּ לְמֵימָר כָּל בְּרָא דְיִתְיְלִיד **לִיהוּדָאֵי** בְּנַהֲרָא תִּרְמֻנֵהּ וְכָל בְּרַתָּא תְּקַיְמוּן:


Pharaoh then commanded all his people, saying, “Every boy who is born [**to the Hebrews**] must be thrown into the river; but every girl shall be allowed to live.”



## Chapter 2

###### Onkelos Exodus 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:1)


וַאֲזַל גַבְרָא מִדְבֵית לֵוִי וּנְסִיב יָת בַּת לֵוִי:


A man of the house of Levi went and married the daughter of Levi.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:2)


וְעַדִיאַת אִתְּתָא וִילִידַת בָּר וַחֲזַת יָתֵהּ אֲרֵי טַב הוּא וְאַטְמַרְתֵּהּ תְּלָתָא יַרְחִין:


The woman conceived and bore a son. She saw that he was [exceptionally] good, and she kept him hidden for three months.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:3)


וְלָא יְכֵילַת עוֹד לְאַטְמָרוּתֵהּ וּנְסֵיבַת לֵהּ תֵּבוּתָא דְגוֹמֶא וְחַפָּתַהּ בְּחֵמָרָא וּבְזִפְתָּא וְשַׁוִיאַת בַּהּ יָת רַבְיָא וְשַׁוִיתַהּ בְּיַעֲרָא עַל כֵּיף נַהֲרָא:


When she could no longer hide him, she took a papyrus box and coated it with clay and tar. She placed the child in it, and placed it in the reeds near the bank of the river.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:4)


וְאִתְעַתָּדַת אֲחָתֵהּ מֵרָחִיק לְמִידַע מָה יִתְעֲבֵד לֵהּ:


[The child’s] sister stood herself at a distance to see what would happen to him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:5)


וּנְחָתַת בַּת פַּרְעֹה לְמִסְחֵי עַל נַהֲרָא וְעוּלֵימָתָהָא מְהַלְכָן עַל כֵּיף נַהֲרָא וַחֲזַת יָת תֵּבוּתָא בְּגוֹ יַעֲרָא וְאוֹשֵׁטַת יָת אַמְתַהּ וּנְסֵבְתַהּ:


Pharaoh’s daughter went down to bathe by the river, while her maids walked along the river’s edge. She saw the box among the reeds and sent her maid and she fetched it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:6)


וּפְתָחַת וַחֲזַת יָת רַבְיָא וְהָא עוּלֵימָא בָּכֵי וְחָסַת עֲלוֹהִי וַאֲמֶרֶת מִבְּנֵי יְהוּדָאֵי הוּא דֵין:


She opened it and saw the child, and behold a boy was crying. She took pity on it, and said, “This is one of the Hebrew boys.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:7)


וַאֲמֶרֶת אֲחָתֵהּ לְבַת פַרְעֹה הַאֵיזִיל וְאִקְרֵי לִיךְ אִתְּתָא מֵינִקְתָּא מִן יְהוּדַיָתָא וְתוֹנִיק לִיךְ יָת רַבְיָא:


[The infant’s] sister said to Pharaoh’s daughter, “Shall I go and call to you a nursing [mother] from the Hebrew women to nurse the child for you?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:8)


וַאֲמֶרֶת לַהּ בַּת פַּרְעֹה אֵיזִילִי וַאֲזָלַת עוּלֶמְתָּא וּקְרַת יָת אִמֵהּ דְרַבְיָא:


“Go,” said Pharaoh’s daughter to her. The young girl went and called the child’s mother.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:9)


וַאֲמֶרֶת לַהּ בַּת פַּרְעֹה אוֹבִילִי יָת רַבְיָא הָדֵין וְאוֹנְקִיהִי לִי וַאֲנָא אֶתֵּן יָת אַגְרָךְ וּנְסֵיבַת אִתְּתָא יָת רַבְיָא וְאוֹנִקְתֵּהּ:


Pharaoh’s daughter said to her [the child’s mother], “Take this child and nurse him for me, and I will pay your fee.” The woman took the child and nursed it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:10)


וּרְבָא רַבְיָא וְאַיְתִיתֵהּ לְבַת פַּרְעֹה וַהֲוָה לָהּ לְבָר וּקְרַת שְׁמֵהּ משֶׁה וַאֲמֶרֶת אֲרֵי מִן מַיָא שְׁחַלְתֵּהּ:


When the child grew up, she brought him to Pharaoh’s daughter, and he became her son. She named him Moshe, for she said, “I drew him from the water.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:11)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמַיָא הָאִנוּן וּרְבָא משֶׁה וּנְפַק לְוָת אַחוֹהִי וַחֲזָא בְּפָלְחַנְהוֹן וַחֲזָא גְבַר מִצְרִי מָחֵי לִגְבַר יְהוּדִי מֵאַחוֹהִי:


It was in those days when Moshe was grown that he [began] to go out to his brethren, and he saw their burdens [hard labor]. [One day] he saw an Egyptian beating one of his Hebrew brethren.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:12)


וְאִתְפְּנֵי לְכָא וּלְכָא וַחֲזָא אֲרֵי לֵית גַבְרָא וּמְחָא יָת מִצְרָאָה וְטַמְרֵהּ בְּחָלָא:


He [Moshe] looked all around and [when] he saw that no man was there [watching], he killed the Egyptian and hid his body in the sand.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:13)


וּנְפַק בְּיוֹמָא תִנְיָנָא וְהָא תְּרֵין גֻבְרִין יְהוּדָאִין נָצָן וַאֲמַר לְחַיָבָא לְמָא אַתְּ מָחֵי לְחַבְרָךְ:


He went out the next day, and behold two Hebrew men were quarreling. And he said to the wicked one, “Why are you beating your friend?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:14)


וַאֲמַר מָן שַׁוְיָךְ לִגְבַר רַב וְדַיָן עֲלָנָא הַלְמִקְטְלִי אַתְּ אָמַר כְּמָא דִקְטַלְתָּ יָת מִצְרָאָה וּדְחִיל משֶׁה וַאֲמַר **בְּקוּשְׁטָא** אִתְיְדַע פִּתְגָמָא:


He [the wicked one] said, “Who made you a man, officer and judge over us? Do you intend to kill me as you killed the Egyptian?” Moshe was frightened, and he said, “So [**Certainly**] the matter is known.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:15)


וּשְׁמַע פַּרְעֹה יָת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין וּבְעָא לְמִקְטַל יָת משֶׁה וַעֲרַק משֶׁה מִן קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה וִיתֵיב בְּאַרְעָא דְמִדְיָן וִיתֵיב עַל בֵּירָא:


Pharaoh heard about the matter, and he planned to kill Moshe. Moshe fled from Pharaoh, and resided in the land of Midian, and he sat [lived] near the well.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:16)


וּלְ**רַבָּא** דְמִדְיָן שְׁבַע בְּנָן וְאָתָאָה וּדְלָאָה וּמְלָאָה יָת רְהָטַיָא לְאַשְׁקָאָה עֲנָא דַאֲבוּהֵן:


The priest [**minister**] of Midian had seven daughters. They came to draw water [from the well] and fill the troughs to water their father’s sheep.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:17)


וַאֲתוֹ רָעַיָא וּטְרָדוּנוּן וְקָם משֶׁה וּפְרָקִנוּן וְאַשְׁקֵי יָת עַנְהֵן:


Then the shepherds came and chased them away. Moshe got up and came to their aid, and then watered their sheep.


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:18)


וַאֲתָאָה לְוַת רְעוּאֵל אֲבוּהֵן וַאֲמַר מָה דֵין אוֹחִיתוּן לְמֵיתֵי יוֹמָא דֵין:


When they came to Reu’eil, their father, he said [to them], “How did you get to come [home] so early today?”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:19)


וַאֲמָרָא גַבְרָא מִצְרָאָה שֵׁזְבָנָא מִיַד רָעַיָא וְאַף מִדְלָא דְלָא לָנָא וְאַשְׁקֵי יָת עָנָא:


They said, “An Egyptian rescued us from the hand of the shepherds; and he also drew [water] for us, and watered the sheep.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:20)


וַאֲמַר לִבְנָתֵיהּ וְאָן הוּא לְמָא דְנַן שְׁבַקְתִּין יָת גַבְרָא קְרַן לֵהּ וְיֵכוּל לַחְמָא:


He said to his daughters, “And where is he? Why did you abandon the man? Call him and let him eat bread.”

Moshe agreed to reside with the man, and he gave Moshe his daughter Tzipporah.

וִילִדַת בַּר וּקְרָא יָת שְׁמֵהּ גֵרְשֹׁם אֲרֵי אֲמַר דַיָר הֲוֵיתִי בְּאַרַע נוּכְרָאָה:


When she gave birth to a son, he named him Gershom, for he said, “I have been a foreigner in a strange land.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 2:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:23)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמַיָא סַגִיאַיָא הָאִנוּן וּמִית מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם וְאִתְנָחוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן פָּלְחָנָא **דַהֲוָה קְשֵׁי עֲלֵיהוֹן** וּזְעִיקוּ וּסְלֵיקָת קְבֶלְתְּהוֹן **לָקֳדָם** יְיָ מִן פָּלְחָנָא:


A long time passed and the king of Egypt died. The B’nei Yisrael moaned because of their enslavement [**which was hard upon them**], and they cried. Their plea about their enslavement went up to [**before**] God.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:24)


**וּשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ** יָת קְבֶלְתְּהוֹן וּדְכִיר יְיָ יָת קַיָמֵהּ דְעִם אַבְרָהָם דְעִם יִצְחָק וּדְעִם יַעֲקֹב:


Elohim heard their groaning [**was heard before Elohim**] and Elohim remembered His covenant with Avraham, with Yitzchok and with Yaakov.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/2:25)


**וּגְלִי קְדָם יְיָ שִׁעְבּוּדָא** דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **וַאֲמַר בְּמֵימְרֵהּ לְמִפְרַקְהוֹן** יְיָ:


Elohim saw [**The enslavement of**] the B’nei Yisrael [**became revealed before Elohim**], and Elohim took knowledge of them [**said with His Word that He would deliver them**].



## Chapter 3

###### Onkelos Exodus 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:1)


וּמשֶׁה הֲוָה רָעֵי יָת עָנָא דְיִתְרוֹ חֲמוֹהִי **רַבָּא** דְמִדְיָן וּדְבַר יָת עָנָא לְבָתַר **שְׁפַר רַעֲיָא** לְמַדְבְּרָא וְאָתָא לְטוּרָא **דְאִתְגְלִי עֲלוֹהִי יְקָרָא** דַיְיָ לְחוֹרֵב:


Moshe tended the sheep of his father-in-law Yisro, priest [**minister**] of Midian. He led the sheep to the edge of the wilderness [**to the best pastures**] and he came to the mountain [**on which was the revealed the Glory**] of God, in the area of Choreiv.


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:2)


**וְאִתְגְלִי** מַלְאָכָא דַיְיָ לֵהּ **בְּשַׁלְהוֹבִית** אֶשָׁתָא מִגוֹ אַסָנָא וַחֲזָא וְהָא אַסָנָא בָּעֵר בְּאֶשְָׁתָא וַאֲסָנָא לֵיתוֹהִי מִתְאָכִיל:


An angel of Adonoy appeared [**became revealed**] to him [Moshe] in the heart [**a flame**] of a fire in the midst of a thorn-bush. He looked and behold the bush was on fire, but the bush was not being consumed.


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:3)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה אִתְפְּנֵי כְעַן וְאֶחֱזֵי יָת חָזְוָנָא רַבָּא הָדֵין מָא דֵין לָא מִתּוֹקַד אַסָנָא:


Moshe said, “I must turn aside and see [investigate] this great sight. Why doesn’t the bush burn?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:4)


וַחֲזָא יְיָ אֲרֵי אִתְפְּנֵי לְמֶחֱזֵי וּקְרָא לֵהּ יְיָ מִגוֹ אַסָנָא וַאֲמַר משֶׁה משֶׁה וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא:


When Adonoy saw that [Moshe] turned aside to see, Elohim called to him from the midst of the thorn-bush, and said, “Moshe, Moshe.’” He [Moshe] said, “Here I am.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:5)


וַאֲמַר לָא תִקְרַב הַלְכָא שְׁרִי סֵינָךְ מֵעַל רִגְלָךְ אֲרֵי אַתְרָא דְאַתְּ קָאִים עֲלוֹהִי **אֲתַר** קַדִישׁ הוּא:


He [God] said, “Do not come any closer. Take your shoes off your feet, because the place upon which you are standing is [**a**] holy ground [**place**].”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:6)


וַאֲמַר אֲנָא אֱלָהָא דְאָבוּךְ אֱלָהָא דְאַבְרָהָם אֱלָהָא דְיִצְחָק וֵאלָהָא דְיַעֲקֹב וּכְבֵשִׁנּוּן משֶׁה לְאַפּוֹהִי אֲרֵי דָחִיל מִלְאִסְתַּכָּלָא **בְּצֵית יְקָרָא דַ**יְיָ:


He [God] then said, “I am the God of your father, the God of Avraham, the God of Yitzchok, and the God of Yaakov.” Moshe hid his face, for he was afraid to look at [**the side of the Glory of**] Elohim.


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:7)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ **מִגְלָא גְלִי קֳדָמַי** יָת שִׁעְבּוּד עַמִי דִי בְמִצְרָיִם וְיָת **קְבֶלְתְּהוֹן שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָמַי** מִן קֳדָם מַפְלְחֵיהוֹן אֲרֵי **גְלֵי קָדָמַי** יָת כֵּיבֵיהוֹן:


Adonoy said, “I have indeed seen the suffering of My people that are in Egypt [**has indeed been revealed before Me**]. I have heard how they [**Their**] cry out [**is heard before Me**] because [of the harshness] of their slave-masters, and I am aware of their pain [**has become revealed before Me**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:8)


**וְאִתְגְלֵיתִי** לְשֵׁזָבוּתְהוֹן מִידָא דְמִצְרָאֵי וּלְאַסָקוּתְהוֹן מִן אַרְעָא הַהִיא לְאַרְעָא טָבָא וּפַתְיָא לְאַרְעָא **עָבְדָא** חֲלָב וּדְבָשׁ לַאֲתַר כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וְחִתָּאֵי וֶאֱמוֹרָאֵי וּפְרִזָאֵי וְחִוָאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי:


I have descended [**become revealed**] to free them from the hand of Egypt, and to bring them up from that land to a good, spacious land, to a land flowing with [**producing**] milk and honey; to the territory of the Canaanites, the Chittites, the Emorites, the Perizites, the Chivites and the Yevusites.


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:9)


וּכְעַן הָא קְבֵילַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עָלַת **קֳדָמָי וְאַף גְלֵי קֳדָמַי** יָת דוֹחֲקָא דִי מִצְרָאֵי דָחֲקִין לְהוֹן:


Now, behold, the cry of the B’nei Yisrael has come to [**before**] Me. I have also seen the oppression with which the Egyptians oppress them [**is also revealed before Me**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:10)


וּכְעַן **אִיתָא** וַאֲשַׁלְחִנָּךְ לְוַת פַּרְעֹה וְאַפֵּיק יָת עַמִי בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִצְרָיִם:


Now go [**come**]. I am sending you to Pharaoh, and you will bring My people, the B’nei Yisrael, out of Egypt.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:11)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה **קֳדָם** יְיָ מָן אֲנָא אֲרֵי אֵזִיל לְוָת פַּרְעֹה וַאֲרֵי אַפֵּיק יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִצְרָיִם:


Moshe said to [**before**] Elohim, “Who am I that I should go to Pharaoh? Am I able to bring the B’nei Yisrael out of Egypt?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:12)


וַאֲמַר אֲרֵי יְהֵי **מֵימְרִי בְסַעְדָךְ** וְדֵין לָךְ אָתָא אֲרֵי אֲנָא שְׁלַחְתָּךְ בְּאַפָּקוּתָךְ יָת עַמָא מִמִצְרַיִם תִּפְלְחוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ עַל טוּרָא הָדֵין:


He [God] said, “Because I will be with you [**My Word will be your support**]. This will be the proof that I have sent you—when you bring the people out of Egypt—you will serve [**before**] Elohim on this mountain.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:13)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה **קֳדָם** יְיָ הָא אֲנָא אָתֵי לְוָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאֵימַר לְהוֹן אֱלָהָא דְאַבְהַתְכוֹן שַׁלְחַנִי לְוָתְכוֹן וְיֵימְרוּן לִי מָא שְׁמֵהּ מָא אֵימַר לְהוֹן:


Moshe said to [**before**] Elohim, “Behold, when I come to the B’nei Yisrael and say to them, ‘The God of your fathers sent me to you,’ they will say to me, ‘What is His name?’ What shall I say to them?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:14)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֶהְיֶה אֲשֶׁר אֶהְיֶה וַאֲמַר כִּדְנַן תֵּימַר לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶהְיֶה שַׁלְחַנִי לְוָתְכוֹן:


Elohim said to Moshe, “Eh’yeh Asher Eh’yeh;” and He said, “This is what you must say to the B’nei Yisrael, ‘Eh’yeh sent me to you.’”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:15)


וַאֲמַר עוֹד יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כִּדְנַן תֵּימַר לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְאַבְהַתְכוֹן אֱלָהֵהּ דְאַבְרָהָם אֱלָהֵהּ דְיִצְחָק וֵאלָהֵהּ דְיַעֲקֹב שַׁלְחַנִי לְוָתְכוֹן דֵין שְׁמִי לְעָלַם וְדֵין דָכְרָנִי לְכָל דָר וְדָר:


Elohim continued talking to Moshe, “This is what you must say to the B’nei Yisrael, ‘Adonoy, the God of your fathers, the God of Avraham, the God of Yitzchok, and the God of Yaakov, sent me to you. This is My eternal Name, and this is how I am to be mentioned for all generations.’”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:16)


אִזֵיל וְתִכְנוֹשׁ יָת סָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְאַבְהַתְכוֹן **אִתְגְלֵי** לִי אֱלָהֵהּ דְאַבְרָהָם יִצְחָק וְיַעֲקֹב לְמֵימָר מִדְכַּר דְכִירְנָא יַתְכוֹן וְיָת דְאִתְעֲבַד לְכוֹן בְּמִצְרַיִם:


“Go, gather the elders of Yisrael, and say to them, ‘Adonoy, the God of your fathers appeared [**became revealed**] to me—the God of Avraham, Yitzchok and Yaakov—saying, “I have indeed been mindful of you, regarding that which is being done to you in Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:17)


וַאֲמָרִית אַסֵיק יָתְכוֹן מִשִׁעְבּוּד מִצְרָאֵי לַאֲרַע כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וְחִתָּאֵי וֶאֱמוֹרָאֵי וּפְרִזָאֵי וְחִוָאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי לְאַרְעָא **עַבְדָא** חֲלָב וּדְבָשׁ:


I have said, I will bring you up out of the suffering of Egypt, to the land of the Canaanites, the Chittites, the Emorites, the Perizites, the Chivites and the Yevusites; to a land flowing with [**producing**] milk and honey.’”


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:18)


**וִיקַבְּלוּן לְמֵימְרָךְ** וְתֵיתֵי אַתְּ וְסָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְוָת מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם וְתֵימְרוּן לֵהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי אִתְקְרֵי עֲלָנָא **וּכְעַן** נֵזֵיל כְּעַן מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא וּנְדַבַּח **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא:


They will listen to [**obey**] your voice [**Words**]. You and the elders of Yisrael will come to the king of Egypt, and say to him, ‘Adonoy, God of the Hebrews happened to meet with us. Now we request that you let us go [**now**] into the wilderness for three days, to bring an offering [sacrifice] to [**before**] Adonoy, our God.’


###### Onkelos Exodus 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/3:19)


**וּקְדָמַי גְלֵי** אֲרֵי לָא יִשְׁבּוֹק יָתְכוֹן מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְמֵיזָל וְלָא מִן קֳדָם **דְחֵילֵהּ** תַּקִיף:


I know [**It is revealed before Me**] that the king of Egypt will not allow you to go [on this journey], but not because of his [**power is**] strong hand.

I will then send forth my hand [**the blow of My power**], and strike Egypt with all My wondrous deeds that I will perform in their midst. Then he [Pharaoh] will send you out.

וְאֶתֵּן יָת עַמָא הָדֵין לְרַחֲמִין בְּעֵינֵי מִצְרָאֵי וִיהֵי אֲרֵי תְהָכוּן לָא תְהָכוּן רֵיקָנִין:


I will give this people grace in the eyes of the Egyptians; and when you go, you will not leave empty handed.

וְתִשְׁאַל אִתְּתָא מִשְׁבַבְתַּהּ וּמִקְרִיבַת בֵּיתַהּ מָנִין דִכְסַף וּמָנִין דִדְהַב וּלְבוּשִׁין וּתְשַׁווּן עַל בְּנֵיכוֹן וְעַל בְּנָתֵיכוֹן וּתְרוֹקִנוּן יָת מִצְרָיִם:


Every woman shall borrow from her neighbor, and from the woman living in her house, articles of silver and gold, and clothing. You shall put them on your sons and daughters, and thus you will divest Egypt of its wealth.”



## Chapter 4

###### Onkelos Exodus 4:1

Moshe answered and said, “They will not believe me. They will not listen to my voice [**obey me**]. They will say, ‘Adonoy did not appear [**become revealed**] to you.’”

Adonoy said to him, “What is that in your hand?” He said, “A rod.”

[God] said, “Throw it on the ground.” He [Moshe] threw it on the ground and it turned into a snake. Moshe ran away from it.

Adonoy said to Moshe, “Reach out your hand and grasp its tail.” He reached out his hand and took hold of it, and it became a rod in his hand.

וַאֲמַר יְיָ לֵהּ עוֹד אָעֵיל כְּעַן יְדָךְ בְּעִטְפָךְ וְאָעֵיל יְדֵהּ בְּעִטְפֵהּ וְאַפְקַהּ וְהָא יְדֵהּ **חַוְרָא כְתַלְגָא**:


Adonoy said furthermore to him [Moshe], “Put your hand into your bosom.” He put his hand into his bosom, and when he withdrew it, his hand had become a snow-like tzora’as [**white as snow**].

He [Adonoy] said, “Put your hand into your bosom again.” [Moshe] put his hand into his bosom again, and when he withdrew it from his bosom, it had become [normal] as his flesh.

“If they do not believe you, and they do not listen to [**accept**] the voice [of proof] of the first [miraculous] sign; then they will believe the voice [of proof] of the latter [second] sign.

And if they will not believe even these two signs, and they will not listen to your voice [**accept you**], then you shall take from the water of the river and pour it on the dry land. The water that you will take from the river will turn into blood on the dry land.”

Moshe said to [**before**] Adonoy, “I beg You Adonoy, I am not a man of words—not since yesterday, not since the day before—not from the time You [first] spoke to Your servant, for I am clumsy [slow] of mouth [speech] [**heavy of speech**] and clumsy [slow] of tongue [language].”

Now go. I [**My Word**] will be with your mouth, and teach you what to say.”

[Moshe] said, “I beg You Adony [my Master,] please [**now**] send the one You usually send [**who is fit to be sent**].”

Adonoy displayed anger toward Moshe and said, “Is not Aharon, the Levite, your brother? I know [**It is revealed before Me**] that he knows how to speak. Behold he is setting out to meet you, and when he sees you he will rejoice in his heart.


###### Onkelos Exodus 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/4:15)

You will speak to him and put the words in his mouth. I [**My Word**] will be with your mouth and with his mouth, and I will teach you what to do.

Take this rod in your hand, for with it you will perform the [miraculous] signs.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/4:18)


וַאֲזַל משֶׁה וְתָב לְוָת יֶתֶר חֲמוֹהִי וַאֲמַר לֵהּ אֵיזִיל **כְּעַן** וְאֵיתוּב לְוָת אַחַי דִי בְמִצְרַיִם וְאֶחֱזֵי הַעַד כְּעַן קַיָמִין וַאֲמַר יִתְרוֹ לְמשֶׁה אֱזֵיל לִשְׁלָם:


Moshe went and returned to Yeser, his father-in-law, and said to him, “I would like to leave [**now**] and return to my brothers in Egypt, to see if they are still alive.” Yisro said to Moshe, “Go in peace.”

Your slaves are not given straw, and yet we are told to make bricks. Your slaves are beaten, and it is a sin for your people [**sin against them**].”

He said, “You are lazy, therefore you say, ‘Let us go sacrifice to [**before**] Adonoy.’


###### Onkelos Exodus 5:18

Now go, get to work. You will not be given straw, and you must deliver your quota of bricks.”

The officers of the B’nei Yisrael realized their bad position, saying, “You must not reduce your daily quota of bricks.”

They met Moshe and Aharon who were waiting to meet them as they were leaving Pharaoh.

They said to them [Moshe and Aharon], “Let Adonoy look at [**become revealed to**] you and judge [**take revenge**]. You have made our aroma abhorrent in the eyes of Pharaoh and in the eyes of his servants, putting a sword in their hands to kill us.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/5:22)


וְתָב משֶׁה **קֳדָם** יְיָ וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמָא אַבְאֵשְׁתָּא לְעַמָא הָדֵין וּלְמָא דְנַן שְׁלַחְתָּנִי:


Moshe returned to [**before**] Adonoy and said, “My Master. Why have You brought harm to this people? Why did You send me?

Ever since I came to Pharaoh to speak in Your Name, he has done harm to this people; and You have not rescued Your people.”



## Chapter 6

###### Onkelos Exodus 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כְּעַן תֶּחֱזֵי דְאֶעְבֵּד לְפַרְעֹה אֲרֵי בִידָא תַקִיפָא יְשַׁלְחִנוּן וּבִידָא תַקִיפָא יְתָרֵכִנוּן מֵאַרְעֵהּ:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Now you will see what I will do to Pharaoh, for by [My] strong hand he will let them go, and by [a] strong hand, he will drive them out of his land.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:2)

Elohim spoke to Moshe and said to him, “I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:3

וְאִתְגְּלֵיתִי לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב בְּאֵל שַׁדָּי וּשְׁמִי יְיָ לָא הוֹדָעִית לְהוֹן:


I revealed Myself to Avraham, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov as Almighty Shaddai, but My Name Adonoy I did not make known to them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:4

I also established My covenant with them, to give them the land of Canaan, the land of their sojourning where they lived as strangers.

וְאַף **קֳדָמַי** שְׁמִיעַ יָת קְבֵילַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי מִצְרָאֵי מַפְלְחִין בְּהוֹן וְדָכִירְנָא יָת קְיָמִי:


And also [**before Me**] I have heard the groaning of the B’nei Yisrael, that the Egyptians are enslaving them, [**has been heard before Me**] and I have remembered My covenant.

בְּכֵן אֱמַר לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲנָא יְיָ וְאַפֵּיק יָתְכוֹן **מִגּוֹ דְחוֹק** פָּלְחַן **מִצְרָאֵי** וֶאֱשֵׁיזֵב יָתְכוֹן מִפָּלְחָנְהוֹן וְאֶפְרוֹק יָתְכוֹן בִּדְרָעָא מְרָמָא וּבְדִינִין רַבְרְבִין:


Therefore say to the B’nei Yisrael, ‘I am Adonoy. I will bring you out from under [**amongst**] the [**heavy**] burdens of Egypt [**the Egyptians**], and I will save you from their slavery. I will redeem you with an outstretched arm, and with acts of great judgments.

I will take [**bring**] you to Myself [**before Me**] as a people, and I will be to you Elohim. You will know that I am Adonoy, your God Who is bringing you out from under [**amongst**] the [**heavy**] burdens of Egypt.

I will bring you to the land regarding which I raised My hand [in oath] [**swore with My Word**] that I would give it to Avraham, Yitzchok and Yaakov, and I will give it to you as an inheritance. I am Adonoy.’”

Moshe spoke [these words] to the B’nei Yisrael, but they would not listen to [**obey**] Moshe because of [their] shortness of wind and [**the**] hard labor [**that was hard upon them**].

Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying,

“Come, speak to Pharaoh, king of Egypt, that he send the B’nei Yisrael out of his land.”

וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ לְמֵימָר הָא בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא **קַבִּילוּ** מִנִּי וְאֶכְדֵּין **יְקַבֵּל** מִנִּי פַרְעֹה וַאֲנָא **יַקִּיר מַמְלָל**:


Moshe spoke before Adonoy, saying, “Behold the B’nei Yisrael have not listened to [**obeyed**] me, how then will Pharaoh listen to [**accept**] me—I whose lips are covered [**am heavy of speech**].”

Adonoy [then] spoke to Moshe and Aharon, commanding them regarding the B’nei Yisrael and Pharaoh, king of Egypt, to bring out the B’nei Yisrael from the land of Egypt.

These are the heads of their paternal households: the sons of Reuvein, the first-born of Yisrael [are]: Chanoch, Pallu, Chetzron and Carmi. These are the families of Reuvein.

The sons of Shimon: Yemuel, Yamin, Ohad, Yachin, Tzochar and Shaul, the son of the Canaanite woman. These are the families of Shimon.

These are the names of the sons of Leivi, according to their generations: Gershon, Kehos, and Merari. The years of Leivi’s life were one hundred and thirty-seven years.

The sons of Merari: Machli and Mushi. These are the families of the Levites according to their [descending] generation.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:20)

The sons of Uzziel: Mishaeil, Eltzaphan and Sisri.

וּנְסִיב אַהֲרֹן יָת אֱלִישֶׁבַע בַּת עַמִּינָדָב אֲחָתֵיהּ דְּנַחְשׁוֹן לֵיהּ לְאִנְתּוּ וִילִידַת לֵיהּ יָת נָדָב וְיָת אֲבִיהוּא יָת אֶלְעָזָר וְיָת אִיתָמָר:


Aharon took Elisheva, daughter of Aminadav, sister of Nachshon, for a wife, and she bore him Nadav, Avihu, Elazar and Isamar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:24)


וּבְנֵי קֹרַח אַסִּיר וְאֶלְקָנָה וַאֲבִיאָסָף אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת קֹרַח:


The sons of Korach: Assir, Elkanah and Aviasaf. These are the families of the Korachites.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:25)


וְאֶלְעָזָר בַּר אַהֲרֹן נְסִיב לֵיהּ מִבְּנַת פּוּטִיאֵל לֵיהּ לְאִנְתּוּ וִילִידַת לֵיהּ יָת פִּינְחָס אִלֵּין רֵישֵׁי אֲבָהַת לֵיוָאֵי לְזַרְעֲיָתְהוֹן:


Elazar, son of Aharon took, for himself one of the daughters of Putieil, for a wife, and she bore him Pinchas. These are the heads of the Levites’ patriarchs according to their families.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:26)


הוּא אַהֲרֹן וּמשֶׁה דִּי אֲמַר יְיָ לְהוֹן אַפִּיקוּ יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם עַל חֵילֵיהוֹן:


This is [the lineage of] Aharon and Moshe to whom Adonoy had said, “Bring the B’nei Yisrael out of the land of Egypt with their multitudes.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:27)


אִנּוּן דְּמַלְּלִין עִם פַּרְעֹה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְאַפָּקָא יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִּצְרָיִם הוּא משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן:


They are the ones who spoke to Pharaoh, king of Egypt, to bring the B’nei Yisrael out of Egypt. These are Moshe and Aharon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:28)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא דְמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


It was the day when Adonoy spoke to Moshe in the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:29)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימַר אֲנָא יְיָ מַלֵּל עִם פַּרְעֹה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם יָת כָּל דִּי אֲנָא מְמַלֵּל עִמָּךְ:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying, “I am Adonoy. Speak to Pharaoh, king of Egypt, all that I am speaking to you.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 6:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/6:30)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ הָא אֲנָא **יַקִּיר מַמְלָל** וְאֶכְדֵּין **יְקַבֵּל** מִנִּי פַּרְעֹה:


Moshe said before Adonoy, “Behold, I have covered lips [**am heavy of speech**]. How will Pharaoh listen to [**accept**] me?”



## Chapter 7

###### Onkelos Exodus 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה חֲזֵי **מַנֵּיתָךְ רַב** לְפַרְעֹה וְאַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ יְהֵי מְתֻרְגְּמָנָךְ:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “See, I have made [**appointed**] you a god [**master**] to Pharaoh, and Aharon, your brother will be your spokesman.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:2)


אַתְּ תְּמַלֵּל יָת כָּל דִּי אֲפַקְּדִנָּךְ וְאַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ יְמַלֵּל עִם פַּרְעֹה וִישַׁלַּח יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵאַרְעֵיהּ:


You will speak all that I command you and Aharon, your brother, will speak to Pharaoh, that he send the B’nei Yisrael out of his land.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:3)


וַאֲנָא אַקְשֵׁי יָת לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְאַסְגֵּיתִי יָת אַתְוָתַי וְיָת מוֹפְתַי בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


I will harden Pharaoh’s heart, [in order to] increase My signs and wonders in the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:4)


וְלָא **יְקַבֵּל** מִנְּכוֹן פַּרְעֹה וְאֶתֵּן יָת **מָחַת גְּבֻרְתִּי** בְּמִצְרָיִם וְאַפֵּיק יָת חֵילִי יָת עַמִּי בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּדִינִין רַבְרְבִין:


[Thus] Pharaoh will not listen to [**accept**] you. But then I will display My hand [**the blow of My power**] against Egypt, and I will bring My multitude, My people, the B’nei Yisrael out of the land of Egypt with great acts of judgment.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:5)


וְיִדְּעוּן מִצְרַיִם אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ כַּד אָרֵים יָת **מָחַת גְּבֻרְתִּי** עַל מִצְרָיִם וְאַפֵּיק יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִבֵּינֵיהוֹן:


Egypt will then know that I am Adonoy when I send forth My hand [**the blow of My power**] over Egypt and bring out the B’nei Yisrael from among them.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:6)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן כְּמָא דִפַקִּיד יְיָ יָתְהוֹן כֵּן עֲבָדוּ:


Moshe and Aharon did so. They did exactly as Adonoy commanded them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:7)


וּמשֶׁה בַּר תְּמָנָן שְׁנִין וְאַהֲרֹן בַּר תְּמָנָן וּתְלָת שְׁנִין בְּמַלָּלוּתְהוֹן עִם פַּרְעֹה:


Moshe was eighty years old and Aharon was eighty-three years old when they spoke to Pharaoh.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:8)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרֹן לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy said to Moshe and Aharon, saying:


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:9)


אֲרֵי יְמַלֵּל לְוָתְכוֹן פַּרְעֹה לְמֵימַר הָבוּ לְכוֹן אָתָא וְתֵימַר לְאַהֲרֹן סַב יָת חוּטְרָךְ וּרְמֵי קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה יְהִי לְתַנִּינָא:


“When Pharaoh speaks to you, saying, ‘Give yourselves [credibility by displaying] a marvel;’ you [Moshe] should then say to Aharon, ‘Take your rod and throw it down before Pharaoh.’ It will become a serpent.’”


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:10)


וְעַל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן לְוָת פַּרְעֹה וַעֲבָדוּ כֵן כְּמָא דְּפַקֵּיד יְיָ וּרְמָא אַהֲרֹן יָת חוּטְרֵיהּ קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה וְקָדָם עַבְדּוֹהִי וַהֲוָה לְתַנִּינָא:


Moshe and Aharon came to Pharaoh and they did exactly as Adonoy commanded [them]. Aharon threw his rod before Pharaoh and before his servants and it became a serpent.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:11)


וּקְרָא אַף פַּרְעֹה לְחַכִּימַיָּא וּלְחָרָשַׁיָּא וַעֲבָדוּ אַף אִנּוּן חָרָשֵׁי מִצְרַיִם בְּלַחֲשֵׁיהוֹן כֵּן:


Pharaoh too called [his] wise men and magicians. The sorcerers of Egypt also did likewise with their magic art.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:12)


וּרְמוֹ גְּבַר חוּטְרֵיהּ וַהֲווֹ לְתַנִּינַיָּא וּבְלַע חֻטְרָא דְאַהֲרֹן יָת חֻטְרֵיהוֹן:


When each man threw down his rod they (the rods) became serpents, and Aharon’s rod swallowed their rods.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:13)


וְאִתַּקַּף לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְלָא **קַבִּיל** מִנְּהוֹן כְּמָא דִּמַלִּיל יְיָ:


Pharaoh’s heart [remained] hard and he did not listen to [**accept**] them, just as Adonoy had foretold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:14)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אִתְיַקַּר (לפרש"י יַקִּיר) לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה סָרִיב לְשַׁלָּחָא עַמָּא:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Pharaoh’s heart is heavy [unmoved]. He refuses to let the people go.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:15)


אִזֵּיל לְוָת פַּרְעֹה בְּצַפְרָא הָא נָפֵיק לְמַיָּא וְתִתְעַתַּד לְקַדָּמוּתֵיהּ עַל כֵּיף נַהֲרָא וְחֻטְרָא דְּאִתְהֲפִיךְ לְחִוְיָא תִּסַּב בִּידָךְ:


Go to Pharaoh in the morning when he goes out to the water, and stand there to meet him by the bank of the river. The rod that was turned into a snake, take in your hand.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:16)


וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי שַׁלְּחַנִי לְוָתָךְ לְמֵימַר שַׁלַּח יָת עַמִּי וְיִפְלְחוּן **קֳדָמַי** בְּמַדְבְּרָא וְהָא לָא **קַבֵּילְתָּא** עַד כְּעָן:


Say to him, “Adonoy, God of the Hebrews, sent me to you to say [to you], ‘Send out My people that they may worship [**before**] Me in the wilderness. Behold, thus far you have not listened [**accepted**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:17)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ בְּדָא תִדַּע אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ הָא אֲנָא מָחֵי בְּחֻטְרָא דִּי בִידִי עַל מַיָּא דִּי בְנַהֲרָא וְיִתְהַפְּכוּן לִדְמָא:


This is what Adonoy has said, ‘By this you will know that I am Adonoy. Behold, with the rod that is in my hand, I will strike the water of the river and it will turn into blood.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:18)


וְנוּנֵי דִי בְנַהֲרָא תְּמוּת וּסְרִי נַהֲרָא וְיִלְאוּן מִצְרָאֵי לְמִשְׁתֵּי מַיָּא מִן נַהֲרָא:


The fish that is in the river will die, and the river will become polluted. The Egyptians will weary themselves [trying to purify] drinking water from the river.’”


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:19)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֱמַר לְאַהֲרֹן טוֹל חֻטְרָךְ וַאֲרֵים יְדָךְ עַל מַיָּא דְמִצְרַיִם עַל נַהֲרֵיהוֹן עַל אֲרִתֵּיהוֹן וְעַל אַגְמֵיהוֹן וְעַל כָּל בֵּית כְּנִישׁוּת מֵימֵיהוֹן וִיהוֹן דְּמָא וִיהֵי דְמָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּבְמָנֵי אָעָא וּבְמָנֵי אַבְנָא:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Say to Aharon, ‘Take your rod and extend your hand over the waters of Egypt—over their rivers, over their canals, over their lakes, and over every place where their water is gathered, and it [the water] will become blood. There will be blood throughout all the land of Egypt, [even] in [vessels] of wood and [vessels] of stone.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:20)


וַעֲבָדוּ כֵן משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן כְּמָא דְּפַקִּיד יְיָ וַאֲרֵים בְּחֻטְרָא וּמְחָא יָת מַיָּא דִּי בְנַהֲרָא לְעֵינֵי פַרְעֹה וּלְעֵינֵי עַבְדּוֹהִי וְאִתְהֲפִיכוּ כָּל מַיָּא דִּי בְנַהֲרָא לִדְמָא:


Moshe and Aharon did exactly as Adonoy commanded them. He [Aharon] lifted the rod and struck the water in the river before the eyes of Pharaoh and before the eyes of his [Pharaoh’s] servants, and all the water of the river turned into blood.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:21)


וְנוּנֵי דִי בְנַהֲרָא מִיתוּ וּסְרִי נַהֲרָא וְלָא יְכִילוּ מִצְרָאֵי לְמִשְׁתֵּי מַיָּא מִן נַהֲרָא וַהֲוָה דְמָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


The fish that were in the river died, and the river became so polluted that the Egyptians were unable to drink the water from the river. There was blood throughout all the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/7:22)


וַעֲבָדוּ כֵן חָרָשֵׁי מִצְרַיִם בְּלַחֲשֵׁיהוֹן וְאִתַּקַּף לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְלָא קַבֵּיל מִנְּהוֹן כְּמָא דִּמַלִּיל יְיָ:


The sorcerers of Egypt did likewise with their magic art. Pharaoh’s heart remained hardened and he did not listen to [**accept**] them, just as Adonoy had spoken.


###### Onkelos Exodus 7:23

###### Onkelos Exodus 8:1

###### Onkelos Exodus 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:22)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לָא תַקֵּן לְמֶעְבַּד כֵּן אֲרֵי **בְעִירָא דְמִצְרָאֵי דָּחֲלִין לֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ אֲנַחְנָא נָסְבִין לְדַבָּחָא קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא **הָא נְדַבַּח יָת בְּעִירָא דְמִצְרָאֵי דָּחֲלִין לֵיהּ וְאִנּוּן יְהוֹן חָזָן הֲלָא יֵימְרוּן לְמִרְגְּמָנָא**:


Moshe said, “It would not be proper to do so. For that which is abominable [sacred] to Egypt we intend to sacrifice to [**the animal that the Egyptians worship we are taking to sacrifice before**] Adonoy, our God. Could we sacrifice the abomination of the Egyptians before their eyes and not have them stone us? [**Behold, we will sacrifice the animal that the Egyptians worship, and they will see. Will they not want to stone us?**]


###### Onkelos Exodus 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:23)


מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין נֵיזִיל בְּמַדְבְּרָא וּנְדַבַּח **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא כְּמָא דִּיֵימַר לָנָא:


We must make a three day trip into the wilderness, and sacrifice to [**before**] Adonoy, our God, in the manner that He will tell us.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:24)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעֹה אֲנָא אֲשַׁלַּח יָתְכוֹן וְתִדְבְּחוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן בְּמַדְבְּרָא לְחוֹד אַרְחָקָא לָא תְרַחֲקוּן לְמֵיזָל צַלּוֹ אַף עָלָי:


Pharaoh, said, “I will send you out so that you may sacrifice to [**before**] Adonoy, your God in the wilderness, as long as you do not go too far away. Pray [**also**] for me.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 8:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:25)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה הָא אֲנָא נָפֵק מֵעִמָּךְ וֶאֱצַלֵּי קֳדָם יְיָ וְיַעְדֵּי עָרוֹבָא מִפַּרְעֹה מֵעַבְדּוֹהִי וּמֵעַמֵּיהּ מְחָר לְחוֹד לָא יוֹסֵף פַּרְעֹה לְשַׁקָּרָא בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְשַׁלָּחָא יָת עַמָּא לְדַבָּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Moshe said, “Behold, when I leave your presence I will pray to [**before**] Adonoy, and the wild animals will depart from Pharaoh, and from his servants and from his people, tomorrow. But let Pharaoh never again deceive us [by] refusing to send the people to sacrifice to [**before**] Adonoy.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 8:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:26)


וּנְפַק משֶׁה מִן קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה וְצַלִּי קֳדָם יְיָ:


Moshe went out from Pharaoh[’s presence] and prayed to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 8:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:27)


וַעֲבַד יְיָ כְּפִתְגָּמָא דְמשֶׁה וְאַעְדִּי עָרוֹבָא מִפַּרְעֹה מֵעַבְדּוֹהִי וּמֵעַמֵּיהּ לָא אִשְׁתָּאַר חָד:


Adonoy did as Moshe spoke [requested]. He caused the wild animals to depart from Pharaoh from his servants and from his people. Not a single one remained.


###### Onkelos Exodus 8:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/8:28)


וְיַקַּר פַּרְעֹה יָת לִבֵּיהּ אַף בְּזִמְנָא הָדָא וְלָא שַׁלַּח יָת עַמָּא:


But Pharaoh hardened his heart again, this time and he did not send out the people.



## Chapter 9

###### Onkelos Exodus 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עוּל לְוָת פַּרְעֹה וּתְמַלֵּל עִמֵּיהּ כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי שַׁלַּח יָת עַמִּי וְיִפְלְחוּן **קֳדָמָי**:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Go to Pharaoh and say to him, ‘This is what Adonoy, God of the Hebrews has said, Send out My people and let them serve [**before**] Me.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:2)


אֲרֵי אִם סָרִיב אַתְּ לְשַׁלָּחָא וְעַד כְּעַן אַתְּ מַתְקֵף בְּהוֹן:


For if you refuse to send them out, and continue holding them;


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:3)


הָא **מָחָא מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ הָוְיָא בִּבְעִירָךְ דִּבְחַקְלָא בְּסוּסָוָתָא בַּחֲמָרֵי בְּגַמְלֵי בְּתוֹרֵי וּבְעָנָא מוֹתָא תַּקִּיף לַחֲדָא:


behold, the hand of [**a plague from before**] Adonoy is directed at your livestock that are in the field—upon the horses, the donkeys, the camels, the cattle and the sheep—there will be a very heavy [serious] plague.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:4)


וְאַפְרֵשׁ יְיָ בֵּין בְּעִירָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וּבֵין בְּעִירָא דְמִצְרָיִם וְלָא יְמוּת מִכָּל לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִדָּעַם:


Adonoy will distinguish between the livestock of the [B’nei] Yisrael and the livestock of Egypt. None belonging to the B’nei Yisrael will die.’”


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:5)


וְשַׁוִּי יְיָ זִמְנָא לְמֵימָר מְחַר יַעְבֵּד יְיָ פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין בְּאַרְעָא:


Adonoy has set a time, saying, Tomorrow Adonoy will do this thing in the land.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:6)


וַעֲבַד יְיָ יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין בְּיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וּמִית כֹּל בְּעִירָא דְמִצְרָיִם וּמִבְּעִירָא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא מִית חָד:


Adonoy did this thing on the next day, and all the livestock in Egypt died, but of the livestock of the B’nei Yisrael, not one died.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:7)


וּשְׁלַח פַּרְעֹה וְהָא לָא מִית מִבְּעִירָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל עַד חָד וְאִתְיַקַּר לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְלָא שַׁלַּח יָת עַמָּא:


Pharaoh sent [scouts] and behold not a single animal of the B’nei Yisrael died. The heart of Pharaoh remained hard and he did not send out the people.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:8)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרֹן סִיבוּ לְכוֹן מְלֵי חָפְנֵיכוֹן פִּיחָא דְאַתּוּנָא וְיִזְרְקִנֵּיהּ משֶׁה לְצֵית שְׁמַיָּא לְעֵינֵי פַרְעֹה


Adonoy spoke to Moshe and Aharon, “Both of you take full handfuls of furnace soot and let Moshe throw it heavenward before the eyes of Pharaoh.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:9)


וִיהֵי לְאַבְקָא עַל כָּל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם וִיהֵי עַל אֲנָשָׁא וְעַל בְּעִירָא לְשִׁיחֲנָא סַגִּי אֲבַעְבֻּעִין בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


It will become a fine dust [and settle] over the whole land of Egypt. It will settle on man and beast causing a rash which will break out into boils [affecting] the whole land of Egypt.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:10)


וּנְסִיבוּ יָת פִּיחָא דְאַתּוּנָא וְקָמוּ קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה וּזְרַק יָתֵיהּ משֶׁה לְצֵית שְׁמַיָּא וַהֲוָה שִׁיחֲנָא אֲבַעְבֻּעִין סָגֵי בַּאֲנָשָׁא וּבִבְעִירָא:


They took the furnace soot and stood before Pharaoh. Moshe threw it heavenward, and it caused a rash which broke into boils in man and beast.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:11)


וְלָא יָכִילוּ חָרָשַׁיָּא לְמֵיקָם קֳדָם משֶׁה מִין קֳדָם שִׁיחֲנָא אֲרֵי הֲוָה שִׁיחֲנָא בְּחָרָשַׁיָּא וּבְכָל מִצְרָאֵי:


The sorcerers could not stand before Moshe as a result of the rash, for the rash affected the sorcerers as well as all of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:12)


וְתַקִּיף יְיָ יָת לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְלָא **קַבִּיל** מִנְּהוֹן כְּמָא דְּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה:


Adonoy caused Pharaoh’s heart to remain hard and he did not listen to [**accept**] them just as Adonoy had spoken to Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:13)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אַקְדֵּם בְּצַפְרָא וְאִתְעַתַּד קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי שַׁלַּח יָת עַמִּי וְיִפְלְחוּן **קֳדָמָי**:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Rise early in the morning and stand before Pharaoh, and say to him, ‘This is what Adonoy, God of the Hebrews, has said, “Send out My people and let them serve [**before**] Me.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:14)


אֲרֵי בְּזִמְנָא הָדָא אֲנָא שָׁלַח יָת כָּל מְחָתַי לְלִבָּךְ וּבְעַבְדָּיךְ וּבְעַמָּךְ בְּדִיל דְּתִדַּע אֲרֵי לֵית דִּכְוָתִי **שַׁלִּיט** בְּכָל אַרְעָא:


For this time, I will send all My plagues against your [very] heart. [They will strike] your servants and your people, so that you will know that there is none like Me[**, a ruler**] in all the earth.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:15)


אֲרֵי כְעַן **קְרֵיב קֳדָמַי דְּאֶשְׁלַח פּוֹן יָת מַחַת גְּבֻרְתִּי** וְאֶמְחֵי יָתָךְ וְיָת עַמָּךְ בְּמוֹתָא וְשֵׁיצִיאַת מִן אַרְעָא:


For now [**had I wanted to**] I [could] have extended My hand [**sent the plague of My strength**], and struck you and your people with the pestilence, and you would have been obliterated from the earth.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:16)


וּבְרַם בְּדִיל דָּא קַיֵּמִתָּךְ בְּדִיל לְאַחֲזָיוּתָךְ יָת חֵילִי וּבְדִיל דִּיהוֹן מִשְׁתָּעַן **גְּבוּרַת שְׁמִי** בְּכָל אַרְעָא:


Nevertheless, for this reason I have let you survive—to show you My strength—so that [**the strength of**] My Name will be declared throughout all the earth.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:17)


עַד כְּעַן אַתְּ כְּבֵישַׁת בֵּיהּ בְּעַמִּי בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְשַׁלָּחוּתְהוֹן:


But you are still oppressing My people refusing to send them out.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:18)


הָא אֲנָא **מָחֵית** מִטְרָא בְּעִדָּנָא הָדֵין מְחַר בַּרְדָּא תַּקִּיף לַחֲדָא דִּי לָא הֲוָה דִכְוָתֵיהּ בְּמִצְרַיִם לְמִן יוֹמָא דְאִשְׁתַּכְלָלַת וְעַד כְּעָן:


Tomorrow at this time, I will cause it to rain [**down**] a tremendously heavy hail. There was never anything like it in Egypt, from the time it was founded until now.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:19)


וּכְעַן שְׁלַח כְּנוֹשׁ יָת בְּעִירָךְ וְיָת כָּל דִּי לָךְ בְּחַקְלָא כָּל אֲנָשָׁא וּבְעִירָא דְּאִשְׁתְּכַח בְּחַקְלָא וְלָא יִתְכְּנֵשׁ לְבֵיתָא וְיֵחוֹת עֲלֵיהוֹן בַּרְדָּא וִימוּתוּן:


Now send [word] and gather [shelter] your livestock and all that you have in the field. Any man or beast that is found [remains] in the field and will not be brought into the house [indoors]—the hail will descend upon them and they will die.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:20)


דְּדָחִיל מִפִּתְגָּמָא דַיְיָ מֵעַבְדֵי פַּרְעֹה **כְּנַשׁ** יָת עַבְדּוֹהִי וְיָת גֵּיתוֹהִי לְבָתַּיָּא:


He that feared the word of Adonoy among Pharaoh’s servants, made [**gathered**] his servants and his livestock flee into the houses [indoors].


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:21)


וְדִי לָא שַׁוִּי לִבֵּיהּ לְפִתְגָּמָא דַיְיָ שְׁבַק יָת עַבְדּוֹהִי וְיָת גֵּיתוֹהִי בְּחַקְלָא:


But he whose heart did not heed the word of Adonoy, left his servants and his livestock in the field.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:22)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֲרֵים יָת יְדָךְ עַל צֵית שְׁמַיָּא וִיהֵי בַרְדָּא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָאֵי עַל אֲנָשָׁא וְעַל בְּעִירָא וְעַל כָּל עֶסְבָּא דְחַקְלָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Extend your hand toward the heavens, and there will be hail throughout the land of Egypt—on man and on beast, and upon all the vegetation in the land of Egypt.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:23)


וַאֲרֵים משֶׁה יָת חֻטְרֵיהּ עַל צֵית שְׁמַיָּא וַייָ יְהַב קָלִין וּבַרְדָּא וּמְהַלְּכָא אֶשָּׁתָא עַל אַרְעָא וְאַמְטַר יְיָ בַּרְדָּא עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


Moshe extended his rod toward the heavens, and Adonoy sent thunder and hail, and fire came streaming down upon the earth. Adonoy caused it to rain hail on the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:24)


וַהֲוָה בַרְדָּא וְאֶשָּׁתָא מִשְׁתַּלְהָבַת בְּגוֹ בַרְדָּא תַּקִּיף לַחֲדָא דִּי לָא הֲוָה דִכְוָתֵיהּ בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מֵעִדַּן דַּהֲוַת לְעָם:


There was hail and fire [lightning] flashing among the hailstones. It was an extremely heavy [downfall]. There had never been anything like it in all the land of Egypt since it became a nation.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:25)


וּמְחָא בַרְדָּא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם יָת כָּל דִּי בְחַקְלָא מֵאֲנָשָׁא וְעַד בְּעִירָא וְיָת כָּל עֶסְבָּא דְחַקְלָא מְחָא בַרְדָּא וְיָת כָּל אִילַן דִּבְחַקְלָא תְּבַר:


The hail struck throughout the whole land of Egypt, [killing] all that was in the field [outdoors]—both man and beast. All the vegetation in the fields was struck by the hail and every tree in the fields was shattered.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:26)


לְחוֹד בְּאַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן דִּי תַמָּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא הֲוָא בַּרְדָּא:


Only in the land of Goshen where the B’nei Yisrael were, there was no hail.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:27)


וּשְׁלַח פַּרְעֹה וּקְרָא לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרֹן וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן חָבִית זִמְנָא הָדָא יְיָ זַכָּאָה וַאֲנָא וְעַמִּי חַיָּבִין:


Pharaoh sent, and called for Moshe and Aharon, and he said to them, “This time I have sinned. Adonoy is the righteous One and I and my people are the sinners.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:28)


צַלּוֹ **קֳדָם** יְיָ **וְסַגִּי קֳדָמוֹהִי רְוַח דְּלָא יְהוֹן עֲלָנָא קָלִין דִּלְוַט כְּאִלֵּין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּבַרְדָּא** וְאֶשְׁלַח יָתְכוֹן וְלָא תוֹסְפוּן לְאִתְעַכָּבָא:


Pray to [**before**] Adonoy [**for there is much relief before Him, so that there will be no thunders of anger like these upon us from before God, nor hail.**] There has been enough of God’s thunder and hail. I will send you out without further delay.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:29)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ משֶׁה כְּמִפְּקִי מִן קַרְתָּא אֶפְרוֹשׂ יְדַי **בִּצְלוֹ קֳדָם** יְיָ קָלַיָּא יִתְמַנְּעוּן וּבַרְדָּא לָא יְהֵי עוֹד בְּדִיל דְּתִדַּע אֲרֵי דַיְיָ אַרְעָא:


Moshe said to him, “When I leave the city, I will spread my hands [**in prayer**] to [**before**] Adonoy. The thunder will cease and their will be no more hail, so that you will know that the earth belongs to Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:30)


וְאַתְּ וְעַבְדָּיךְ יְדַעְנָא אֲרֵי עַד כְּעַן לָא **אִתְכְּנַעְתּוּן מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלֹּהִים:


But regarding you and your servants, I know that you are not yet in fear of [**humbled from before**] Adonoy, Elohim.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:31)


וְכִתָּנָא וְסַעֲרֵי לְקוֹ אֲרֵי סַעְרַנְיָא אַבִּיבִין וְכִתָּנָא גַּבְעוֹלִין:


The flax and barley have been broken since the barley was ripe, and the flax had formed into stalks.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:32)


וְחִטָּאָה וְכֻנְתַּיָּא לָא לְקוֹ אֲרֵי אֲפִילָתָא אִנּוּן:


But neither the wheat or the spelt were broken since they are late in sprouting.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:33)


וּנְפַק משֶׁה מִלְּוָת פַּרְעֹה יָת קַרְתָּא וּפְרַשׂ יְדוֹהִי בִּצְלוֹ **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְיִתְמַנְּעוּן קָלַיָּא וּבַרְדָּא וּמִטְרָא **דַהֲוָה נָחִית** לָא מְטָא עַל אַרְעָא:


Moshe [then] left Pharaoh’s presence, and left the city. He spread his hands [in prayer] to [**before**] Adonoy. The thunder ceased, and the hail and the rain [**which had descended**] did not reach the earth.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:34)


וַחֲזָא פַרְעֹה אֲרֵי אִתְמְנַע מִטְרָא וּבַרְדָּא וְקָלַיָּא וְאוֹסֵף לְמֶחֱטֵי וְיַקְּרֵיהּ לְלִבֵּיהּ הוּא וְעַבְדּוֹהִי:


[When] Pharaoh saw that the rain, and the hail and the thunder ceased, he continued his sin[ful ways]. He and his servants hardened their hearts.


###### Onkelos Exodus 9:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/9:35)


וְאִתַּקַּף לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְלָא שַׁלַּח יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּמָא דִי מַלֵּיל יְיָ בִּידָא דְּמשֶׁה:


Pharaoh’s heart was strengthened, and he did not send the B’nei Yisrael out. This was just as Adonoy had spoken through Moshe.



## Chapter 10

###### Onkelos Exodus 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עוּל לְוָת פַּרְעֹה אֲרֵי אֲנָא יַקָרִית יָת לִבֵּהּ וְיָת לִבָּא דְעַבְדוֹהִי בְּדִיל לְשַׁוָאָה אָתַי אִלֵין בֵּינֵיהוֹן:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Go in to Pharaoh for I have hardened his heart and [also] the hearts of his servants, so that I will be able to set [perform] these [miraculous] signs in their midst.


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:2)


וּבְדִיל דְתִשְׁתָּעֵי **קֳדָם** בְּרָךְ וּבַר בְּרָךְ **יָת נִסִין דִי עֲבָדִית בְּמִצְרַיִם** וְיָת אַתְוָתַי דִי שַׁוֵיתִי בְהוֹן וְתִדְעוּן אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ:


And so that you may relate in the ears of [**before**] your son and grandson how I made a mockery of the Egyptians [**the miracles that I performed in Egypt**], and the miraculous signs I performed among them. You will then know that I am Adonoy.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:3)


וַאֲתָא משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן לְוָת פַּרְעֹה וַאֲמָרוּ לֵהּ כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי עַד אִמָתַי מְסָרֵב אַתְּ לְאִתְכְּנָעָא מִן **קֳדָמָי** שַׁלַח עַמִי וְיִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמָי:


Moshe and Aharon went in to Pharaoh and they said to him, “This is what Adonoy, God of the Hebrews, has said, ‘How long will you refuse to be humbled before Me? Send My people out that they may worship [**before**] Me.


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:4)


אֲרֵי אִם סָרֵיב אַתְּ לְשַׁלָחָא יָת עַמִי הָא אֲנָא מַיְיתִי מְחָר גוֹבָא בִּתְחוּמָךְ:


For if you refuse to send My people out, I will bring locusts within your borders tomorrow.


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:5)


וַחֲפָא יָת **עֵין שִׁמְשָׁא** דְאַרְעָא וְלָא יִכּוּל לְמֶחֱזֵי יָת אַרְעָא וְיֵכוּל יָת שְׁאָר שֵׁזַבְתָּא דְאִשְׁתָּאַר לְכוֹן מִן בַּרְדָא וְיֵכוּל יָת כָּל אִילָנָא דְאַצְמַח לְכוֹן מִן חַקְלָא:


They will cover the visible surface [**eye of the sun**] of the land so that one will be unable to see the land. They will eat all that was spared, that remained for you from the hail, and they will eat every tree that grows for you in the field.


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:6)


וְיִמְלוּן בָּתָּיךְ וּבָתֵּי כָל עַבְדָיךְ וּבָתֵּי כָל מִצְרַיִם דִי לָא חֲזוֹ אֲבָהָתָךְ וַאֲבָהַת אֲבָהָתָךְ מִיוֹמָא דְמֶהֱוֵיהוֹן עַל אַרְעָא עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין וְאִתְפְּנִי וּנְפַק מִן קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה:


They will fill your houses, and the houses of all your servants, and the houses of all the Egyptians. [It will be something] that was never seen by your fathers or your fathers’ fathers since the day they were on the earth until this day.’” He [Moshe] then turned and went out from Pharaoh.


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:7)


וַאֲמָרוּ עַבְדֵי פַרְעֹה לֵהּ עַד אִמָתַי יְהֵי דֵין לָנָא לְתַקְלָא שַׁלַח יָת גֻבְרַיָא וְיִפְלְחוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֲהוֹן הַעַד כְּעַן לָא יְדַעְתָּ אֲרֵי אֲבֵדַת מִצְרָיִם:


Pharaoh’s servants said to him, “How long will this [man] be a menace to us? Send these men out and let them worship Adonoy, their God. Did you not yet realize that Egypt is [being] destroyed?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:8)


וְאִתּוֹתַב יָת משֶׁה וְיָת אַהֲרֹן לְוָת פַּרְעֹה וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן אֱזִילוּ פְלָחוּ **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן מָן וּמָן דְאָזְלִין:


Moshe and Aharon were brought back to Pharaoh, and he said to them, “Go worship [**before**] Adonoy, your God. Exactly who will be going?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:9)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה בְּעוּלֵמָנָּא וּבְסָבָנָּא נֵזֵיל בְּבָנָנָא וּבִבְנָתָנָא בְּעָנָנָא וּבְתוֹרָנָא נֵזֵיל אֲרֵי חַגָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ לָנָא:


Moshe said, “With our young and with our old we will go. With our sons, with our daughters, with our sheep and with our cattle we will go, for it is a festival to [**before**] Adonoy for all of us.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:10)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יְהֵי כֵן **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **בְּסַעְדְכוֹן** כַּד אֲשַּׁלַח יָתְכוֹן וְיָת טַפְלְכוֹן חֲזוֹ אֲרֵי **בִישָׁא דְאַתּוּן סְבִירִין לְמֶעְבַּד לָקֳבֵל אַפֵּיכוֹן לְאִסְתַּחָרָא**:


He [Pharaoh] said to them, “So be it. May [**the Word of**] Adonoy be with you [**your support**] just as I will send you and your little ones. Beware for evil confronts you [**the evil which you intend to commit will be turned against your faces**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:11)


לָא כֵן אֱזִילוּ כְעַן גֻבְרַיָא וּפְלָחוּ **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֲרֵי יָתַהּ אַתּוּן בָּעָן וְתָרִיךְ יָתְהוֹן מִן קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה:


This is not right. [Only] the [adult] men should go and worship [**before**] Adonoy for this is what you desire.” He had them expelled from Pharaoh’s presence.


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֲרֵים יְדָךְ עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם וְיֵתֵי גוֹבָא וְיִסַק עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם וְיֵכוּל יָת כָּל עִסְבָּא דְאַרְעָא יָת כָּל דִי אַשְׁאַר בַּרְדָא:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Stretch out your hand over the land of Egypt for the locusts [to bring the locusts] and they will emerge upon the land of Egypt. They will eat all the vegetation in the land, that remained from [the destruction of] the hail.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/10:13)
