וִיהֵי לָךְ לְאָת עַל יְדָךְ וּלְדָכְרָנָא בֵּין עֵינָיךְ בְּדִיל תְּהֵי אוֹרַיְתָא דַיְיָ בְּפוּמָךְ אֲרֵי בִּידָא תַקִיפָא אַפְּקָךְ יְיָ מִמִצְרָיִם:


It shall be to you as a sign on your hand, and for a reminder between your eyes, so that Adonoy’s teachings will be in your mouth, for with a strong hand Adonoy brought you out of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:10)


וְתִטַר יָת קְיָמָא הָדָא לְזִמְנֵהּ מִזְמַן לִזְמָן:


You must preserve this statute in its appointed time, from year to year.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:11)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יָעֵלִנָךְ יְיָ לְאַרְעָא דִכְנַעֲנָאֵי כְּמָא דִי קַיִים לָךְ וְלַאֲבָהָתָךְ וְיִתְּנִנַהּ לָךְ:


When Adonoy brings you to the land of the Canaanites as He swore to you and to your fathers; and He will have given it to you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:12)


וְתַעְבַּר כָּל פָּתַח וַלְדָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְכָל פָּתַח וְלַד בְּעִירָא דִי יְהֵי לָךְ דִכְרִין **תַּקְדֵשׁ קֳדָם** יְיָ:


[At that time] you must pass on, [set aside] every one that [is first to] open the womb, to [**before**] Adonoy. Every firstling that is dropped [born] by animals that belong to you, the males shall belong to [**be consecrated before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:13)


וְכָל פָּתַח (בּוּכְרָא) דַחֲמָרָא תִּפְרוֹק בְּאִמְרָא וְאִם לָא תִפְרוֹק וְתִנְקְפֵהּ וְכָל בּוּכְרָא דַאֲנָשָׁא בִּבְנָיךְ תִּפְרוֹק:


Redeem each firstling donkey with a sheep. If it is not redeemed, you must break its neck. Redeem every first-born male among your sons.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:14)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יִשְׁאֲלִנָךְ בְּרָךְ מְחָר לְמֵימַר מָא דָא וְתֵימַר לֵהּ בִּתְקוֹף יְדָא אַפְּקָנָא יְיָ מִמִצְרַיִם מִבֵּית עַבְדוּתָא:


When your son asks you at a later time saying ‘What is this?’ You should say to him, “With a strong hand Adonoy brought us out of Egypt from the house of slavery.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:15)


וַהֲוָה אֲרֵי אַקְשֵׁי פַרְעֹה לְשַׁלָחוּתָנָא וּקְטַל יְיָ כָּל בּוּכְרָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִבּוּכְרָא דַאֲנָשָׁא וְעַד בּוּכְרָא דִבְעִירָא עַל כֵּן אֲנָא דָבַח **קֳדָם** יְיָ כָּל פָּתַח וַלְדָא דִכְרִין וְכָל בּוּכְרָא דִבְנַי אֶפְרוֹק:


When Pharaoh stubbornly refused to send us out, Adonoy killed every first-born in the land of Egypt, from the first-born of man to the first-born of beast. I am therefore sacrificing to [**before**] Adonoy all that [is first to] open the womb which are male, and the first-born of my sons I redeem.’


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:16)


וִיהֵי לְאָת עַל יְדָךְ וְלִתְפִלִין בֵּין עֵינָיךְ אֲרֵי בִּתְקוֹף יְדָא אַפְּקָנָא יְיָ מִמִצְרָיִם:


[These words] shall be a sign on your hand and for totafos [**phylacteries**] between your eyes, for with a strong hand Adonoy brought us out of Egypt.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:17)


וַהֲוָה כַּד שַׁלַח פַּרְעֹה יָת עַמָא וְלָא דַבָּרִינוּן יְיָ אֹרַח אַרְעָא פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אֲרֵי קָרִיבָא הוּא אֲרֵי אֲמַר יְיָ דִילְמָא **יְזוּעוּן** עַמָא בְּמֶחֱזֵיהוֹן קְרָבָא וִיתוּבוּן לְמִצְרָיִם:


When Pharaoh sent away the people Elohim did not lead them by way of the land of the Philistines because it was the shortest route; for Elohim said, “The people might change their minds [**tremble**] should they encounter war, and return to Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:18)


וְאַסְחַר יְיָ יָת עַמָא אֹרַח מַדְבְּרָא יַמָא דְסוּף וּמְזָרְזִין סְלִיקוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


And so Elohim led the people round-about by way of the Reed Sea Desert, and the B’nei Yisrael went up armed from the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:19)


**וְאַסִיק** משֶׁה יָת גַרְמֵי יוֹסֵף עִמֵהּ אֲרֵי אוֹמָאָה אוֹמִי יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימָר מִדְכַּר דְכִיר יְיָ יָתְכוֹן וְתַסְקוּן יָת גַרְמַי מִכָּא עִמְכוֹן:


Moshe took [**carried up**] the bones of Yoseif with him, for [Yoseif] had bound the B’nei Yisrael by oath saying, “Elohim will surely remember you, and [then] you must carry up my bones out of here with you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:20)


וּנְטָלוּ מִסֻכּוֹת וּשְׁרוֹ בְאֵתָם בִּסְטַר מַדְבְּרָא:


They journeyed from Sukkos and camped at Eisom at the edge of the desert.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:21)


וַיְיָ מְדַבַּר קָדָמֵיהוֹן בִּימָמָא בְּעַמוּדָא דַעֲנָנָא לְדַבָּרוּתְהוֹן בְּאוֹרְחָא וְלֵילְיָא בְּעַמוּדָא דְאֶשָׁתָא לְאַנְהָרָא לְהוֹן לְמֵיזַל בִּימָמָא וּבְלֵילְיָא:


Adonoy went before them by day in a pillar of cloud to lead them on the way, and at night in a pillar of fire to provide them with light, so that they could travel by day and by night.


###### Onkelos Exodus 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/13:22)


לָא יַעֲדֵי עַמוּדָא דַעֲנָנָא בִּימָמָא **וְאַף** לָא עַמוּדָא דְאֶשָׁתָא בְּלֵילְיָא קֳדָם עַמָא:


He did not remove the pillar of cloud by day, or [**even**] the pillar of fire at night, from before the people.



## Chapter 14

###### Onkelos Exodus 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying:


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיתוּבוּן וְיִשְׁרוּן קֳדָם פּוּם חִירָתָא בֵּין מִגְדוֹל וּבֵין יַמָא קֳדָם בְּעֵיל צְפוֹן לְקִבְלֵהּ תִּשְׁרוּן עַל יַמָא:


“Speak to the B’nei Yisrael and have them turn back and camp before Pi haChiros, between Migdol and the sea, facing Ba’al Tzephon. Camp opposite it, near the sea.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:3)


וְיֵימַר פַּרְעֹה עַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מְעַרְבְּלִין אִנוּן בְּאַרְעָא אֲחַד עֲלֵיהוֹן מַדְבְּרָא:


Pharaoh will then say of the B’nei Yisrael, “They are confused in the land, the desert has shut [trapped] them in.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:4)


וְאַתְקֵף יָת לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְיִרְדַף בַּתְרֵיהוֹן וְאֶתְיַקַר בְּפַרְעֹה וּבְכָל מַשִׁרְיָתֵהּ וְיִדְעוּן מִצְרָאֵי אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ וַעֲבָדוּ כֵן:


I will harden Pharaoh’s heart and he will pursue them. and I will be glorified through Pharaoh and his entire army. Egypt will [then] know that I am Adonoy.” They [the B’nei Yisrael] did just that.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:5)


וְאִתְחַוָא לְמַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם אֲרֵי **אֲזַל** עַמָא וְאִתְהֲפִיךְ לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה וְעַבְדוֹהִי לְעַמָא וַאֲמָרוּ מָא דָא עֲבַדְנָא אֲרֵי שַׁלַחְנָא יָת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִפָּלְחָנָנָא:


The king of Egypt was told that the people had fled [**gone**]. Pharaoh and his servants had a change of heart regarding the people, and they said, “What have we done? [How did] we release Israel from serving us?


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:6)


וְטַקִיס יָת רְתִכֵּהּ וְיָת עַמֵהּ דַבַּר עִמֵהּ:


He [Pharaoh] harnessed his chariot and he took his people with him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:7)


וּדְבַר שִׁתְּ מְאָה רְתִכִּין בְּחִירָן וְכֹל רְתִכֵּי מִצְרָאֵי **וְגִבָּרִין מְמַנָן** עַל כֻּלְהוֹן:


He took six hundred elite chariots [and crews], and all the [other] chariots of Egypt, and commanders [**mighty men were appointed**] over all of them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:8)


וְאַתְקֵף יְיָ יָת לִבָּא דְפַרְעֹה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם וּרְדַף בָּתַר בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל נְפָקוּ בְּרֵישׁ גְלִי:


Adonoy hardened the heart of Pharaoh, king of Egypt, and he pursued the B’nei Yisrael. The B’nei Yisrael went out high handedly [in triumph] [**publicly**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:9)


וּרְדָפוּ מִצְרָאֵי בַּתְרֵיהוֹן וְאַדְבִּיקוּ יָתְהוֹן כַּד שָׁרָן עַל יַמָא כָּל סוּסָוַת רְתִכֵּי פַרְעֹה וּפָרָשׁוֹהִי וּמַשִׁרְיָתֵהּ עַל פּוּם חִירָתָא קֳדָם בְּעֵיל צְפוֹן:


The Egyptians pursued them and overtook them as they were encamped by the sea. There were all Pharaoh’s chariot horses, his cavalry and his army [infantry], at Pi haChiros, facing Ba’al Tzephon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:10)


וּפַרְעֹה קְרֵיב וּזְקָפוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת עֵינֵיהוֹן וְהָא מִצְרָאֵי נַטְלִין בַּתְרֵיהוֹן וּדְחִילוּ לַחֲדָא וּזְעִיקוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Pharaoh drew near, and the B’nei Yisrael looked up, and beheld the Egyptians coming after them. They were very frightened, and the B’nei Yisrael cried out to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:11)


וַאֲמָרוּ לְמשֶׁה הֲמִדְלֵית קִבְרִין בְּמִצְרַיִם דְבַרְתָּנָא לִמְמַת בְּמַדְבְּרָא מָא דָא עֲבַדְתָּא לָנָא לְאַפָּקוּתָנָא מִמִצְרָיִם:


They said to Moshe, “Were there not enough graves in Egypt that you took us out to die in the desert? What have you done to us, bringing us out of Egypt?


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:12)


הֲלָא דֵין פִּתְגָמָא דִי מַלֵלְנָא עִמָךְ בְמִצְרַיִם לְמֵימַר שְׁבוֹק מִנָנָא וְנִפְלַח יָת מִצְרָאֵי אֲרֵי טַב לָנָא דְנִפְלַח יָת מִצְרָאֵי מִדִנְמוּת בְּמַדְבְּרָא:


This is the [exact] thing that we told you in Egypt saying, ‘Leave us alone and let us serve the Egyptians.’ It would have been better for us to serve the Egyptians than we should die in the desert.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:13)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְעַמָא לָא תִדְחֲלוּן אִתְעַתָּדוּ וַחֲזוֹ יָת פּוּרְקָנָא דַיְיָ דְיַעְבֵּד לְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין אֲרֵי כְּמָא דִחֲזֵיתוּן יַת מִצְרָאֵי יוֹמָא דֵין לָא תוֹסְפוּן לְמֶחֱזֵיהוֹן עוֹד עַד עָלְמָא:


Moshe said to the people, “Do not be afraid, stand firm and you will see the deliverance of Adonoy, which He will perform for you this day, for the Egyptians you have seen this day you will never again see them, even to eternity.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:14)


יְיָ יְגִיחַ לְכוֹן **קְרָב** וְאַתּוּן תִּשְׁתְּקוּן:


Adonoy will fight [**the war**] for you, and you [must] remain silent.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:15)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה **קַבֵּלִית צְלוֹתָךְ** מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִטְלוּן:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Why do you cry to Me? [**I have accepted your prayer.**] Speak to the B’nei Yisrael and let them move on.

וְאַתְּ טוֹל יָת חֻטְרָךְ וַאֲרֵים יָת יְדָךְ עַל יַמָא וּבְזָעוֹהִי וְיֵעֲלוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּגוֹ יַמָא בְּיַבֶּשְׁתָּא:


And you [do as follows], raise your staff and extend your hand over the sea and split it. The B’nei Yisrael will then come [across] in the midst of the sea on dry land.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:17)


וַאֲנָא הָא אֲנָא מְתַקֵף יָת לִבָּא דְמִצְרָאֵי וְיֵעֲלוּן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן וְאֶתְיַקַר בְּפַרְעֹה וּבְכָל מַשִׁרְיָתֵהּ בִּרְתִכּוֹהִי וּבְפָרָשׁוֹהִי:


I will harden the heart of the Egyptians and they will follow after them. I will be glorified through Pharaoh and his entire army, his chariots and his cavalry.


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:18)


וְיִדְּעוּן מִצְרָאֵי אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ בְּאִתְיַקָרוּתִי בְּפַרְעֹה בִּרְתִכּוֹהִי וּבְפָרָשׁוֹהִי:


The Egyptians will know that I am Adonoy when I am glorified through Pharaoh, his chariots and cavalry.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:19)


וּנְטַל מַלְאֲכָא דַיְיָ דִמְדַבַּר קֳדָם מַשְׁרִיתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲתָא מִבַּתְרֵיהוֹן וּנְטַל עַמּוּדָא דַעֲנָנָא מִן קֳדָמֵיהוֹן וּשְׁרָא מִבַּתְרֵיהוֹן:


The angel of Elohim moved [from its position] when it traveled in front of the camp of Israel, and went behind them. The pillar of cloud moved from in front of them and it stood behind them.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:20)


וְעַל בֵּין מַשְׁרִיתָא דְמִצְרָאֵי וּבֵין מַשְׁרִיתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וַהֲוָה עֲנָנָא וְקַבְלָא לְמִצְרָאֵי **וּלְיִשְׂרָאֵל** נְהַר כָּל לֵילְיָא וְלָא אִתְקְרִיבוּ דֵין לְוָת דֵין כָּל לֵילְיָא:


It came between the Egyptian camp and the camp of Yisrael. There was the cloud and darkness [for the Egyptians] and [the pillar of fire] lit up the night [**for Yisrael**]. The camps did not approach each other all that night.

וַאֲרֵים משֶׁה יָת יְדֵהּ עַל יַמָּא וְדַבַּר יְיָ יָת יַמָּא בְּרוּחַ קִדוּמָא תַקִיף כָּל לֵילְיָא וְשַׁוִי יָת יַמָּא לְיַבֶּשְׁתָּא וְאִתְבְּזָעוּ מַיָּא:


Moshe extended his hand over the sea and Adonoy turned back the sea with a strong east wind [blowing] all through the night. He made the sea into dry land, and the waters were divided.

וְאַעְדִי יָת גַלְגַלֵי רְתִכּוֹהִי וּמְדַבְּרִין לְהוֹן בִּתְקוֹף וַאֲמַר מִצְרָאֵי נֵעִירוֹק מִן קֳדָם יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי **דָא הִיא גְבֻרְתָּא דַיְיָ דַעֲבֵד לְהוֹן קְרָבִין** בְּמִצְרָיִם:


He removed the wheels of their chariots and made them move heavily [clumsily]. The Egyptians said, “Let us flee the [B’nei] Yisrael, for [**this is the might of**] Adonoy [**that**] is fighting [**wars**] for them against Egypt.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:26)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֲרֵים יָת יְדָךְ עַל יַמָא וִיתוּבוּן מַיָא עַל מִצְרָאֵי עַל רְתִכֵּיהוֹן וְעַל פָּרָשֵׁיהוֹן:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Extend your hand over the sea, and the water will come back over the Egyptians, [over] their chariots and their cavalry.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:27)


וַאֲרֵים משֶׁה יָת יְדֵהּ עַל יַמָא וְתָב יַמָא לְעִדַן צַפְרָא לְתָקְפֵּהּ וּמִצְרָאֵי עָרְקִין לְקַדָמוּתֵהּ **וְשַׁנִיק** יְיָ יָת מִצְרָאֵי בְּגוֹ יַמָא:


Moshe extended his hand over the sea, and, toward morning, the sea returned to its strength while the Egyptians were fleeing toward it. Adonoy overturned [**stirred**] the Egyptians in the midst of the sea.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:28)


וְתָבוּ מַיָא וַחֲפוֹ יָת רְתִכַּיָא וְיָת פָּרָשַׁיָא לְכֹל מַשִׁרְיַת פַּרְעֹה דְעַלוּ בַתְרֵיהוֹן בְּיַמָא לָא אִשְׁתָּאַר בְּהוֹן עַד חָד:


The waters returned and covered the chariots, and the cavalry, including Pharaoh’s entire army which had followed them into the sea. Not one of them remained [alive].

וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל הַלִיכוּ בְיַבֶּשְׁתָּא בְּגוֹ יַמָא וּמַיָא לְהוֹן **שׁוּרִין** מִיַמִינֵהוֹן וּמִסְמָאלֵהוֹן:


The B’nei Yisrael walked on dry land in the midst of the sea. For them the waters were a wall [**walls**] to their right and to their left.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:30)

On that day Adonoy delivered [B’nei] Yisrael from the hand of Egypt. [B’nei] Yisrael saw the Egyptians dead on the seashore.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/14:31)


וַחֲזָא יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת **גְבוּרְתָּא** יְדָא רַבְּתָא דִי עֲבַד יְיָ בְּמִצְרַיִם וּדְחִילוּ עַמָא **מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ וְהֵמִינוּ **בְּמֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **וּבִנְבִיאוּת** משֶׁה עַבְדֵהּ:


[B’nei] Yisrael saw the [**might of the**] great hand which Adonoy wielded against Egypt. The people feared [**from before**] Adonoy, and believed in [**the Word of**] Adonoy and [that] [**in the prophecy of**] Moshe [was] His servant.



## Chapter 15

###### Onkelos Exodus 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:1)


בְּכֵן **שַׁבַּח** משֶׁה וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת **תִּשְׁבַּחְתָּא הָדָא קֳדָם** יְיָ וַאֲמָרוּ לְמֵימָר **נְ**שַׁבַּח **וְנוֹדֵי קָדָם** יְיָ אֲרֵי **אִתְגָאֵי עַל גֵוְתָנַיָא וְגֵאוּתָא דִילֵהּ הִיא** סוּסְיָא וְרָכְבֵהּ רְמָא בְיַמָא:


Then Moshe and the B’nei Yisrael sang this song to [**exalted this praise before**] Adonoy and they said, I [**We**] will sing to [**praise and we will give thanks before**] Adonoy for He is most high [**exalted Himself above those who are exalted, and exaltation is His**], horse and its rider He threw into the sea.


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:2)


**תָּקְפִּי וְתֻשְׁבַּחְתִּי דְחִילָא יְיָ וַאֲמַר בְּמֵימְרֵהּ וַהֲוָה לִי לְפָרָק** דֵין אֱלָהַי **וְאֶבְנֵי לֵהּ מַקְדְשָׁא** אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתַי **וְאֶפְלַח קֳדָמוֹהִי**:


The strength and retribution [**The awesomeness**] of God [**is my strength and my praise**] was the cause of my deliverance. [**He said with His Word and He has been a savior for me.**] This is my Almighty and I will glorify [**build a Temple for**] Him; the God of my father and I will exalt [**worship before**] Him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:3)

Adonoy is the master of [**victory of**] war[**s**], Adonoy is His Name.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:4)


רְתִכֵּי פַּרְעֹה וּמַשִׁרְיָתֵהּ שְׁדִי בְיַמָא וּשְׁפַר גִבָּרוֹהִי אִטְבָּעוּ בְּיַמָא דְסוּף:


Pharaoh’s chariots and army He hurled into the sea; his elite officers were sunk in the Reed Sea.

תְּהוֹמַיָא חֲפוֹ עֲלֵיהוֹן נְחָתוּ לְעוּמְקַיָא כְּאַבְנָא:


The depths covered them, they sank into the shadowy depths like stone.


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:6)


יַמִינָךְ יְיָ אַדִירָא בְּחֵילָא יַמִינָךְ יְיָ תְּבָרַת סָנְאָה:


Your right hand Adonoy, is adorned with power, Your right hand Adonoy, crushes the enemy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:7)

In Your great majesty [**might**], You destroy [**crush**] Your opponents [**those who rise up against Your people**]; You send forth Your fury, it consumes them like [**fire consumes**] straw.


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:8)


**וּבְמֵימַר פּוּמָךְ חַכִּימוּ** מַיָא קָמוּ כְשׁוּר אֲזָלַיָא קָפוּ תְהוֹמִין בְּלִבָּא דְיַמָא:


Through the wind [breath] of Your nostrils [**Word of Your mouth**], the waters were heaped up [**became wise**], flowing water stood like a wall; the deep waters congealed in the heart of the sea.


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:9)


דַהֲוָה אֲמַר סָנְאָה אֶרְדוֹף אַדְבֵּק אֲפַלֵג בִּזְתָא **תִּשְׂבַּע מִנְהוֹן נַפְשִׁי** אֶשְׁלוֹף חַרְבִּי תְּשֵׁיצִינוּן יְדִי:


The enemy [**had**] said, “I will pursue [them], I will overtake [them], I will divide the spoils, I will satisfy myself [**My soul will be satisfied from them**]; I will draw my sword, my hand will destroy them.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:10)


**אֲמַרְתָּ בְּמֵימְרָךְ** חֲפָא עֲלֵיהוֹן יַמָא אִשְׁתְּקָעוּ כַּאֲבָרָא בְּמַיִן תַּקִיפִין:


You made Your wind blow [**said with Your Word**], the sea covered them; they sank like lead in the mighty waters.


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:11)


**לֵית בַּר מִנָךְ דְאַתְּ הוּא אֱלָהָא יְיָ לֵית בַּר מִנָךְ אֱלָהָא אַתְּ אַדִיר בְּקוּדְשָׁא דְחֵל תֻּשְׁבְּחָן עָבֵד פָּרִישָׁן**:


Who is like You among the mighty, Adonoy! Who is like You, adorned in holiness, Awesome in praise, doing wonders? [**There is none besides You, for You are God, Adonoy! There is none besides You, O God, You are adorned in holiness, Awesome in praise, doing wonders!**]


###### Onkelos Exodus 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:12)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:14)

Peoples heard and they tremble, terror gripped the inhabitants of [**those who inhabited**] Philistia.

Horror and dread [**will**] fall upon them, at the greatness of Your arm [**power**], they [**will be**] are still [**silent**] as stone; until Your people [**will**] cross over [**the stream of Arnon**], Adonoy, until Your [**this**] people [**who**] You acquired [**redeemed will**] cross over [**the Jordan**].

Miriam led them in the response: “Sing to [**Praise and give thanks before**] Adonoy for He is most high [**exalted Himself above those who are exalted, and exaltation is His**]; horse and its rider He hurled into the sea.”

Moshe led [B’nei] Yisrael away from the Reed Sea, and they went out into the desert of Shur [**Cheger**], they traveled for three days in the desert and they did not find any water.

They came to Marah but they could not drink the water of Marah because it was bitter. The place was therefore called Marah [bitter].

The people complained to Moshe saying, “What shall we drink?”

**וְצַלִי קֳדָם** יְיָ **וְאַלְפֵהּ** יְיָ אָעָא וּרְמָא לְמַיָא **וּבַסִימוּ** מַיָא תַּמָן **גְזַר** לֵהּ קְיָם וְדִין וְתַמָן נַסְיֵהּ:


He [Moshe] cried out to [**prayed before**] Adonoy, Adonoy showed him [**instructed him concerning**] a tree and he threw it into the water, and the water became sweet [**pleasing**]. There He set before [**established for**] them statutes and ordinances, and there He tested them.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:26)


וַאֲמַר אִם קַבָּלָא תְקַבֵּל **לְמֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּדְכָשַׁר **קֳדָמוֹהִי** תַעְבֵּד וְתַצֵית לִפִקוּדוֹהִי וְתִטַר כָּל קְיָמוֹהִי כָּל מַרְעִין דִי שַׁוֵיתִי בְמִצְרַיִם לָא אֲשַׁוֵי עֲלָךְ אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אַסָךְ:


He said [to them,] “If you vigilantly obey the voice [**Word**] of Adonoy, your God, and do what is upright in His eyes [**before Him**], give ear to His commandments, and preserve all His statutes; [then], every sickness that I brought upon Egypt, I will not bring upon you, for I am Adonoy, Who heals you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/15:27)


וַאֲתוֹ לְאֵילִם וְתַמָן תְּרֵי עֲסַר מַבּוּעִין דְמַיִן וְשִׁבְעִין דִקְלִין וּשְׁרוֹ תַמָן עַל מַיָא:


Then they came to Eilim, where there were twelve springs of water and seventy date palms. and they camped the by the water.



## Chapter 16

###### Onkelos Exodus 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:1)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֵילִם וַאֲתוֹ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמַדְבְּרָא דְסִין דִי בֵין אֵילִם וּבֵין סִינָי בְּחַמְשָׁא עַסְרָא יוֹמָא לְיַרְחָא תִנְיָנָא לְמִפָּקְהוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


They journeyed from Eilim, and the entire community of the B’nei Yisrael came to the desert of Sin, which is between Eilim and Sinai. It was the fifteenth day of the second month after they went out of the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:2)


וְאִתְרָעָמוּ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרֹן בְּמַדְבְּרָא:


The entire community of the B’nei Yisrael complained against Moshe and Aharon in the desert.


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:3)


וַאֲמָרוּ לְהוֹן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְוַי דְמִיתְנָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם כַּד הֲוֵינָא יָתְבִין עַל דוּדֵי בִסְרָא כַּד הֲוֵינָא אָכְלִין לַחְמָא וְשָׂבְעִין אֲרֵי אַפֶּקְתּוּן יָתָנָא לְמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין לְקַטָלָא יָת כָּל קְהָלָא הָדֵין בְּכַפְנָא:


The B’nei Yisrael said to them, “If only we had died by the hand of [**before**] Adonoy in the land of Egypt, when we sat by pots of meat and ate our fill of bread, for you have brought us out to this desert to cause this entire congregation to die of starvation.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:4)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה הָא אֲנָא **מָחֵית** לְכוֹן לַחְמָא מִן שְׁמַיָא וְיִפְקוּן עַמָא וְיִלְקְטוּן פִּתְגַם יוֹם בְּיוֹמֵהּ בְּדִיל דַאֲנַסִנוּן הַיְהָכוּן בְּאוֹרַיְתִי אִם לָא:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Behold, I will make bread rain [**come down**] from heaven for you, and the people shall go out and gather enough for each day, that I may test them [to see] if they will walk in [the way of] My teaching or not.


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:5)


וִיהֵי בְּיוֹמָא שְׁתִיתָאָה וִיתַקְנוּן יָת דְיַיְתוּן וִיהֵי עַל חַד תְּרֵין עַל דְיִלְקְטוּן יוֹם יוֹם:


On the sixth day [when] they prepare what they bring [home], it will be twice as much as they gather every [other] day.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:6)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן לְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּרַמְשָׁא וְתִדְעוּן אֲרֵי יְיָ אַפֵּיק יָתְכוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


Moshe and Aharon said to all the B’nei Yisrael, “In the evening you will know that [it is] Adonoy [Who] brought you out from the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:7)


וּבְצַפְרָא וְתֶחֱזוּן יָת יְקָרָא דַיְיָ **כַּד שְׁמִיעַ (קֳדָמוֹהִי)** יָת תֻּרְעֲמוּתְכוֹן **עַל מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וְנַחְנָא מָא אֲרֵי אַתְרַעַמְתּוּן עֲלָנָא:


And in the morning you will see the glory of Adonoy, for He has heard your complaints [**are heard (before Him)**] against [**regarding the Word of**] Adonoy. But what are we that you cause complaints against us?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:8)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה בִּדְיִתֵּן יְיָ לְכוֹן בְּרַמְשָׁא בִּסְרָא לְמֵיכַל וְלַחְמָא בְּצַפְרָא לְמִשְׂבַּע **בְּדִשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם** יְיָ יָת תֻּרְעֲמוּתְכוֹן דִי אַתּוּן מִתְרַעֲמִין עֲלוֹהִי וְנַחְנָא מָא לָא עֲלָנָא תֻּרְעֲמוּתְכוֹן אֶלָהֵן עַל **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ:


[Then] Moshe said, “When Adonoy gives you meat to eat in the evening, and a filling meal of bread in the morning, Adonoy will have heard your complaints [**will have been heard before Adonoy**], that you cause complaints against Him. What are we? Your complaints are not against us but against [**the Word of**] Adonoy.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:9)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרֹן אֱמַר לְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל קְרִיבוּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֲרֵי **שְׁמִיעַן קֳדָמוֹהִי** יָת תֻּרְעֲמוּתְכוֹן:


Then Moshe said to Aharon, “Say to the entire community of the B’nei Yisrael: Come near before Adonoy, for He has heard your complaints [**have been heard before Him**].”


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:10)


וַהֲוָה כַּד מַלִיל אַהֲרֹן עִם כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִתְפְּנִיוּ לְמַדְבְּרָא וְהָא יְקָרָא דַיְיָ **אִתְגְלִי** בַּעֲנָנָא:


As Aharon spoke to the entire community of the B’nei Yisrael, they turned toward to the desert, and behold the glory of Adonoy was visible [**revealed**] in the cloud.


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:11)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying,


###### Onkelos Exodus 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/16:12)

## Chapter 17

###### Onkelos Exodus 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:1)

The entire community of B’nei Yisrael journeyed from the wilderness of Sin, traveling according to the word of Adonoy. They camped in Rephidim, and there was no water for the people to drink.


###### Onkelos Exodus 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:2)

The people quarreled with Moshe, and said, “Give us water to drink.” Moshe said to them, “Why are you quarreling with me? Why are you testing [**before**] Adonoy?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 17:3

וּצְחִי תַמָן עַמָא לְמַיָא וְאִתְרַעַם עַמָא עַל משֶׁה וַאֲמַר לְמָא דְנַן אַסֶקְתָּנָא מִמִצְרַיִם לְקַטָלָא יָתִי וְיָת בְּנַי וְיָת בְּעִירַי בְּצָחוּתָא:


The people were suffering thirst for [the lack of] water, and the people complained against Moshe and said, “Why did you bring us up from Egypt—to kill me and my children, and my livestock with thirst?”


###### Onkelos Exodus 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:4)


**וְצַלִי** משֶׁה **קֳדָם** יְיָ לְמֵימַר מָא אֶעְבֵּד לְעַמָא הָדֵין עוֹד זְעֵיר פּוֹן וְיִרְגְמֻנָנִי:


Moshe cried out to [**prayed before**] Adonoy saying, “What shall I do for this people? Before long they will stone me.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/17:5)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עִבַּר קֳדָם עַמָא וּדְבַר עִמָךְ מִסָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְחֻטְרָךְ דִי מָחֵיתָא בֵהּ יָת נַהֲרָא סַב בִּידָךְ וְתֵיזִל:

###### Onkelos Exodus 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:3)


אִם בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי יֵיעוֹל בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי יִפּוֹק אִם בְּעֵל אִתְּתָא הוּא וְתִפּוֹק אִתְּתֵהּ עִמֵהּ:


If he came by himself [unmarried], he shall go out by himself. If he was married, his wife shall go out with him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:4)


אִם רִבּוֹנֵהּ יִתֶּן לֵהּ אִתְּתָא וּתְלִיד לֵהּ בְּנִין אוֹ בְנָן אִתְּתָא וּבְנָהָא תְּהֵי לְרִבּוֹנָהָא וְהוּא יִפּוֹק בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי:


If his master should give him a wife, and she bears him sons or daughters, then the woman and her children belong to her master, and he shall go out by himself.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:5)


וְאִם מֵימַר יֵימַר עַבְדָא רָחֵימְנָא יָת רִבּוֹנִי יָת אִתְּתִי וְיָת בְּנָי לָא אֶפּוֹק לְבַר חוֹרִין:


If the slave explicitly says, “I have come to love my master, my wife and my children, I do not want to go out free.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:6)


וִיקָרְבִנֵהּ רִבּוֹנֵהּ **לִקֳדָם** דַיָנַיָא וִיקָרְבִנֵהּ לְוָת דָשָׁא אוֹ לְוָת (נ"א דִילְוָת) מְזֻזְתָּא וְיַרְצַע רִבּוֹנֵהּ יָת אוּדְנֵהּ בְּמַרְצְעָא וִיהֵי לֵהּ עֲבַד **פָּלַח** לְעָלָם:


His master must bring him to [**before**] the judges. He must then bring him next to the door, or to the doorpost, and his master shall pierce his ear with an awl. He then serves his master [**will be a working servant for him,**] forever.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:7)


וַאֲרֵי יְזַבֵּן גְבַר יָת בְּרַתֵּהּ לְאַמְהוּ לָא תִפּוֹק כְּמַפְקָנוּת עַבְדַיָא:


If a man sells his daughter to be a maidservant, she shall not go out [free] in the manner of male slaves.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:8)


אִם בִּישָׁא בְּעֵינֵי רִבּוֹנַהּ **דִיקַיְמַהּ לֵהּ** וְיִפְרְקִנַהּ **לִגְבַר אָחֳרָן** לֵית לֵהּ רְשׁוּ לְזַבּוֹנַהּ **בְּמִשְׁלְטֵהּ** בַהּ:


If she is displeasing to her master, who had not designated [**for otherwise he would have designated**] her as his wife, he must allow her to be redeemed. He has no power [right] to sell her to an alien people [**another man**] for he has dealt deceitfully with [**controlled**] her.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:9)


וְאִם לִבְרֵהּ יְקַיְמִנַהּ כְּהִלְכַת בְּנַת **יִשְׂרָאֵל** יַעְבֵּד לַהּ:


If he has designated her as a wife for his son, he must grant her exactly the same rights as daughters [**of Yisrael**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:10)


אִם אָחֳרַנְתָּא יִסַב לֵהּ זִיוּנַהּ כְּסוּתַהּ וְעוֹנָתַהּ לָא יִמְנָע:


If he takes himself another [wife], her sustenance, her clothing and her conjugal rights he must not diminish.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:11)


וְאִם תְּלָת אִלֵין לָא יַעְבֵּד לַהּ וְתִפּוֹק מַגָן בְּלָא כְסַף:


If he does not do these three things to her, she goes out free with no repayment of money.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:12)


דְיִמְחֵי לֶאֱנַשׁ וְיִקְטְלִנֵהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל:


If one strikes a man and he [the victim] dies, he shall be put to death.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:13)


וּדְלָא כְמַן לֵהּ **וּמִן קֳדָם** יְיָ **אִתִּמְסַר** לִידֵהּ וֶאֱשַׁוִי לָךְ אֲתַר דִי יֵעִרוֹק לְתַמָן:


But if he did not lie in wait [to ambush him] but [**from before**] God [**he was**] brought it to his hand, then I will designate for you a place, to which he can flee [and find refuge].


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:14)


וַאֲרֵי יַרְשַׁע גְבַר עַל חַבְרֵהּ לְמִקְטְלֵהּ בִנְכִילוּ מִן מַדְבְּחִי תִּדְבְּרִנֵהּ לִמְמָת:


But if a man plots against his neighbor to kill him intentionally, you may [even] take him from My altar to put him to death.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:15)


וּדְיִמְחֵי אֲבוּהִי וְאִמֵהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל:


Whoever strikes his father or his mother shall be put to death.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:16)


וְדִיִגְנוּב **נַפְשָׁא מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל** וִיזַבְּנִנֵהּ וְיִשְׁתְּכַח בִּידֵהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל:


Whoever steals [kidnaps] a man [**person from B’nei Yisrael**] and sells him, if he is found in his hand, he shall be put to death.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:17)


וְדִיְלוּט אֲבוּהִי וְאִמֵהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל:


Whoever curses his father or his mother shall be put to death.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:18)


וַאֲרֵי יִנְצוּן גֻבְרִין וְיִמְחֵי גְבַר יָת חַבְרֵהּ בְּאַבְנָא אוֹ בְכוּרְמֵיזָא וְלָא יְמוּת **וְיִפּוֹל לְבוּטְלָן**:


When men quarrel and one man hits his fellow with a stone or with [his] fist; and he [the victim] does not die but becomes bedridden [**falls to idleness**],


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:19)


אִם יְקוּם וִיהַלִיךְ בְּבָרָא עַל בּוּרְיֵהּ וִיהֵי זַכָּאָה מָחְיָא לְחוֹד בּוּטְלָנֵהּ יִתֵּן **וַאֲגַר אַסְיָא** יְשַׁלֵם:


If he gets up and is able to walk on the outside on his own power, the one who struck him shall be acquitted. Still he must pay for his loss of work, and must pay for his complete cure [**the doctor’s fee**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:20)


וַאֲרֵי יִמְחֵי גְבַר יָת עַבְדֵהּ אוֹ יָת אַמְתֵהּ **בְּשָׁלְטָן** וִימוּת תְּחוֹת יְדֵהּ **אִתְדָנָא יִתְּדָן**:


If a man strikes his male or female slave with a rod [**power**], and [the slave] dies under his hand, [the death] must be avenged [**judged**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:21)


בְּרַם אִם יוֹמָא אוֹ תְרֵין יוֹמִין יִתְקַיָם לָא **יִתְּדָן** אֲרֵי כַסְפֵּהּ הוּא:


However if he [the slave] survives for a day or two, [his death] is not avenged [**judged**], for he [the slave] is his [master’s] property.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:22)


וַאֲרֵי יִנְצוּן גֻבְרִין וְיִמְחוּן אִתְּתָא מַעַדְיָא וְיִפְּקוּן וַלְדָהָא וְלָא יְהֵי מוֹתָא אִתְגְבָאָה יִתְגְבֵי כְּמָא דִי יְשַׁוִי עֲלוֹהִי בַּעֲלַהּ דְאִתְּתָא וְיִתֵּן עַל מֵימַר דַיָנַיָא:


If men will fight and they strike a pregnant woman, causing her to miscarry, but there is no fatal injury [to the woman], he [the guilty one] is to be punished with a [monetary] penalty when the husband demands compensation. He shall pay as determined by the judges.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:23)


וְאִם מוֹתָא יְהֵא וְתִתֵּן נַפְשָׁא חֳלָף נַפְשָׁא:


However if there is a fatal injury, you shall give [up] a life for a life.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:24)


עֵינָא חֳלָף עֵינָא שִׁנָא חֳלָף שִׁנָא יְדָא חֳלָף יְדָא רַגְלָא חֳלָף רַגְלָא:


[Compensation of] an eye for an eye, [of] a tooth for a tooth, [of] a hand for a hand, [of] a foot for a foot.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:25)


כְּוָאָה חֳלָף כְּוָאָה פִּדְעָא חֳלָף פִּדְעָא **מַשְׁקוֹפֵי** חֳלָף **מַשְׁקוֹפֵי**:


[Compensation of] a burn for a burn, [of] a wound for a wound, [of] a bruise [**beating**] for a bruise [**beating**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:26)


וַאֲרֵי יִמְחֵי גְבַר יָת עֵינָא דְעַבְדֵהּ אוֹ יָת עֵינָא דְאַמְתֵהּ וִיחַבְּלִנַהּ לְבַר חוֹרִין יִפְטְרִנֵהּ חֳלָף עֵינֵהּ:


If a man strikes the eye of his slave, or the eye of his female slave, and destroys [blinds] it, he shall set him [the slave] free as compensation for the eye.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:27)


וְאִם שִׁנָא דְעַבְדֵהּ אוֹ שִׁנָא דְאַמְתֵהּ יַפֵּל לְבַר חוֹרִין יִפְטְרִנֵהּ חֳלָף שִׁנֵהּ:


If [he knocks out] the tooth of his slave, or knocks out the tooth of his female slave, he shall set him [the slave] free as compensation for the tooth.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:28)


וַאֲרֵי יַגַח תּוֹרָא יָת גַבְרָא אוֹ יָת אִתְּתָא וִימוּת אִתְרְגָמָא יִתְרְגֵם תּוֹרָא וְלָא יִתְאָכֵל יָת בִּסְרֵהּ וּמָרֵהּ דְתוֹרָא יְהֵי זַכָּאָה:


If an ox gores a man or woman and the victim dies, the ox shall be stoned [to death], and its flesh may not be eaten. The owner of the ox shall go unpunished.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:29)


וְאִם תּוֹר נַגַח הוּא מֵאִתְּמַלֵי וּמִדְקַמוֹהִי וְיִתַּסְהֵד בְּמָרֵהּ וְלָא נַטְרֵהּ וְיִקְטוֹל גַבְרָא אוֹ אִתְּתָא תּוֹרָא יִתְרְגֵם וְאַף מָרֵהּ יִתְקְטֵל:


But if the ox had gored yesterday and the day before, and a warning was given to its owner, but he had not taken proper precautions to guard it, and kills a man or a woman, the ox must be stoned, and the owner also shall die [by Divine decree].


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:30)


אִם מָמוֹן יְשַׁווּן עֲלוֹהִי וְיִתֵּן פּוּרְקַן נַפְשֵׁהּ כְּכֹל דִי יְשַׁווּן עֲלוֹהִי:


When an atonement fine shall be imposed on him, he must give for the redemption of his soul whatever sum is imposed on him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:31)


אוֹ לְבַר **יִשְׂרָאֵל** יַגַח **תּוֹרָא** אוֹ לְבַת **יִשְׂרָאֵל** יַגָח כְּדִינָא הָדֵין יִתְעֲבֵד לֵהּ:


Whether it is a son [a minor lad] [**of Yisrael**] that is gored [**by the ox**] or a daughter [a minor girl] [**of Yisrael**] that it gored, this law shall apply to it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:32)


אִם לְעַבְדָא יַגַח תּוֹרָא אוֹ לְאַמְתָא כְּסַף תְּלָתִין **סִלְעִין** יִתֵּן לְרִבּוֹנֵהּ וְתוֹרָא יִתְרְגֵם:


If the ox gored a male or female slave, he must give thirty silver shekalim [**selaim**] to their master, and the ox must be stoned.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:33)


וַאֲרֵי יִפְתַּח גְבַר גוֹב אוֹ אֲרֵי יִכְרֶה גְבַר גוֹב וְלָא יְכַסִנֵהּ וְיִפֵּל תַּמָן תּוֹרָא אוֹ חֲמָרָא:


If a man uncovers a pit, or if a man digs a pit, and does not cover it, and an ox or a donkey falls into it;


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:34)


מָרֵי דְגֻבָּא יְשַׁלֵם כַּסְפָּא יָתִיב לְמָרוֹהִי וּמֵיתָא יְהֵי דִילֵהּ:


The owner of the pit must pay. He must compensate its owner with money, and the dead [animal] remains his [its original owner’s] possesion.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:35)


וַאֲרֵי יִגוֹף תּוֹר דִגְבַר יָת תּוֹרָא דְחַבְרֵהּ וִימוּת וִיזַבְּנוּן יָת תּוֹרָא חַיָא וִיפַלְגוּן יָת כַּסְפֵּהּ וְאַף יָת דְמֵי מֵיתָא יְפַלְגוּן:


If a man’s ox injures his neighbor’s ox and it dies, they shall sell the live ox and divide the money received for it. They shall also divide [the value of] the dead animal.


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:36)


אוֹ אִתְיְדַע אֲרֵי תוֹר נַגַח הוּא מֵאִתְּמַלֵי וּמִדְקַמוֹהִי וְלָא נָטְרֵהּ מָרֵהּ שַׁלָמָא יְשַׁלֵם תּוֹרָא חֲלַף תּוֹרָא וּמֵיתָא יְהֵא דִילֵהּ:


If it was known that it was a goring ox from yesterday and the day before, but he had not taken proper precaution to guard it, then he must pay full compensation, an ox for an ox. The dead [animal] remains in the possession [of its owner].


###### Onkelos Exodus 21:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/21:37)


אֲרֵי יִגְנוּב גְבַר תּוֹר אוֹ אִמָר וְיִכְּסִנֵהּ אוֹ יְזַבְּנִנֵהּ חַמְשָׁא תוֹרִין יְשַׁלֵם חָלָף תּוֹרָא וְאַרְבַּע עָנָא חֳלָף אִמְרָא:


If a man steals an ox or a sheep, and then slaughters or sells it, he must pay five oxen for each ox, and four sheep for each sheep.



## Chapter 22

###### Onkelos Exodus 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:1)


אִם בְּמַחְתַּרְתָּא יִשְׁתְּכַח גַנָבָא וְיִתְמְחֵי וִימוּת לֵית לֵהּ דָם:


If the thief is found [caught] while breaking in, and is struck and killed, there is no liability for his blood.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:2)


**אִם עֵינָא דְסָהֲדַיָא נְפָלַת עֲלוֹהִי** דְמָא לֵהּ שַׁלָמָא יְשַׁלֵם אִם לֵית לֵהּ וְיִזְדַבַּן בִּגְנֻבְתֵּהּ:


If the sun shone on him [**eyes of witnesses fell upon him**], there is liability for his blood. He must make full restitution. If he does not have [the means to do so], he shall be sold [as a slave to pay] for his theft.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:3)


אִם אִשְׁתְּכָחָא יִשְׁתְּכַח בִּידֵהּ גְנֻבְתָּא מִתּוֹר עַד חֲמַר עַד אִמַר **כַּד אִנוּן חַיִין** עַל חַד תְּרֵין יְשַׁלֵם:


If the stolen article is found in his hand [possession], whether it is an ox, donkey or sheep [**that is alive**], he must pay two live animals.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:4)


אֲרֵי יֹאכִיל גְבַר חֲקַל אוֹ כְרָם וִישַׁלַח יָת בְּעִירֵהּ וְיֵיכוּל בַּחֲקַל אָחֳרָן שְׁפַר חַקְלֵהּ וּשְׁפַר כַּרְמֵהּ יְשַׁלֵם:


If he damages a field or vineyard with his animal—he lets his livestock loose, and [or] it feeds in another’s field—from his [own] prime field and prime vineyard he must compensate [the owner].


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:5)


אֲרֵי תִתַּפַּק נוּר וְיַשְׁכַּח כּוּבִין וְיֵיכוּל גְדִישִׁין אוֹ קֳמָא אוֹ חַקְלָא שַׁלָמָא יְשַׁלֵם דְאַדְלֵק יָת דְלֶקְתָּא:


If a fire goes out of control and finds [catches on] thorns, and then consumes bound or standing grain or a field, full restitution must be made by the one who started the fire.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:6)


אֲרֵי יִתֵּן גְבַר לְחַבְרֵהּ כְּסַף אוֹ מָנִין לְמִטַר וְיִתְגַנְבוּן מִבֵּית גַבְרָא אִם יִשְׁתְּכַח גַנָבָא יְשַׁלֵם עַל חַד תְּרֵין:


If a man gives his neighbor money or articles to safeguard and they are stolen from the house of the [watch]man, if the thief is found, he must pay double.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:7)


אִם לָא יִשְׁתְּכַח גַנָבָא וְיִתְקָרֵב מָרֵי דְבֵיתָא לִקֳדָם דַיָנַיָא אִם לָא אוֹשִׁיט יְדֵהּ **בְּמָא דִמְסַר לֵהּ חַבְרֵהּ**:


If the thief is not found, the owner of the house [the watchman] must come before the judges [Beis Din], [to swear] that he did not lay a hand on his neighbor’s possessions [for his personal use] [**that which his neighbor handed over to him**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:8)


עַל כָּל פִּתְגַם דְחוֹב עַל תּוֹר עַל חֲמַר עַל אִמָר עַל כְּסוּ עַל כָּל אֲבֶדְתָּא דִי יֵימַר אֲרֵי הוּא דֵין לִקֳדָם דַיָנַיָא יֵיעוֹל דִין תַּרְוֵיהוֹן דִי יְחַיְבוּן דַיָנַיָא יְשַׁלֵם עַל חַד תְּרֵין לְחַבְרֵהּ:


In every question of dishonesty whether it involves an ox, a donkey, a sheep, a garment, or anything that was [allegedly] lost of which [a witness] says, “This is it!” the claims of both parties must be brought to [**before**] the judges. The one whom the judges find guilty, must pay double restitution to his neighbor.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:9)


אֲרֵי יִתֵּן גְבַר לְחַבְרֵהּ חֲמַר אוֹ תוֹר אוֹ אִמָר וְכָל בְּעִירָא לְמִטָר וּמִית אוֹ אִתְּבַר אוֹ אִשְׁתְּבִי לֵית דְחָזֵי:


If a man gives his neighbor, a donkey, an ox, a sheep, or any animal for safeguarding; and it dies, or is injured, or is captured, and there is no eyewitness,


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:10)


מוֹמָתָא דַיְיָ תְּהֵי בֵּין תַּרְוֵיהוֹן אִם לָא אוֹשִׁיט יְדֵהּ בְּמָא דִמְסַר לֵהּ חַבְרֵהּ וִיקַבֵּל מָרֵהּ מִנֵהּ מוֹמָתָא וְלָא יְשַׁלֵם:


an oath to Adonoy shall be between them, that he [the watcher] did not lay his hand on his neighbor’s possession [for his personal use] [**that which his neighbor handed over to him**]. The owner must accept it [the oath], and [the watcher] need not make restitution.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:11)


וְאִם אִתְגְנָבָא יִתְגְנֵב מֵעִמֵהּ יְשַׁלֵם לְמָרוֹהִי:


But if it was indeed stolen from him, he must make restitution to its owner.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:12)


אִם אִתְּבָרָא יִתְּבַר יַיְתִי סַהֲדִין **דִתְבִירָא** לָא יְשַׁלֵם:


If it was torn apart [killed by wild beasts], he [the watcher] must provide witnesses [**that is was torn apart**]. He then need not pay for that which was torn apart.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:13)


וַאֲרֵי יִשְׁאַל גְבַר מִן חַבְרֵהּ וְיִתְּבַר אוֹ מִית מָרֵהּ לֵית עִמֵהּ שַׁלָמָא יְשַׁלֵם:


If a man borrows something from his neighbor, and it becomes broken or dies; if the owner is not [involved] with him, he [the borrower] must make full restitution.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:14)


אִם מָרֵהּ עִמֵהּ לָא יְשַׁלֵם אִם אֲגִירָא הוּא עַל בְּאַגְרֵהּ:


If the owner is [involved] with him, then he [the borrower] need not make restitution. If it was hired, it [the ox] comes in its rental price.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:15)


וַאֲרֵי יְשַׁדֵל גְבַר בְתֻלְתָּא דִּי לָא מְאַרְסָא וְיִשְׁכּוּב עִמַהּ **קַיָמָא יְקַיְמִנַהּ** לֵהּ לְאִנְתּוּ:


If a man seduces a virgin who is not betrothed, and lies with her; he must give the dowry to acquire [**establish**] her as his wife.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:16)


אִם מִצְבָּא לָא יִצְבֵּי אֲבוּהָא לְמִתְּנַהּ לֵהּ כַּסְפָּא יִתְקוֹל כְּמוֹהֲרֵי בְּתֻלְתָּא:


If her father utterly refuses to give her to him, he must give [the father] the weight of silver equal to the dowry usually given to virgins.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:17)

###### Onkelos Exodus 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:18)


כָּל דְיִשְׁכּוּב עִם בְּעִירָא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל:


Whoever lies with an animal must be put to death.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:19)


דְדַבַּח **לְטַעְוַת עַמְמַיָא** יִתְקְטֵל אֶלָהֵן **לִשְׁמָא דַ**יְיָ בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי:


Whoever slaughters to a god [**false gods of the peoples**] must be condemned [to die]. [Sacrifices may be offered] to [**the Name of**] Adonoy alone.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:20)


וְגִיוֹרָא לָא תוֹנוּן וְלָא תְעִיקוּן לֵהּ אֲרֵי דַיָרִין הֱוֵיתוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


You must not abuse or oppress a stranger, for you were strangers in the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:21)


כָּל אַרְמְלָא וְיִתָּם לָא תְעַנוּן:


You must not mistreat any widow or orphan.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:22)


אִם עַנָאָה תְעַנֵי יָתֵהּ אֲרֵי אִם מִקְבַל יִקְבֵל **קֳדָמַי קַבָּלָא אֱקַבֵּל** קְבִלְתֵּהּ:


If you do mistreat him [them], when he cries out to [**before**] Me, I will indeed hear [**obey**] his cry.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:23)


וְיִתְקֵף רוּגְזִי וְאֶקְטוֹל יָתְכוֹן בְּחַרְבָּא וִיהֶוְיָן נְשֵׁיכוֹן אַרְמְלִין וּבְנֵיכוֹן יַתְמִין:


Then I will cause My anger to flare, and I will kill you by the sword. Your wives will become widows and your children will be orphans.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:24)


אִם כַּסְפָּא תּוֹזֵף בְּעַמִי לְעַנְיָא דִי עִמָךְ לָא תְהֵי לֵהּ כְּרַשְׁיָא לָא תְשַׁוּוֹן עֲלוֹהִי **חִבּוּלְיָא**:


When you lend money to My people, to the poor person who is with [among] you, do not act toward him as a [demanding] creditor. Do not place the burden of interest [**an injury**] upon him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:25)


אִם מַשְׁכּוֹנָא תִסַב כְּסוּתָא דְחַבְרָךְ עַד מֵעַל שִׁמְשָׁא תְּתִיבִנֵהּ לֵהּ:


If you take your neighbor’s garment as security, you must return it to him till sunset;


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:26)


אֲרֵי הִיא כְסוּתֵהּ בִּלְחוֹדַהּ הִיא תוֹתְבֵהּ לְמַשְׁכֵּיהּ בְּמָא יִשְׁכּוּב וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יִקְבֵל **קֳדָמַי וֶאֱקַבֵּל** קְבִלְתֵּהּ אֲרֵי חַנָנָא אֲנָא:


For this alone is his covering, the garment [to cover] his skin. With what shall he lie down [to sleep]? If it happens that he cries out to [**before**] Me, I will hear [**obey**] [his cry] for I am gracious.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:27)


דַיָנָא לָא תְקִיל וְרַבָּא בְעַמָךְ לָא תְלוּט:


Do not curse judges. Do not curse a leader of your people.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:28)


בִּכּוּרָךְ וְדִמְעָךְ לָא תְאַחֵר בּוּכְרָא דִבְנָיךְ **תַּפְרֵשׁ** קֳדָמָי:


[Your offering of] the fullness [of your newly ripened produce] and of your terumah-offering’s you must not delay. The first-born of your sons, you shall give to [**separate before**] Me.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:29)


כֵּן תַּעְבֵּד לְתוֹרָךְ לְעָנָךְ שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין יְהֵי עִם אִמֵהּ בְּיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה **תִּפְרְשִׁנֵהּ** קֳדָמָי:


You must do likewise with your oxen and with your sheep. Seven days it shall remain with its mother. On the eighth day you must give it to [**separate it before**] Me.


###### Onkelos Exodus 22:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/22:30)


וֶאֱנָשִׁין קַדִישִׁין תְּהוֹן **קֳדָמָי** וּבְשַׂר תְּלִישׁ **מִן חֵיוָא חַיְתָא** לָא תֵיכְלוּן לְכַלְבָּא תִּרְמוּן יָתֵהּ:


You shall be men of holiness to [**before**] Me, and you must not eat flesh that was torn off in the field [**from a living animal**]. Throw it to the dog.



## Chapter 23

###### Onkelos Exodus 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:1)


לָא תְקַבֵּל שְׁמַע דִשְׁקָר לָא תְשַׁוִי יְדָךְ עִם חַיָבָא לְמֶהֱוֵי **לֵהּ** סָהִיד שְׁקָר:


Do not accept a false report. Do not join hands with a wicked man to be a corrupt [false] witness [**for him**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:2)

Do not follow the majority to do evil. Do not respond with your opinion in a dispute [merely] to lean [toward one side] [**(Do not) refrain from teaching what is proper in your eyes regarding a judgment**]. It must be decided by the majority.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:3)

Do not show favor [even] to the poor in his dispute [**judgment**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:4)


אֲרֵי תִפְגַע תּוֹרָא דְסָנְאָךְ אוֹ חֲמָרֵהּ דְטָעֵי אָתָבָא תְּתִיבִנֵהּ לֵהּ:


If you come across your enemy’s ox or his donkey going astray, bring it back to him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:5)


אֲרֵי תֶחֱזֵי חֲמָרָא דְסָנְאָךְ רְבִיעַ תְּחוֹת טוֹעֲנֵיהּ **וְתִתִּמְנַע מִלְמִשְׁבַּק לֵהּ** מִשְׁבַּק **תִּשְׁבּוֹק מָא דִבְלִבָּךְ עֲלוֹהִי וּתְפָרֵק עִמֵהּ**:


If you see the donkey of your enemy lying under its burden, and you might not want to help him [**stop yourself from abandoning him**], [but you should] [**[you should]**] make every effort to help him [**abandon what is in you heart against him and unload with him**] .


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:6)

Keep far away from anything false. Do not kill an [**one who is**] innocent [**or one who goes out innocent from judgment**] righteous man, for I will not acquit a wicked person.

Do not oppress a stranger. You [should] know the soul [feelings] of the stranger, for you were strangers in the land of Egypt.

Preserve everything that I have said to you. Do not mention the name of other [**false**] gods [**of the peoples**]. You must not cause it to be heard through your mouth.

[This festival must be celebrated] at the appointed time, during the month when the grain is ripened, for in it [at this time] you went out of Egypt. Do not appear before My Presence [**Me**] empty-handed.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:18)

The beginning of the first fruits of your land must be brought to the House of [**the Sanctuary of**] Adonoy, your God. You must not cook a young animal in the milk of its mother [**eat meat with milk**].

אִסְתַּמַּר מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי וְקַבֵּל **לְמֵימְרֵהּ** לָא תְסָרֵב לְקִבְלֵהּ אֲרֵי לָא יִשְׁבּוֹק לְחוֹבֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי **בִ**שְׁמִי **מֵימְרֵהּ**:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:23)

###### Onkelos Exodus 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:24)

You must not prostrate yourself to their [**false**] gods, nor serve [sacrifice to] them. You must not adopt their customs. You must totally destroy [their idols] and completely break down their monuments.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:25)


וְתִפְלְחוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן וִיבָרֵךְ יָת **מֵיכְלָךְ** וְיָת **מִשְׁתְּיָךְ** וְאַעְדִי מַרְעִין **בִּישִׁין** מִבֵּינָךְ:


You must serve [**before**] Adonoy, your God, and He will bless your bread [**food**] and your water [**drink**]. I will eliminate [**bad**] sickness from your midst.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:26)


לָא תְהֵי מְתַכְּלָא וְעַקְרָא בְּאַרְעָךְ יָת מִנְיַן יוֹמָיךְ אַשְׁלִים:


No woman will suffer miscarriage or remain childless in your land. I will cause the number of your days to be full.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:27)


יָת אֵימְתִי אֲשַׁלַח קֳדָמָךְ וְאִיתְבַּר (נ"א וֶאֱשַׁגֵשׁ) יָת כָּל עַמָא דְאַתְּ אָתֵי **לַאֲגָחָא בְּהוֹן** וְאֶמְסַר יָת כָּל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ **קֳדָמָךְ** מְחַזְרֵי קְדָל:


I will send My dread ahead of you, and I will bring panic to all the people among whom you will come [**to fight against**]. I will make all your enemies turn their backs to [**before**] you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:28)


וְאֶשְׁלַח יָת עֲרָעִיתָא קֳדָמָךְ וּתְתָרֵךְ יָת חִוָאֵי יָת כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וְיָת חִתָּאֵי מִן קֳדָמָךְ:


I will send [deadly] hornets ahead of you, and they will drive out the Chivites, the Canaanites, and the Chittites from before you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:29)


לָא אֱתָרֵכִנוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ בְּשַׁתָּא חֲדָא דִילְמָא תְהֵי אַרְעָא צַדְיָא וְתִסְגֵי עֲלָךְ חֵיוַת בָּרָא:


I will not drive them out in a single year, lest the land become desolate, and the beasts of the field multiply against you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:30)


זְעֵיר זְעֵיר אֱתָרֵכִנוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ עַד דְתִסְגֵי וְתַחְסֵן יָת אַרְעָא:


Little by little I will drive them from before you, until you have increased and occupy the land.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:31)


וֶאֱשַׁוֵי יָת תְּחוּמָךְ מִיַמָא דְסוּף וְעַד יַמָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּמִמַדְבְּרָא עַד פְּרָת אֲרֵי אֶמְסַר בִּידֵיכוֹן יָת יָתְבֵי אַרְעָא וּתְתָרֵכִנוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ:


I will set your borders from the Reed Sea to the Philistine Sea, and from the desert to the [Euphrates] river, for I will deliver into your hands all the inhabitants of the land, and you will drive them away before you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:32)


לָא תִגְזַר לְהוֹן **וּלְטַעֲוָתְהוֹן** קְיָם:


Do not make a pact with them or with their [**false**] gods.


###### Onkelos Exodus 23:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/23:33)


לָא יֵתְבוּן בְּאַרְעָךְ דִילְמָא יְחַיְבוּן יָתָךְ **קֳדָמָי** אֲרֵי תִפְלַח יָת טַעֲוָתְהוֹן אֲרֵי יְהוֹן לָךְ לְתַקְלָא:


Do not allow them to live in your land, since they may cause you to sin to [**before**] Me; that you [might] worship their [**false**] gods, and this would be a [fatal] trap to you.

###### Onkelos Exodus 24:1

וּלְמשֶׁה אֲמַר סַק לִ**קֳדָם** יְיָ אַתְּ וְאַהֲרֹן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא וְשִׁבְעִין מִסָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתִסְגְדוּן מֵרָחִיק:


He [God] had said to Moshe, ‘Go up to [the Presence of] [**before**] Adonoy, you, Aharon, Nadav, Avihu, and seventy of the elders of [the B’nei] Yisrael, and prostrate yourselves at a distance.

Moshe alone shall approach [the Presence of] [**before**] Adonoy, but they must not approach, and the people must not go up with him.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:3)


וַאֲתָא משֶׁה וְאִשְׁתָּעִי לְעַמָא יָת כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא דַיְיָ וְיָת כָּל דִינַיָא וַאֲתֵיב כָּל עַמָא קָלָא חַד וַאֲמָרוּ כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא דִי מַלִיל יְיָ נַעְבֵּד:


Moshe came and told the people all the words of Adonoy, and all the laws. The people responded with one voice and said, “All the words that Adonoy has spoken, we will do.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:4)

Moshe wrote down all the words of Adonoy. He arose early in the morning, and built an altar beneath [**at the foot of**] the mountain, and [also] twelve monuments [pillars] for the twelve tribes of [the B’nei] Yisrael.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:5)

Moshe took half the blood and put it in the basins, and [the other] half the blood he sprinkled on the altar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 24:7

Moshe then took the blood and cast it on the [**Altar to atone on behalf of the**] people, and he said, “Behold, this is the blood of the covenant which Adonoy has made with you regarding all these words.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:9)


וּסְלֵיק משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא וְשִׁבְעִין מִסָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל

###### Onkelos Exodus 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:10)


וַחֲזוֹ יָת **יְקַר** אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וּתְחוֹת **כָּרְסָא יְקָרֵהּ** כְּעוֹבַד **אֶבֶן טָבָא וּכְמֶחֱזֵי** שְׁמַיָא לְבָרִירוּ:


They saw [a vision of] the [**Glory of the**] God of Yisrael, and under His feet [**the throne of His Glory**] [there was something] like a brickwork of sapphire [**precious stone**], and it was like the essence [**image**] of heaven in purity.

But against the nobility of the B’nei Yisrael, He [God] did not send His hand [**there was no damage**]. They envisioned [**saw the Glory of**] God, and they [**rejoiced in their sacrifices which were accepted graciously as if they**] ate and drank.

Adonoy then said to Moshe, “Come up to [**before**] Me to the mountain and remain there. I will give you the tablets of stone, the Torah and the commandment, which I have written [in order] to teach them.”

Moshe and Yehoshua, his attendant, set out, but [only] Moshe went up to the mountain [**upon which the Glory**] of God [**was revealed**].

He [Moshe] said to the elders, “Wait here for us until we return to you, and Aharon and Chur will be with you. Whoever has a claim can approach [**before**] them.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/24:15)

Moshe went up to the mountain, and the cloud covered the mountain.

The appearance of Adonoy’s glory was like [**the appearance of**] a consuming flame at the top to the mountain; before the eyes of the B’nei Yisrael.

And Moshe came into the midst of the cloud. and went up into the mountain. Moshe remained on the mountain for forty days and forty nights.

###### Onkelos Exodus 25:1

Speak to the B’nei Yisrael and have them take for [**separate before**] Me a terumah-offering. From every man whose heart impels him to generosity shall you take My terumah-offering.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:3)


וְדָא אַפְרָשׁוּתָא דִּתִסְּבוּן מִנְּהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וְכַסְפָּא וּנְחָשָׁא:


This is the terumah-offering that you shall take from them: gold, silver and copper.

Greenish-blue [wool], dark red [wool], crimson [**colored**] [wool], fine linen, and goats [hair].
