[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:5)


וּמַשְׁכֵי דְדִכְרֵי מְסַמְּקֵי וּמַשְׁכֵי **סַסְגּוֹנָא** וְאָעֵי שִׁטִּין:


Red[-dyed] ram’s skins, tachash [**multicolored**] skins, and acacia wood.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:6)


מִשְׁחָא לְאַנְהָרוּתָא בּוּסְמַיָּא לְמִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְלִקְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָּא:


Oil for the lamp. Spices for anointing-oil, and for the incense of aromatic spices.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:7)


אַבְנֵי בוּרְלָא וְאַבְנֵי אַשְׁלָמוּתָא **לְשַׁקָּעָא** בְאֵפוֹדָא וּבְחוּשְׁנָא:


Onyx stones, and filling stones [**for setting**] [into] the eiphod and breastplate.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:8)


וְיַעְבְּדוּן **קֳדָמַי** מִקְדְּשָׁא **וְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִנְתִּי בֵּינֵיהוֹן**:


They shall make a Sanctuary for [**before**] Me, and I will [then] dwell in their midst [**rest My Shechina among them**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:9)


כְּכֹל דִּי אֲנָא מַחֲזֵי יָתָךְ יָת דְּמוּת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיָת דְּמוּת כָּל מָנוֹהִי וְכֵן תַּעְבְּדוּן:


According to all that I am showing you—the form of the mishkon [tabernacle] and the form of all its vessels—and so should you make [it].


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:10)


וְיַעְבְּדוּן אֲרוֹנָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין וּפַלְגָּא אֻרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָּא פְּתָיֵהּ וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָּא רוּמֵיהּ:


They shall make an ark of acacia wood, two and one-half amohs long, one and one-half amohs wide, and one and one-half amohs high.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:11)


וְתֶחֱפֵי יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי מִגָּו וּמִבָּרָא תַּחֲפִנֵּיהּ וְתַעְבֵּד עֲלוֹהִי זֵיר דִּדְהַב סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


Cover it with pure gold—you shall cover it inside and out. Make for it a golden crown-like rim around [its top].


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:12)


וְתַתִּיךְ לֵיהּ אַרְבַּע עִזְקָן דִּדְהַב וְתִתֵּן עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָתֵיהּ וְתַרְתֵּין עִזְקָן עַל סִטְרֵיהּ חַד וְתַרְתֵּין עִזְקָן עַל סִטְרֵיהּ תִּנְיֵתָא:


Cast four gold rings, and place them on its [the ark’s] four corners, two rings on one side, and two rings on the second side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:13)


וְתַעְבֵּד אֲרִיחֵי דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וְתַחֲפֵי יָתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא:


Make [two] poles of acacia wood, and cover them with gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:14)


וְתָעֵל יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא בְּעִזְקָתָא עַל סִטְרֵי אֲרוֹנָא לְמִטַּל יָת אֲרוֹנָא בְּהוֹן:


Place the poles in the rings on the sides of the ark, so that the ark can be carried with them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:15)


בְּעִזְקְתָא דַּאֲרוֹנָא יְהוֹן אֲרִיחַיָּא לָא יְעִדּוּן מִנֵּיהּ:


In the rings of the ark, must the poles remain. They must not be removed from them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:16)


וְתִתֵּן בַּאֲרוֹנָא יָת סַהֲדוּתָא דִּי אֶתֵּן לָךְ:


You shall then place in the ark, the Testimony that I will give you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:17)


וְתַעְבֵּד כַּפֻּרְתָּא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין וּפַלְגָּא אֻרְכַּהּ וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָּא פְּתָיַהּ:


Make a cover of pure gold [for the ark], two and one-half amohs long, and one and one-half amohs wide.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:18)


וְתַעְבֵּד תְּרֵין כְּרוּבִין דִּדְהָב נְגִיד תַּעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן מִתְּרֵין סִטְרֵי כַפֻּרְתָּא:


Make two golden cherubim. You must make them by hammering them out of the two ends of the [ark] cover.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:19)


וְעִבֵּד כְּרוּבָא חַד **מִסִּטְרָא** מִכָּא וּכְרוּבָא חַד **מִסִּטְרָא** מִכָּא מִן כַּפֻּרְתָּא תַּעְבְּדוּן יָת כְּרוּבַיָּא עַל תְּרֵין **סִטְרוֹהִי**:


Make one cherub out of one end [**side**], and one cherub out of the other end [**side**]. From the [same piece of gold as] the cover itself, you shall make the cherubim on its two ends [**sides**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:20)


וִיהוֹן כְּרוּבַיָּא פְּרִיסָן גַּדְפֵיהוֹן לְעֵלָּא מְטַלִּין בְּגַדְפֵיהוֹן עַל כַּפֻּרְתָּא וְאַפֵּיהוֹן חַד לָקֳבֵל חַד לָקֳבֵל כַּפֻּרְתָּא יְהוֹן אַפֵּי כְרוּבַיָּא:


The cherubim shall spread their wings upward, sheltering the cover with their wings, and the cherubim shall face one another. Their faces shall be [inclined] toward the cover.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:21)


וְתִתֵּן יָת כַּפֻּרְתָּא עַל אֲרוֹנָא מִלְּעֵלָּא וּבַאֲרוֹנָא תִּתֵּן יָת סַהֲדוּתָא דִּי אֶתֵּן לָךְ:


Place the cover on top of the ark and place the Testimony in the ark that I will give you.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:22)


**וֶאֱזַמֵּן מֵימְרִי** לָךְ תַּמָּן וֶאֱמַלֵּל עִמָּךְ מֵעִלָּוֵי כַפֻּרְתָּא מִבֵּין תְּרֵין כְּרוּבַיָּא דִּי עַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא יָת כָּל דִּי אֲפַקֵּד יָתָךְ לְוָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


I will set [**prepare**] My meetings [**Word**] with you there, and I will speak with you from above the cover, from between the cherubim that are on the Ark of the Testimony. All that which I will command you concerning the B’nei Yisrael.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:23)


וְתַעְבֵּד פָּתוֹרָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין אֻרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְּתָא פְתָיֵהּ וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָּא רוּמֵיהּ:


You shall make a table out of acacia wood, two amohs will be its length, one amoh will be its width, and one and one-half amohs its height.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:24)


וְתַחֲפֵי יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי וְתַעְבֵּד לֵיהּ זֵיר דִּדְהַב סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


Overlay it with pure gold, and make a gold crown-like rim around it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:25)


וְתַעְבֵּד לֵיהּ גְּדַנְפָא **רוּמֵיהּ** פֻּשְׁכָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר וְתַעְבֵּד זֵיר דִּדְהַב לִגְדַנְפֵיהּ סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


Make a ledge of a tefach [**high**] around [the table], and make a gold crown-like rim around the ledge.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:26)


וְתַעְבֵּד לֵיהּ אַרְבַּע עִזְקָן דִּדְהָב וְתִתֵּן יָת עִזְקָתָא עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָתָא דִּי לְאַרְבַּע רַגְלוֹהִי:


Make four gold rings [for the table], and place the rings on the four corners that are on its legs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:27)


לָקֳבֵל גְּדַנְפָא יְהֶוְיָן עִזְקָתָא לְאַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָּא לְמִטַּל יָת פָּתוֹרָא:


The rings shall be against the ledge, and they shall be houses [receptacles] for the poles with which to carry the table.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:28)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וְתַחֲפֵי יָתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וִיהוֹן נָטְלִין בְּהוֹן יָת פָּתוֹרָא:


Make the poles out of acacia wood, and overlay them with gold. The table will be carried by them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:29)


וְתַעְבֵּד מָגִיסוֹהִי וּבָזִכּוֹהִי וְקַסְוָתֵיהּ וּמְכִילָתֵיהּ דִּיִתְנַסַּךְ בְּהֵן דְּהַב דְּכֵי תַּעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן:


Make its dishes [bread forms], spoons, its half-hollow tubes, and its supports, by which they [the breads] shall be covered. Make them out of pure gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:30)


וְתִתֵּן עַל פָּתוֹרָא לְחֵם אַפַּיָּא קֳדָמַי תְּדִירָא:


You shall place the showbread upon the table, before Me at all times.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:31)


וְתַעְבֵּד מְנַרְתָּא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי נְגִיד תִּתְעֲבֵד מְנַרְתָּא שִׁדַּהּ וּקְנַהּ כַּלִּידָהָא חֵזוּרָהָא וְשׁוֹשַׁנָּהָא מִנַּהּ יְהוֹן:


You shall make a menorah [out] of pure gold. The menorah must be made by hammering [its form out of a solid piece of gold]. Its base, its shaft, its cups, its knobs, and its flowers shall be of it [the same piece of gold].


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:32)


וְשִׁתָּא קְנִין נָפְקִין מִסִּטְרָהָא תְּלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִסִּטְרַהּ חַד וּתְלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִסִּטְרַהּ תִּנְיָנָא:


Six branches shall extend from its sides, three branches of the menorah from one of its sides, and three branches of the menorah from the second side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:33)


תְּלָתָא כַלִּידִין מְצַיְרִין בְּקַנְיָא חַד חֵזוּר וְשׁוֹשַׁן וּתְלָתָא כַלִּידִין מְצַיְרִין בְּקַנְיָא חַד חֵזוּר וְשׁוֹשָׁן כֵּן לְשִׁתָּא קְנִין דְּנָפְקִין מִן מְנַרְתָּא:


Three cups, engraved in almond shaped patterns, on one branch, with a knob and a flower. And three cups engraved in almond shaped patterns, on one branch, with a knob and a flower. So shall be [the form] of each of the six branches that extend from [the shaft of] the menorah.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:34)


וּבִמְנַרְתָּא אַרְבְּעָא כַלִּידִין מְצַיְרִין חֵזוּרָהָא וְשׁוֹשַׁנָּהָא:


The [shaft of the] menorah shall have four cups, engraved in almond shaped patterns, with their knobs and their flowers.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:35)


וְחֵזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין מִנַּהּ וְחֵזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין מִנַּהּ וְחֵזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין מִנַּהּ לְשִׁתָּא קְנִין דְּנָפְקִין מִן מְנַרְתָּא:


[There shall be] a knob under the two branches extending from it. [There should be] a [second] knob under the [second] two branches extending from it. [There should be] a [third] knob under the [third] two branches extending from it. [This holds true] for the six branches that extend from [the shaft of] the menorah.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:36)


חֵזוּרֵיהוֹן וּקְנֵיהוֹן מִנַּהּ יְהוֹן כֻּלַּהּ נְגִידָא חֲדָא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי:


Their knobs and their branches shall be made out of [the menorah] itself. It shall all be made of a single hammered work of pure gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:37)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת בּוֹצִינָהָא שִׁבְעָא וְתַדְלֵק יָת בּוֹצִינָהָא וִיהוֹן מְנַהֲרִין עַל קֳבֵל אַפָּהָא:


You shall make seven lamps [for the menorah]. and its lamps shall be lit so that they shine toward it [the center lamp].


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:38)


וְצִבְיָתָהָא וּמַחְתְּיָתָהָא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי:


Its tongs and its [ash] scoops [shall also be made of] pure gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:39)


כִּכְּרָא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי יַעְבֵּד יָתַהּ יָת כָּל מָנַיָּא הָאִלֵּין:


It shall be made out of a kikar of pure gold [together] with all these vessels.


###### Onkelos Exodus 25:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/25:40)


וַחֲזֵי וַעֲבֵיד כִּדְמוּתְהוֹן דִּאַתְּ מִתְחֲזִי בְּטוּרָא:


Observe [carefully] and make them like the pattern that will be shown to you on the mountain.



## Chapter 26

###### Onkelos Exodus 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:1)


וְיָת מַשְׁכְּנָא תַּעְבֵּד עֲשַׂר יְרִיעָן דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי צוּרַת כְּרוּבִין עוֹבַד אֳמָן תַּעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן:


You shall [then] make the mishkon (Tabernacle) out of ten drapes, [consisting of] fine twined linen, greenish blue, dark red and crimson [**colored**] [wool]. Figures of cherubim, the work of a craftsman, shall be woven into them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:2)


אֻרְכָּא דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עַשְׂרִין וְתַמְנֵי אַמִּין וּפוּתְיָא אַרְבַּע אַמִּין דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מְשַׁחְתָּא חֲדָא לְכָל יְרִיעֲתָא:


Each drape shall be twenty eight amohs long, and each drape shall be four amohs wide. All the drapes shall have the same measure.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:3)


חֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעָן יְהֶוְיַן מְלַפְּפָן חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא וַחֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעָן מְלַפְּפָן חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא:


Five of these drapes shall be joined [sewn] together and [the other] five drapes shall be joined [sewn] together.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:4)


וְתַעְבֵּד עֲנוּבִין דְּתִכְלָא עַל שִׂפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מִסִּטְרָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי וְכֵן תַּעְבֵּד בְּשִׂפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא בְּסִטְרָא דְּבֵית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיֵתָא:


Make loops of greenish blue [wool] on the edge of the drape which is at the [innermost] end of the [first] group. Do the same on the edge of the drape which is at the [innermost] end of the second group.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:5)


חַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין תַּעְבֵּד בִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין תַּעְבֵּד בְּסִטְרָא דִירִיעֲתָא דְּבֵית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיָנָא מְכַוְּנָן עֲנוּבַיָּא חֲדָא לָקֳבֵל חֲדָא:


Make fifty loops on [the edge of] one drape, and make fifty loops on the edge of the drape which is on the second group. The loops shall be opposite one another.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:6)


וְתַעְבֵּד חַמְשִׁין פּוּרְפִין דִּדְהָב וּתְלָפֵף יָת יְרִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא בְּפוּרְפַיָּא וִיהֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא חָד:


Make fifty golden clasps, and join the drapes [of each group] together with the clasps, so that the mishkon is one.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:7)


וְתַעְבֵּד יְרִיעָן מְעַזֵּי לִפְרָסָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא חֲדָא עֶסְרֵי יְרִיעָן תַּעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן:


Make drapes of goats hair for a covering over the mishkon. Make eleven drapes.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:8)


אֻרְכָּא דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא תְּלָתִין אַמִּין וּפוּתְיָא אַרְבַּע אַמִּין דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מְשַׁחְתָּא חֲדָא לַחֲדָא עֶסְרֵי יְרִיעָן:


The length of each drape shall be thirty amohs, and the width of each drape shall be four amohs. The eleven drapes must have the same measure.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:9)


וּתְלָפֵף יָת חֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעָן לְחוֹד וְיָת שִׁית יְרִיעָן לְחוֹד וְתֵיעוּף יָת יְרִיעֲתָא שְׁתִיתֵיתָא לָקֳבֵל אַפֵּי מַשְׁכְּנָא:


Join [sew] together five drapes by themselves, and [the other] six drapes by themselves. Let the sixth drape overhang before the entrance of the tent [mishkon].


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:10)


וְתַעְבֵּד חַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עַל שִׂפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא בְּסִטְרָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עַל שִׂפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא דְּבֵית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיֵתָא:


Make fifty loops on the edge of one drape which is at the [innermost] end of the [first] group, and fifty loops on the edge of the drape which is on the second group.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:11)


וְתַעְבֵּד פּוּרְפִין דִּנְחָשָׁא חַמְשִׁין וְתָעֵיל יָת פּוּרְפַיָּא בַּעֲנוּבַיָּא וּתְלָפֵף יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא וִיהֵי חָד:


Make fifty copper clasps. Bring the clasps into the loops, and join the tent together, making it one.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:12)


וְסִרְחָא דְּיַתִּירָא בִּירִיעַת מַשְׁכְּנָא פַּלְגּוּת יְרִיעֲתָא דְּיַתִּירָא תִּסְרַח עַל אֲחֹרֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא:


The overlapping excess [from what is left over] [in the breadth] of the drapes of the tent—half of the extra drape shall overlap the back of the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:13)


וְאַמְּתָא מִכָּא וְאַמְּתָא מִכָּא בִּדְיַתִּירָא בְּאֻרְכָּא יְרִיעֲתָא דְמַשְׁכְּנָא יְהֵי סְרִיחַ עַל סִטְרֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא מִכָּא וּמִכָּא לְכַסָּיוּתֵיהּ:


The [extra] amoh on one side, and the [extra] amoh on the other side that were in excess of the length of the drapes of the tent shall overlap the sides of the mishkon, on both sides, to cover it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:14)


וְתַעְבֵּד חוֹפָאָה לִפְרָסָא מַשְׁכֵי דְדִכְרֵי מְסַמְּקֵי וְחוֹפָאָה מַשְׁכֵי **דְסַסְגּוֹנָא** מִלְּעֵלָּא:


Make a covering for the tent out of red dyed ram’s skins, and a covering of tachash [**multicolored**] skins above that.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:15)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת דַּפַּיָּא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין קָיְמִין:


Make the planks for the mishkon out of acacia wood; [these planks are] to stand upright [vertically].


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:16)


עֲשַׂר אַמִּין אֻרְכָּא דְדַפָּא וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגּוּת אַמְּתָא פּוּתְיָא דְּדַפָּא חָד:


Each plank shall be ten amohs long, and one and one half amohs shall be the width of each plank.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:17)


תַּרְתֵּין צִירִין לְדַפָּא חַד מְשַׁלְּבִין חֲדָא לָקֳבֵל חֲדָא כֵּן תַּעְבֵּד לְכֹל דַּפֵּי מַשְׁכְּנָא:


Each plank shall have two [square] pegs, exactly parallel to each other. So shall you make all the planks of the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:18)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת דַּפַּיָּא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא עַסְרִין דַּפִּין לְרוּחַ עֵבַר דָּרוֹמָא:


Make these planks for the mishkon, twenty planks for the southern side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:19)


וְאַרְבְּעִין סַמְכִין דִּכְסַף תַּעְבֵּד תְּחוֹת עַסְרִין דַּפִּין תְּרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חַד לִתְרֵין צִירוֹהִי וּתְרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חַד לִתְרֵין צִירוֹהִי:


Make forty silver sockets, [and place them] under the twenty planks. There shall be two sockets under one plank [one] for each of the two pegs, and two sockets under the other plank, [one] for each of the two pegs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:20)


וְלִסְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא תִּנְיֵתָא לְרוּחַ צִפּוּנָא עַסְרִין דַּפִּין:


For the second wall of the mishkon, to the northern side, [also make] twenty planks.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:21)


וְאַרְבְּעִין סַמְכֵיהוֹן דִּכְסַף תְּרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חַד וּתְרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חָד:


[Make] their forty silver sockets, two sockets under each plank, two sockets under each plank.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:22)


וְלִסְיָפֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא מַעַרְבָּא תַּעְבֵּד שִׁתָּא דַפִּין:


For the west end of the mishkon make six planks.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:23)


וּתְרֵין דַּפִּין תַּעְבֵּד לְזָוְיַת מַשְׁכְּנָא **בְּסוֹפֵיהוֹן**:


Make two planks for the corners of the mishkon at the rear side [**their ends**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:24)


וִיהוֹן מְכַוְּנִין מִלְּרַע וְכַחֲדָא יְהוֹן מְכַוְּנִין עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ לְעִזְקְתָא חֲדָא כֵּן יְהֵי לְתַרְוֵיהוֹן לִתְרֵין זָוְיָן יְהוֹן:


They must be coupled together on the bottom, and they must also be coupled together evenly on the top with one [square] ring. So must it be done with the two [planks], which are at the two corners.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:25)


וִיהוֹן תְּמַנְיָא דַפִּין וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן דִּכְסַף שִׁתָּא עֲשַׂר סַמְכִין תְּרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חַד וּתְרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חָד:


Thus there will be eight planks, and their silver sockets, totaling sixteen sockets, two sockets under each plank, two sockets under each plank.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:26)


וְתַעְבֵּד עַבְּרֵי דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין חַמְשָׁא לְדַפֵּי סְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא חָד:


You shall make bars out of acacia wood, five for the planks of one wall,


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:27)


וְחַמְשָׁא עַבְּרִין לְדַפֵּי סְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא תִּנְיֵתָא וְחַמְשָׁא עַבְּרִין לְדַפֵּי סְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא לְסוֹפֵיהוֹן מַעַרְבָא:


and five bars for the planks of the second wall of the mishkon. [There shall also be] five bars for the planks of the western [back] wall of the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:28)


וְעַבְּרָא מְצִיעָאָה בְּגוֹ דַפַּיָּא מַעְבַּר מִן סְיָפֵי לִסְיָפֵי:


The middle [center] bar shall go through the planks, reinforcing the planks from one end to the other.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:29)


וְיָת דַּפַּיָּא תַּחְפֵּי דַהֲבָא וְיָת עִזְקָתְהוֹן תַּעְבֵּד דַּהֲבָא אַתְרָא לְעַבְרַיָּא וְתַחְפֵּי יָת עַבְּרַיָּא דַּהֲבָא:


You shall overlay the planks with gold, and make their rings out of gold, to hold the bars. You shall [also] overlay the bars with gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:30)


וּתְקִים יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא כְּהִלְכְּתֵיהּ דִּי אִתְחֲזֵיתָא בְּטוּרָא:


You shall erect the mishkon according to its rules. As shown to you on the mountain.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:31)


וְתַעְבֵּד פָּרֻכְתָּא תִּכְלָא וְאַגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד אֳמָן יַעְבֵּד יָתַהּ צוּרַת כְּרוּבִין:


You shall make a paroches [partition] out of greenish blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and twined, fine linen. It shall be the work of a craftsman, with figures of cherubim [woven into it].


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:32)


וְתִתֵּן יָתַהּ עַל אַרְבְּעָא עַמּוּדֵי שִׁטִּין מְחַפְּיָן דַּהֲבָא וָוֵיהוֹן דַּהֲבָא עַל אַרְבְּעָא סַמְכֵי דִכְסָף:


Place it [the paroches] upon four acacia-wood pillars, that are overlaid with gold. Their hooks shall [also] be of gold. These pillars are to be set into four silver sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:33)


וְתִתֵּן יָת פָּרֻכְתָּא תְּחוֹת פּוּרְפַיָּא וְתָעֵל תַּמָּן מִגָּו לְפָרֻכְתָּא יָת אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְתַפְרֵשׁ פָּרֻכְתָּא לְכוֹן בֵּין קוּדְשָׁא וּבֵין קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָּא:


Place the paroches under the clasps [which kept the drapes together]. Bring into the space within [behind] the paroches, the Ark of the Testimony, and the paroches will divide for you between the Holy and the Holy of Holies.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:34)


וְתִתֵּן יָת כַּפֻּרְתָּא עַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא בְּקֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָּא:


Place the cover upon the Ark of the Testimony in the Holy of Holies.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:35)


וּתְשַׁוִּי יָת פָּתוֹרָא מִבָּרָא לְפָרֻכְתָּא וְיָת מְנַרְתָּא לָקֳבֵל פָּתוֹרָא עַל צִדָּא דְמַשְׁכְּנָא דָּרוֹמָא וּפָתוֹרָא תִתֵּן עַל סְטַר צִפּוּנָא:


Put the table outside the paroches, and the menorah opposite the table, toward the southern side of the mishkon. The table should be placed on the northern side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:36)


וְתַעְבֵּד פְּרָסָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד צַיָּר:


You shall make a screen for the entrance of the tent. [Make it out of] greenish blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and twined, fine linen. It shall be the work of an embroiderer.


###### Onkelos Exodus 26:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/26:37)


וְתַעְבֵּד לִפְרָסָא חַמְשָׁא עַמּוּדֵי שִׁטִּין וְתַחֲפֵּי יָתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וָוֵיהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וְתַתִּיךְ לְהוֹן חַמְשָׁא סַמְכֵי נְחָשָׁא:


Make five pillars of acacia wood for the screen. Overlay them with gold and their hooks are to be gold. Cast five copper sockets for them [the pillars].



## Chapter 27

###### Onkelos Exodus 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:1)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת מַדְבְּחָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין חֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין אֻרְכָּא וַחֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין פּוּתְיָא מְרַבַּע יְהֵי מַדְבְּחָא וּתְלַת אַמִּין רוּמֵיהּ:


You shall make the altar out of acacia wood. It shall be five amohs long, and five amohs wide. The altar shall be square, and its height shall be three amohs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:2)


וְתַעְבֵּד קַרְנוֹהִי עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָתֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ תְּהֶוְיָן קַרְנוֹהִי וְתַחֲפֵּי יָתֵיהּ נְחָשָׁא:


Make its raised projections on its four corners. Its raised projections shall be of one piece with it, and you shall overlay it [the altar] with copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:3)


וְתַעְבֵּד פְּסַכְתְּרָוָתֵיהּ **לְמִסְפֵי** קִטְמֵיהּ וּמַגְרוֹפְיָתֵיהּ וּמִזְרְקָתֵיהּ וְצִנּוּרְיָתֵיהּ וּמַחְתְּיָתֵיהּ לְכָל מָנוֹהִי תַּעְבֵּד נְחָשָׁא:


Make its pots to remove [**collect**] its ashes, [also] its scoops, its basins, its flesh hooks, and its fire pans. Make all of its vessels out of copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:4)


וְתַעְבֵּד לֵיהּ סְרָדָא עוֹבַד מְצַדְתָּא דִנְחָשָׁא וְתַעְבֵּד עַל מְצַדְתָּא אַרְבַּע עִזְקָן דִּנְחָשָׁא עַל אַרְבַּע סִטְרוֹהִי:


Make for it a grate of copper netting. On the grate you shall make four copper rings on its four corners.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:5)


וְתִתֵּן יָתַהּ תְּחוֹת סוֹבָבֵי מַדְבְּחָא מִלְּרָע וּתְהֵי מְצַדְתָּא עַד פַּלְגּוּת מַדְבְּחָא:


Place it under the rim of the altar, extending downward, so that the grate reaches the middle of the altar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:6)


וְתַעְבֵּד אֲרִיחַיָּא לְמַדְבְּחָא אֲרִיחֵי דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וְתַחֲפֵי יָתְהוֹן נְחָשָׁא:


Make poles for the altar, poles of acacia wood, and overlay them with copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:7)


וְיָעֵל יָת אֲרִיחוֹהִי בְּעִזְקָתָא וִיהוֹן אֲרִיחַיָּא עַל תְּרֵין סִטְרֵי מַדְבְּחָא בְּמִטַּל יָתֵיהּ:


Place its poles into the rings, and the poles shall be on the two sides of the altar when it is carried.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:8)


חֲלִיל לוּחֵי תַּעְבֵּד יָתֵיהּ כְּמָא דִי אַחֲזִי יָתָךְ בְּטוּרָא כֵּן יַעְבְּדוּן:


It should be a hollow structure constructed from boards. As it was shown to you on the mountain, so shall they make it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:9)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת דָּרַת מַשְׁכְּנָא לְרוּחַ עֵבַר דָּרוֹמָא סְרָדִין לְדָרְתָּא דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר מְאָה בְאַמִּין אֻרְכָּא לְעֵבַר חָד:


You shall make the enclosure for the mishkon. On the south side there shall be curtains for the enclosure made of fine twined linen. It shall be one hundred amohs long on one side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:10)


וְעַמּוּדוֹהִי עֶשְׂרִין וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן עֶשְׂרִין נְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָּא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן כְּסָף:


It shall have twenty pillars, and they shall have twenty copper sockets. The hooks and bands of the pillars shall be of silver.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:11)


וְכֵן לְרוּחַ צִפּוּנָא בְּאֻרְכָּא סְרָדִין מְאָה אֻרְכָּא וְעַמּוּדוֹהִי עֶשְׂרִין וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן עֶשְׂרִין נְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָּא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף:


The same shall be on the north side. The curtains shall be one hundred amohs long, with twenty pillars and twenty copper sockets. The hooks and bands of the pillar shall be of silver.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:12)


וּפוּתְיָא דְדַרְתָּא לְרוּחַ מַעַרְבָּא סְרָדִין חַמְשִׁין אַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עַשְׂרָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן עַשְׂרָא:


For the width of the enclosure on the west side there shall be curtains of fifty amohs. They shall have ten pillars and ten sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:13)


וּפוּתְיָא דְדַרְתָּא לְרוּחַ קִדּוּמָא מַדִּינְחָא חַמְשִׁין אַמִּין:


The width of the enclosure on the east side shall be fifty amohs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:14)


וַחֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵי אַמִּין סְרָדִין לְעִבְרָא עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא:


There shall be curtains fifteen amohs long on one flank [of the entrance]. They shall have three pillars and three sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:15)


וּלְעִבְרָא תִּנְיָנָא חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵי סְרָדִין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא:


The other flank shall [also] have curtains of fifteen amohs. They [too] shall have three pillars and three sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:16)


וְלִתְרַע דַּרְתָּא פְּרָסָא עֶשְׂרִין אַמִּין תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד צַיָּר עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעָא:


The entrance of the enclosure shall have a screen of twenty amohs. [It shall be made of] greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and twined, fine linen. It shall be the work of an embroiderer. It [the screen] shall have four pillars and four sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:17)


כָּל עַמּוּדֵי דַרְתָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר מְכַבְּשִׁין כְּסַף וָוֵיהוֹן כְּסַף וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן נְחָשָׁא:


All the pillars around the enclosure shall have silver bands, and silver hooks, and copper sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:18)


אֻרְכָּא דְדַרְתָּא מְאָה בְאַמִּין וּפוּתְיָא חַמְשִׁין בְּחַמְשִׁין וְרוּמָא חֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן נְחָשָׁא:


The length of the enclosure shall be one hundred amohs. The width shall be fifty by fifty amohs. The height [of the pillars holding up the curtains] of twisted fine linen shall be five amohs, and their sockets shall be [made] of copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:19)


לְכֹל מָאנֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא בְּכֹל פָּלְחָנֵיהּ וְכָל סִכּוֹהִי וְכָל סִכֵּי דְדַרְתָּא נְחָשָׁא:


All the vessels of the mishkon in all its service, all of its stakes and all the stakes of the enclosure, shall be made of copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:20)


וְאַתְּ תְּפַקֵּד יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִסְּבוּן לָךְ מִשְׁחָא זֵיתָא דַכְיָא כָּתִישָׁא לְאַנְהָרָא לְאַדְלָקָא בוֹצִינַיָּא תְּדִירָא:


You shall command the B’nei Yisrael and have them bring you clear olive oil, [made from olives that were] crushed for lighting, to keep the lamp burning constantly.


###### Onkelos Exodus 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/27:21)


בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא מִבָּרָא לְפָרֻכְתָּא דִּעַל סַהֲדוּתָא יְסַדַּר יָתֵיהּ אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי מֵרַמְשָׁא עַד צַפְרָא קֳדָם יְיָ קְיָם עָלָם לְדָרֵיהוֹן מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


In the Tent of Meeting, outside the paroches, which is before the Testimony, Aharon and his sons shall arrange it [for the lamp to burn] from evening to morning before Adonoy. This is an everlasting statute for their generations of the B’nei Yisrael.



## Chapter 28

###### Onkelos Exodus 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:1)


וְאַתְּ קָרֵב לְוָתָךְ יָת אַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ מִגּוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְשַׁמָּשָׁא **קֳדָמָי** אַהֲרֹן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא אֶלְעָזָר וְאִיתָמָר בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן:


And [now separate] and take unto you your brother, Aharon, and his sons with him, from among the B’nei Yisrael, to serve [**before**] Me [as kohanim]: Aharon, Nadav, Avihu, Elazar, and Isamar, the sons of Aharon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:2)


וְתַעְבֵּד לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא לְאַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ לִיקָר וּלְתֻשְׁבְּחָן:


Make sacred garments for your brother, Aharon, for honor and splendor.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:3)


וְאַתְּ תְּמַלֵּל עִם כָּל חַכִּימֵי לִבָּא דִּאַשְׁלֵמִית עִמֵּיהּ רוּחַ חָכְמְתָא וְיַעְבְּדוּן יָת לְבוּשֵׁי אַהֲרֹן לְקַדָּשׁוּתֵיהּ לְשַׁמָּשָׁא **קֳדָמַי**:


Speak to all who are wise in heart, whom I have filled with the spirit of wisdom, and have them make Aharon’s garments, to sanctify him to serve [**before**] Me [as kohein].


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:4)


וְאִלֵּין לְבוּשַׁיָּא דִּי יַעְבְּדוּן חוּשְׁנָא וְאֵפוֹדָא וּמְעִילָא וְכִתּוּנָא מְרַמְּצָא מִצְנֶפְתָּא וְהֶמְיָנָא וְיַעְבְּדוּן לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא לְאַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ וְלִבְנוֹהִי לְשַׁמָּשָׁא **קֳדָמַי**:


These are the garments that they shall make: a breastplate, an eiphod, a robe, a checkered undershirt, a turban and a sash-belt. Have them make sacred garments for your brother Aharon, and for his sons, to serve [**before**] Me [as kohanim].


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:5)


וְאִנּוּן יִסְּבוּן יָת דַּהֲבָא וְיָת תִּכְלָא וְיָת אַרְגְּוָנָא וְיָת **צְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וְיָת בּוּצָא:


They shall take the gold, the greenish-blue wool, the dark red wool the crimson [**colored**] wool, and the fine twined linen.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:6)


וְיַעְבְּדוּן יָת אֵפוֹדָא דַּהֲבָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **צְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד אֳמָן:


They shall make the eiphod out of gold, greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and fine twined linen, the work of a craftsman.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:7)

It shall have two shoulder straps attached to it, they shall be attached at its two corners [**sides**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:8)


וְהֶמְיַן **תִּקּוּנֵיהּ** דִּי עֲלוֹהִי כְּעוֹבָדוֹהִי מִנֵּיהּ יְהֵי דַּהֲבָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר:


The eiphod’s belt [**that enhances it**], which is on it, shall be made in the same manner [as the eiphod]—it shall be woven with it. [with] gold, greenish blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool and fine twined linen.

Take two shoham [onyx] stones and engrave on them the names of the B’nei Yisrael.

וּתְשַׁוִּי יָת תַּרְתֵּין אַבְנַיָא עַל כִּתְפֵּי אֵפוֹדָא אַבְנֵי דָכְרָנָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִטּוֹל אַהֲרֹן יָת שְׁמָהָתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ עַל תְּרֵין כִּתְפּוֹהִי לְדָכְרָנָא:

Make gold settings,

Make a breastplate of judgment, the work of a craftsman. Make it in the same manner you made the eiphod. [Out of] gold, greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool and fine twined linen, shall you make it.

It shall be a doubled square, a span in its length and a span in its width.

Fill it [the breastplate settings] with fillings of [mounted] stones. Their shall be four rows of [**precious**] stones: [**The first row:**] An odem [ruby], pitda [topaz], and a barekes [emerald], [shall be placed on] the first row [**one**].

The third row: leshem [opal], shevo [agate], and achlomo [amethyst].

The fourth row: tarshish [chrysolite], shoham [onyx], and yoshfei [jasper]. [These stones] shall be placed in gold settings in their fullness.

Make [two] twisted end-chains for the breastplate, braided work of pure gold.

Make two gold ring’s for the breastplate, and set the two rings on the two [upper] corners [**sides**] for the breastplate.

Place [attach] the two gold-braided chains on the two rings, at the corners [**sides**] of the breastplate.

The two ends of the two braided chains [**which are on its two sides**], you shall place [attach] to the two settings, and place them on the shoulder straps of the eiphod toward its face.

וְתַעְבֵּד תַּרְתֵּין עִזְקָן דִּדְהַב וּתְשַׁוִּי יָתְהוֹן עַל תְּרֵין **סִטְרֵי** חוּשְׁנָא עַל סִפְתֵיהּ דִּי לְעִבְרָא דְאֵפוֹדָא מִלְּגָיו:


Make two gold rings and set them on the two [lower] corners [**sides**] of the breastplate, on its edge that is on the inner side of the eiphod.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:27)

Make two gold rings and place them on the two shoulder straps of the eiphod, on the bottom, toward its face, a gainst where they are joined, above the eiphod’s belt.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:28)


וְיֶחְדּוּן יָת חוּשְׁנָא מֵעִזְקְתֵיהּ לְעִזְקְתָא דְאֵפוֹדָא בְּחוּטָא דִתְכֶלְתָּא לְמֵהֱוֵי עַל הֶמְיַן אֵפוֹדָא וְלָא יִתְפָּרֵק חוּשְׁנָא מֵעִלָּוֵי אֵפוֹדָא:


They shall bind the [lower] rings of the breastplate to the [lower] rings of the eiphod with a cord of greenish-blue wool, so that it [the breastplate] remains against the eiphod’s belt, so that the breastplate does not move from the eiphod.

Aharon will thus carry the names of the B’nei Yisrael on the breastplate of judgment, over his heart, when he comes into the Holy [sanctuary], as a constant remembrance before Adonoy.

וְתִתֵּן בְּחשֶׁן דִּינָא יָת אוּרַיָּא וְיָת תֻּמַּיָּא וִיהוֹן עַל לִבָּא דְאַהֲרֹן בְּמֵעֲלֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִטּוֹל אַהֲרֹן יָת דִּינָא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל לִבֵּיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ תְּדִירָא:


Place in the breastplate the Urim and Tumim, and they will be over Aharon’s heart when he comes before Adonoy. Aharon will carry the judgment of the B’nei Yisrael over his heart, before Adonoy always.
