[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:31)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת מְעִילָא דְאֵפוֹדָא גְּמִיר תִּכְלָא:


Make the robe of the eiphod completely out of greenish-blue wool.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:32)


וִיהֵי פוּם רֵישֵׁיהּ כָּפִיל לְגַוֵּיהּ תּוֹרָא יְהֵי מַקַּף לְפוּמֵיהּ **סְחוֹר סְחוֹר** עוֹבַד מָחֵי כְּפוּם שִׁרְיָן יְהֵי לֵיהּ לָא יִתְבְּזָע:


Its opening at the top shall be turned inward. Its opening shall have a border [**encircling**] all around, [this border shall be] of woven work, like there is around the opening of a coat of armor, so that it will not be torn.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:33)


וְתַעְבֵּד עַל שִׁיפוֹלוֹהִי רִמּוֹנֵי תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי עַל שִׁיפוֹלוֹהִי סְחוֹר סְחוֹר וְזַגִּין דְּדַהֲבָא בֵּינֵיהוֹן סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


On its [lower] hem you shall make pomegranates of greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, and crimson [**colored**] wool—around its [lower] hem. There shall be gold bells between them, all around.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:34)


זַגָּא דְדַהֲבָא וְרִמּוֹנָא זַגָא דְדַהֲבָא וְרִמוֹנָא עַל שִׁיפוֹלֵי מְעִילָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


A gold bell and a pomegranate, a gold bell and a pomegranate, on the [lower] hem of the robe all around.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:35)


וִיהֵי עַל אַהֲרֹן לְשַׁמָּשָׁא וְיִשְׁתְּמַע קָלֵיהּ בְּמֵעֲלֵיהּ לְקוּדְשָׁא קֳדָם יְיָ וּבְמִפְּקֵיהּ וְלָא יְמוּת:


It shall be on Aharon when he performs [Divine] service. Its sound shall be heard when he comes into the Holy [sanctuary] in the presence of Adonoy, and when he comes out, so that he will not die.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:36)


וְתַעְבֵּד צִיצָא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי וְתִגְלוֹף עֲלוֹהִי **כְּתַב מְפָרַשׁ** קֹדֶשׁ לַייָ:


You shall make a diadem of pure gold, and engrave on it in the manner of a signet [ring] [**script that is clear**], “Holy unto Adonoy.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:37)


וּתְשַׁוִּי יָתֵיהּ עַל חוּטָא דִתְכֶלְתָּא וִיהֵי עַל מִצְנֶפְתָּא לָקֳבֵל אַפֵּי מִצְנֶפְתָּא יְהֵי:


Set [fasten] it on a greenish-blue wool cord, so that it will be on the turban. It shall be near the front of the turban.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:38)


וִיהֵי עַל בֵּית עֵינוֹהִי דְאַהֲרֹן וְיִטּוֹל אַהֲרֹן יָת עֲוָיַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא דִּי יְקַדְּשׁוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכָל מַתְּנַת קוּדְשֵׁיהוֹן וִיהֵי עַל בֵּית עֵינוֹהִי תְּדִירָא לְרַעֲוָא לְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ:


It shall be [worn] on Aharon’s forehead, and Aharon will carry [atone] the iniquity of the sacred offerings that the B’nei Yisrael consecrate for all their sacred gifts. It shall be on his forehead always to make them [the sacred-offerings] acceptable before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:39)


וּתְרַמֵּץ כִּתּוּנָא בוּצָא וְתַעְבֵּד מִצְנֶפְתָּא דְבוּצָא וְהֶמְיָנָא תַּעְבֵּד עוֹבַד צַיָּר:


Make the undershirt with checkered work of fine linen. Make the turban of fine linen. and the sash-belt shall be the work of an embroiderer.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:40)


וְלִבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן תַּעְבֵּד כִּתּוּנִין וְתַעְבֵּד לְהוֹן הֶמְיָנִין וְכוֹבָעִין תַּעְבֵּד לְהוֹן לִיקָר וּלְתֻשְׁבְּחָן:


For Aharon’s sons, make undershirts, and [also] make sash-belts for them. Make turban-like hats for them, for honor and splendor.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:41)


וְתַלְבֵּשׁ יָתְהוֹן יָת אַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ וּתְרַבֵּי יָתְהוֹן **וּתְקָרֵב יָת קֻרְבַּנְהוֹן** וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתְהוֹן וִישַׁמְּשׁוּן **קֳדָמָי**:


Clothe your brother, Aharon, with [these garments], and also [clothe] his sons along with him. [Then] anoint them, and install them [**offer their sacrifices**], and consecrate them to serve [**before**] Me [as kohanim].


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:42)


וְעִבֵד לְהוֹן מִכְנְסִין דְּבוּץ לְכַסָּאָה בְּשַׂר עֶרְיְתָא מֵחַרְצָן וְעַד יַרְכָּן יְהוֹן:


Make for them linen pants to cover their nakedness, reaching from their waists to their thighs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 28:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/28:43)


וִיהוֹן עַל אַהֲרֹן וְעַל בְּנוֹהִי בְּמֵעַלְהוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא אוֹ בְמִקְרָבְהוֹן לְמַדְבְּחָא לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בְקוּדְשָׁא וְלָא יְקַבְּלוּן חוֹבָא וִימוּתוּן קְיָם עָלָם לֵיהּ וְלִבְנוֹהִי בַתְרוֹהִי:


These [garments] must be worn by Aharon and his sons whenever they come into Tent of Meeting, or approach the altar to serve in the Holy [sanctuary], in order that they not bear iniquity and die. This is an everlasting statute for him and his descendants after him.



## Chapter 29

###### Onkelos Exodus 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:1)


וְדֵין פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי תַעְבֵּד לְהוֹן לְקַדָּשָׁא יָתְהוֹן לְשַׁמָּשָׁא **קֳדָמָי** סַב תּוֹר חָד בַּר תּוֹרֵי וְדִכְרִין תְּרֵין שַׁלְמִין:


This is what you must do for them to consecrate them to serve [**before**] Me [as kohanim]. Take one young bull and two unblemished rams,


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:2)


וּלְחֵם פַּטִּיר וּגְרִיצִין פַּטִּירִין דְּפִילָן בִּמְשַׁח וְאֶסְפּוֹגִין פַּטִּירִין דִּמְשִׁיחִין בִּמְשָׁח סֻלְתָּא חִטִּין תַּעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן:


[also] unleavened bread, unleavened loaves mixed with [olive] oil. and unleavened wafers anointed with oil. You must make them with fine wheat flour.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:3)


וְתִתֵּן יָתְהוֹן עַל סַלָּא חַד וּתְקָרֵב יָתְהוֹן בְּסַלָּא וְיָת תּוֹרָא וְיָת תְּרֵין דִּכְרִין:


Place them in one basket and bring them in the basket [along] with the young bull and the two rams.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:4)


וְיָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי תְקָרֵב לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִּמְנָא וְתַסְחֵי יָתְהוֹן בְּמַיָּא:


Bring Aharon and his sons near to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, and immerse them in the water.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:5)


וְתִסַּב יָת לְבוּשַׁיָּא וְתַלְבֵּשׁ יָת אַהֲרֹן יָת כִּתּוּנָא וְיָת מְעִיל אֵפוֹדָא וְיָת אֵפוֹדָא וְיָת חוּשְׁנָא **וְתַתְקֵן** לֵיהּ בְּהֶמְיַן אֵפוֹדָא:


Take the garments and clothe Aharon with the undershirt, the robe of the eiphod, the eiphod, the breastplate, and gird [**beautify**] him with the belt of the eiphod.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:6)


וּתְשַׁוִּי מִצְנֶפְתָּא עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ וְתִתֵּן יָת כְּלִילָא דְּקוּדְשָׁא עַל מִצְנֶפְתָּא:


Set the turban upon his head and place the sacred diadem upon the turban.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:7)


וְתִסַּב יָת מִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וּתְרִיק עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ וּתְרַבִּי יָתֵיהּ:


Take the anointing oil and pour it on his head and anoint him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:8)


וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי תְּקָרֵב וְתַלְבֵּשִׁנּוּן כִּתּוּנִין:


Bring his sons near and clothe them with undershirts.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:9)


וּתְזָרֵז יָתְהוֹן הֶמְיָנִין אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי וְתַתְקַן לְהוֹן כּוֹבָעִין וּתְהֵי לְהוֹן כְּהֻנְתָּא לִקְיָם עָלָם **וּתְקָרֵב קֻרְבָּנָא דְ**אַהֲרֹן **וְקֻרְבָּנָא** דִבְנוֹהִי:


Gird them—Aharon and his sons—with sash-belts, and bind turban-like hats for them, and the priesthood shall be for them an everlasting statute. Thus you shall install [**offer the sacrifice of**] Aharon and [**the sacrifice of**] his sons.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:10)


וּתְקָרֵב יָת תּוֹרָא לִקֳדָם מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיִסְמוֹךְ אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי יָת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ תּוֹרָא:


Bring the young bull before the Tent of Meeting, and Aharon and his sons shall support their hands on the head of the young bull.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:11)


וְתִכּוֹס יָת תּוֹרָא קֳדָם יְיָ בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Slaughter the bull before Adonoy, at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:12)


וְתִסַּב מִדְּמָא דְתוֹרָא וְתִתֵּן עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא בְּאֶצְבָּעָךְ וְיָת כָּל דְּמָא תֵישׁוֹד לִיסוֹדָא דְּמַדְבְּחָא:


Take of the blood of the bull and put it on the horn-like elevations of the altar with your finger, and pour all the [remaining] blood at the base of the altar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:13)


וְתִסַּב יָת כָּל תַּרְבָּא דְּחָפֵי יָת גַּוָּא וְיָת חִצְרָא דִּעַל כַּבְדָא וְיָת תַּרְתֵּין כָּלְיָן וְיָת תַּרְבָּא דִּי עֲלֵיהֶן וְתַסֵּיק לְמַדְבְּחָא:


Take all the fat that covers the inner organs, the lobe that is with the liver, the two kidneys with the fat that is upon them, and burn them on the altar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:14)


וְיָת בִּסְרָא דְתוֹרָא וְיָת מַשְׁכֵּיהּ וְיָת אוּכְלֵיהּ תּוֹקִיד בְּנוּרָא מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא חַטָּאתָא הוּא:


[However] the flesh of the bull, its skin, and its intestinal waste, you shall burn with fire outside the camp. It is a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:15)


וְיָת דִּכְרָא חַד תִּסַּב וְיִסְמְכוּן אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי יָת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ דִּכְרָא:


Take the first ram and have Aharon and his sons support their hands on the head of the ram.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:16)


וְתִכּוֹס יָת דִּכְרָא וְתִסַּב יָת דְּמֵיהּ וְתִזְרוֹק עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


Slaughter the ram, and take its blood, and sprinkle it around the altar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:17)


וְיָת דִּכְרָא תְּפַלֵּג **לְאֵבָרוֹהִי** וּתְחַלִּיל גַּוֵּיהּ וּכְרָעוֹהִי וְתִתֵּן עַל **אֵבָרוֹהִי** וְעַל רֵישֵׁיהּ:


Cut the ram into pieces [**its limbs**]. [Then] wash off its intestines and its legs and place them with its cut up pieces [**limbs**] and its head.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:18)


וְתַסֵּיק יָת כָּל דִּכְרָא לְמַדְבְּחָא עֲלָתָא הוּא **קֳדָם** יְיָ **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֻרְבָּנָא** קֳדָם יְיָ הוּא:


Burn the entire ram on the altar, it is a burnt-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, a pleasing fragrance [**to be accepted with favor**], a fire-offering to [**sacrifice before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:19)


וְתִסַּב יָת דִּכְרָא תִנְיָנָא וְיִסְמוֹךְ אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי יָת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ דִּכְרָא:


Take the second ram and have Aharon and his sons support their hands on the head of the ram.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:20)


וְתִכּוֹס יָת דִּכְרָא וְתִסַּב מִדְמֵיהּ וְתִתֵּן עַל רוּם אוּדְנָא דְאַהֲרֹן וְעַל רוּם אוּדְנָא דִבְנוֹהִי דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן יְדֵיהוֹן דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן רַגְלֵיהוֹן דְּיַמִּינָא וְתִזְרוֹק יָת דְּמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


Slaughter the ram, and take of its blood, and place it on the tip of Aharon’s right ear, and on the tip of the right ear of his sons, and on the thumb of their right hands, and on the big toe of their right feet. Sprinkle the [remaining] blood all around the altar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:21)


וְתִסַּב מִן דְּמָא דִּי עַל מַדְבְּחָא וּמִמִּשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְתַדֵּי עַל אַהֲרֹן וְעַל לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְעַל בְּנוֹהִי וְעַל לְבוּשֵׁי בְנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ הוּא וּלְבוּשׁוֹהִי וּבְנוֹהִי וּלְבוּשֵׁי בְנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ:


Take of the blood that is on the altar, and of the anointing oil and sprinkle it upon Aharon and his garments, and upon his sons and their garments as well. Thus he and his garments will be consecrated, along with his sons and his son’s garments.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:22)


וְתִסַּב מִן דִּכְרָא תַּרְבָּא וְאַלִּיתָא וְיָת תַּרְבָּא דְחָפֵי יָת גַּוָּא וְיָת חֲצַר כַּבְדָּא וְיָת תַּרְתֵּין כָּלְיָן וְיָת תַּרְבָּא דִּי עֲלֵיהֶן וְיָת שׁוֹקָא דְיַמִּינָא אֲרֵי דְּכַר **קֻרְבָּנָא** הוּא:


Take from the ram the [intestinal] fat, the broad tail, the fat that covers the inner organs, the lobe of the liver, the two kidneys, and the fat that is upon them, and the right shoulder, for it is a ram of a completion-offering [**offering**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:23)


וּפִתָּא דִלְחֵם חַד וּגְרִצְתָּא דִלְחֵם מְשַׁח חֲדָא וְאֶסְפּוֹג חַד מִסַּלָּא דְפַטִּירַיָּא דִּי קֳדָם יְיָ:


[Take also] one bread-cake, and one loaf of oiled bread, and one wafer from the basket of unleavened bread that is before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:24)


וּתְשַׁוִּי כֹלָּא עַל יְדָא דְאַהֲרֹן וְעַל יְדָא דִבְנוֹהִי וּתְרִים יָתְהוֹן אֲרָמוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Place all [these things] upon the [open] hands of Aharon, and upon the [open] hands of his sons, and you shall wave them for wave-offering before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:25)


וְתִסַּב יָתְהוֹן מִידֵיהוֹן וְתַסֵּיק לְמַדְבְּחָא עַל עֲלָתָא **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ **קֻרְבָּנָא** הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Then take [these things] from their hands and burn them on the altar after the burnt-offering as a pleasing fragrance [**to be accepted with favor**] before Adonoy. It is a fire-offering to [**sacrifice before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:26)


וְתִסַּב יָת חֶדְיָא מִדְּכַר **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** דִּי לְאַהֲרֹן וּתְרִים יָתֵיהּ אֲרָמוּתָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ וִיהֵי לָךְ לָחֳלָק:


Take the breast from Aharon’s installation ram [**offering**] and wave it as wave-offering to [**before**] Adonoy. Then it shall be your [Moshe’s] portion.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:27)


וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָת חֶדְיָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא וְיָת שׁוֹקָא דְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא דִּי אִתָּרַם וְדִי אִתַּפְרָשׁ מִדְּכַר **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** מִדִּי לְאַהֲרֹן וּמִדִּי לִבְנוֹהִי:


Sanctify the breast of the wave-offering, and the shoulder of the terumah-offering which were [the] waved and uplifted [parts] of the ram of the completion-offering [**offering**] [which was used to install] Aharon and his sons.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:28)


וִיהֵי לְאַהֲרֹן וְלִבְנוֹהִי לִקְיָם עָלָם מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי אַפְרָשׁוּתָא הוּא וְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא יְהֵי מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִנִּכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיהוֹן אַפְרָשׁוּתְהוֹן **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


It [the breast and shoulder] shall be for Aharon and his sons’ by statute for ever. [This portion is taken] from the B’nei Yisrael for it is a terumah-offering. It shall be a terumah-offering from the B’nei Yisrael taken from their peace-offerings. It is their terumah-offering to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:29)


וּלְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא דִּי לְאַהֲרֹן יְהוֹן לִבְנוֹהִי בַּתְרוֹהִי לְדַכָּאָה בְהוֹן **וּלְקָרָבָא בְהוֹן יָת קֻרְבָּנְהוֹן**:


Aharon’s sacred garments shall be his son’s after him, to be uplifted through them and to be installed [**offer their sacrifices**] through them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:30)


שִׁבְעַת יוֹמִין יַלְבֵּשִׁנּוּן כַּהֲנָא תְּחוֹתוֹהִי מִבְּנוֹהִי דִּי יֵעוֹל לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בְקוּדְשָׁא:


Seven [consecutive] days shall they be worn by he who serves in his place—one of his sons—who will come into the Tent of Meeting to serve in the Holy [Sanctuary].


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:31)


וְיָת דְּכַר **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** תִּסָּב וּתְבַשֵּׁל יָת בִּסְרֵיהּ בַּאֲתַר קַדִּישׁ:


Take the [remainder of the] ram of the completion-offering [**offering**] and cook its flesh in a sacred place.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:32)


וְיֵכוּל אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי יָת בְּסַר דִּכְרָא וְיָת לַחְמָא דִּי בְסַלָּא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Aharon and his sons shall eat the ram’s meat, along with the bread that is in the basket, at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:33)


וְיֵיכְלוּן יָתְהוֹן דִּי יִתְכַּפַּר בְּהוֹן **לְקָרָבָא יָת קֻרְבָּנְהוֹן** לְקַדָּשָׁא יָתְהוֹן וְחִלּוֹנַי לָא יֵיכוּל אֲרֵי קוּדְשָׁא אִנּוּן:


They are to eat those things through which atonement was achieved, to install them [**offer their sacrifices**] and [thereby] to sanctify them. A stranger [a non-kohein] may not eat thereof for they are sacred.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:34)


וְאִם יִשְׁתָּאַר מִבְּסַר **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** וּמִן לַחְמָא עַד צַפְרָא וְתוֹקִיד יָת דְּאִשְׁתָּאַר בְּנוּרָא לָא יִתְאֲכֵל אֲרֵי קוּדְשָׁא הוּא:


If any meat of the installation-offering [**offering**] is left over or if any of the bread [is left over] until morning, you must burn the leftovers in fire. It must not be eaten for it is sacred.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:35)


וְתַעְבֵּד לְאַהֲרֹן וְלִבְנוֹהִי כְּדֵין כְּכֹל דִּי פַקֵּדִית יָתָךְ שִׁבְעַת יוֹמִין **תְּקָרֵב קֻרְבָּנְהוֹן**:


So shall you do for Aharon and his sons, according to all that I have commanded you. Seven days shall you install them [**offer their sacrifices**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:36)


וְתוֹרָא דְחַטָּאתָא תַּעְבֵּד לְיוֹמָא עַל כִּפּוּרַיָּא וּתְדַכֵּי עַל מַדְבְּחָא בְּכַפָּרוּתָךְ עֲלוֹהִי **וּתְרַבֵּי** יָתֵיהּ לְקַדָּשׁוּתֵיהּ:


Offer a bull for a sin-offering each day for atonement; and by sprinkling [its blood] on the altar you will purify [**elevate**] it [the altar.] You shall then anoint it to sanctify it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:37)


שִׁבְעַת יוֹמִין תְּכַפֵּר עַל מַדְבְּחָא וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ וִיהֵי מַדְבְּחָא קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָּא כָּל דְּיִקְרַב בְּמַדְבְּחָא יִתְקַדָּשׁ:


For seven days you shall make atonement for the altar and sanctify it and thus the altar will be Holy of Holies. Anything that touches the altar becomes sacred.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:38)


וְדֵין דִּי תַעְבֵּד עַל מַדְבְּחָא אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא תְּרֵין לְיוֹמָא תְּדִירָא:


And this is what you shall offer upon the altar: two yearling lambs each day, continually.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:39)


יָת אִמְּרָא חַד תַּעְבֵּד בְּצַפְרָא וְיָת אִמְרָא תִנְיָנָא תַּעְבֵּד בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא:


Offer one lamb in the morning and offer the second lamb in the afternoon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:40)


וְעִסְרוֹנָא סֻלְתָּא דְּפִילָא בִמְשַׁח כָּתִישָׁא רַבְעוּת הִינָא וְנִסְכָּא רַבְעוּת הִינָא חַמְרָא לְאִמְרָא חָד:


And a tenth [of an ephah] of fine flour mixed with one fourth of a hin of pressed olive oil, and a libation of a fourth of a hin of wine for the first lamb.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:41)


וְיָת אִמְרָא תִנְיָנָא תַּעְבֵּד בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא כְּמִנְחַת צַפְרָא וּכְנִסְכַּהּ תַּעְבֵּד לַהּ **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Offer the second lamb in the afternoon. As the meal-offering of the morning together with its libation, shall you offer it, for a pleasing fragrance [**to be accepted with favor**], a fire-offering to [**sacrifice before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:42)


עֲלָתָא תְדִירָא לְדָרֵיכוֹן בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא קֳדָם יְיָ **דִּאֱזַמֵּן מֵימְרִי** לְכוֹן תַּמָּן לְמַלָּלָא עִמָּךְ תַּמָּן:


This shall be a continual burnt-offering for your generations. [It shall be offered] at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, before Adonoy, where I will meet [**prepare My Word**] with you, to speak to you there.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:43)


וְאֱזַמֵּן מֵימְרִי תַמָּן לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ בִּיקָרִי:


It is there that I will meet [**prepare My Word**] with the B’nei Yisrael, and it [**I**] will be sanctified with My Glory.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:44)


וֶאֱקַדֵּשׁ יָת מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיָת מַדְבְּחָא וְיָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי אֱקַדֵּשׁ לְשַׁמָּשָׁא **קֳדָמַי**:


I will sanctify the Tent of Meeting and the altar, and Aharon and his sons I will sanctify to serve [**before**] Me as kohanim.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:45)


**וְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִנְתִּי** בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאֶהֱוֵי לְהוֹן לֶאֱלָהּ:


I will dwell [**rest My Shechina**] among the B’nei Yisrael, and I will be their God.


###### Onkelos Exodus 29:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/29:46)


וְיִדְּעוּן אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהֲהוֹן דִּי אַפֵּקִית יָתְהוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם **לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִנְתִּי** בֵּינֵיהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהֲהוֹן:


They will know that I, Adonoy, am their God who brought them out of the land of Egypt to dwell [**rest My Shechina**] among them. I am Adonoy, their God.



## Chapter 30

###### Onkelos Exodus 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:1)


וְתַעְבֵּד מַדְבְּחָא **לְאַקְטָרָא עֲלוֹהִי קְטֹרֶת** בּוּסְמַיָּא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין תַּעְבֵּד יָתֵיהּ:


You shall make an altar [**upon which**] to burn incense [**spices**]. Make it out of Acacia wood.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:2)


אַמְּתָא אֻרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְּתָא פְתָיֵהּ מְרַבַּע יְהֵי וְתַרְתֵּין אַמִּין רוּמֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ קַרְנוֹהִי:


It shall be one amoh long and one amoh wide, it shall be a square, and two amohs high. Its horn-like elevations shall be of one piece with it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:3)


וְתַחֲפֵי יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי יָת אִגָּרֵיהּ וְיָת כָּתְלוֹהִי סְחוֹר סְחוֹר וְיָת קַרְנוֹהִי וְתַעְבֵּד לֵיהּ דֵּיר דִּדְהַב סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


You shall overlay it with pure gold, on its top, its walls all around, and its horn-like elevations, and make a gold crown-like rim all around it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:4)


וְתַרְתֵּין עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב תַּעְבֵּד לֵיהּ מִלְּרַע לְדֵירֵיהּ עַל תַּרְתֵּין זִוְיָתֵיהּ תַּעְבֵּד עַל תְּרֵין סִטְרוֹהִי וִיהֵי לְאַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָּא לְמִטַּל יָתֵיהּ בְּהוֹן:


Make two gold rings for it under its crown-like rim, on two of its corners. Make them on its two opposite sides. They will house the poles with which it is carried.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:5)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וְתַחֲפֵי יָתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא:


Make the poles out of acacia wood and overlay them with gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:6)


וְתִתֵּן יָתֵיהּ קֳדָם פָּרֻכְתָּא דִּי עַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא לִקֳדָם כַּפֻּרְתָּא דִּי עַל סַהֲדוּתָא דִּי **אֲזַמֵּן מֵימְרִי** לָךְ תַּמָּן:


Place it in front of the paroches that is by the Ark of the Testimony, in front of the covering that is on the Testimony where I will meet [**prepare My Word**] with you there.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:7)


וְיַקְטַר עֲלוֹהִי אַהֲרֹן קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין בִּצְפַר בִּצְפַר בְּאַתְקָנוּתֵיהּ יָת בּוֹצִינַיָּא יַסֵּקִנַּהּ:


Aharon shall burn upon it incense of spices. Every morning when he cleans the lamps, he shall burn it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:8)


וּבְאַדְלָקוּת אַהֲרֹן יָת בּוֹצִינַיָּא בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא יַסֵּקִנַּהּ קְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמַיָּא** תְדִירָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְדָרֵיכוֹן:


And when Aharon lights the lamps in the afternoon, he shall burn it. It is a continual incense [**spice**]-offering before Adonoy for you generations.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:9)


לָא תַסְּקוּן עֲלוֹהִי קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין נוּכְרָאִין וַעֲלָתָא וּמִנְחָתָא וְנִסְכָּא לָא תְנַסְּכוּן עֲלוֹהִי:


Do not bring upon it strange [unauthorized] incense [**spices**]; neither burnt-offerings nor meal-offerings. You shall not pour a libation-offering upon it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:10)


וִיכַפֵּר אַהֲרֹן עַל קַרְנוֹהִי חֲדָא בְּשַׁתָּא מִדְּמָא חַטַּאת כִּפּוּרַיָּא חֲדָא בְשַׁתָּא יְכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי לְדָרֵיכוֹן קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הוּא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Aharon shall make atonement on its horn-like elevations once each year. With the blood of the sin-offering of atonement, he shall make atonement on it once each year for your generations. It is Holy of Holies to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:11)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying,


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:12)


אֲרֵי תְקַבֵּל יָת חֻשְׁבַּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִנְיָנֵיהוֹן וְיִתְּנוּן גְּבַר **פֻּרְקַן** נַפְשֵׁיהּ **קֳדָם** יְיָ כַּד תִּמְנֵי יָתְהוֹן וְלָא יְהֵי בְהוֹן מוֹתָא כַּד תִּמְנֵי יָתְהוֹן:


“When you take the count of the B’nei Yisrael to determine their numbers, each man shall give an atonement pledge [**a redemption**] for his soul to [**before**] Adonoy, when you count them. Thus there will be no plague among them when you count them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:13)


דֵּין יִתְּנוּן כָּל דְּעָבַר עַל מִנְיָנַיָּא פַּלְגּוּת **סִלְעָא** בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא עֶסְרִין **מָעִין סִלְעָא** פַּלְגּוּת **סִלְעָא** אַפְרָשׁוּתָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Everyone passing by to be counted must give this—half a shekel [**sela**] based on the shekel [**sela**] of the Holy [Sanctuary,] where a shekel [**sela**] is twenty geirah [**ma’in**]. Half of such a shekel [**sela**] must be given as a terumah-offering to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:14)


כֹּל דְּעָבַר עַל מִנְיָנַיָּא מִבַּר עֶסְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵלָּא יִתֵּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Everyone passing by to be counted, from [age] twenty years and older, must give a terumah-offering to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:15)


דְּעַתִּיר לָא יַסְגֵּי וּדְמִסְכֵּן לָא יַזְעֵר מִפַּלְגּוּת **סִלְעָא** לְמִתַּן יָת אַפְרָשׁוּתָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עַל נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן:


The rich shall not give more, and the poor shall not give less than half a shekel [**sela**], when giving this terumah-offering to [**before**] Adonoy to atone for your souls.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:16)


וְתִסַּב יָת כְּסַף כִּפּוּרַיָּא מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתִתֵּן יָתֵיהּ עַל פָּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וִיהֵי לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְדָכְרָנָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עַל נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן:


Take this atonement money from the B’nei Yisrael and donate it for the work of the Tent of Meeting. It will be a remembrance for the B’nei Yisrael before Adonoy to atone for your souls.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:17)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:18)


וְתַעְבֵּד כִּיּוֹרָא דִנְחָשָׁא וּבְסִיסֵיהּ נְחָשָׁא **לְקִדּוּשׁ** וְתִתֵּן יָתֵיהּ בֵּין מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּבֵין מַדְבְּחָא וְתִתֵּן תַּמָּן מַיָּא:


“Make a copper wash basin and its copper base for washing [**sanctification**]; and place it between the Tent of Meeting and the Altar, and put water in it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:19)


**וִיקַדְּשׁוּן** אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי **מִנֵּיהּ** יָת יְדֵיהוֹן וְיָת רַגְלֵיהוֹן:


Aharon and his sons will wash from it [**sanctify**] their hands and their feet [**from it**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:20)


בְּמֵיעַלְהוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא **יְקַדְּשׁוּן** מַיָּא וְלָא יְמוּתוּן אוֹ בְמִקְרָבְהוֹן לְמַדְבְּחָא לְשַׁמָּשָׁא לְאַסָּקָא **קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Before entering the Tent of Meeting they must wash [**sanctify**] with water and they will not die, or whenever they approach the Altar for Divine service, or to burn a fire-offering to [**sacrifice before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:21)


**וִיקַדְּשׁוּן** יְדֵיהוֹן וְרַגְלֵיהוֹן וְלָא יְמוּתוּן וּתְהֵי לְהוֹן קְיָם עָלָם לֵיהּ וְלִבְנוֹהִי לְדָרֵיהוֹן:


They must wash [**sanctify**] their hands and their feet and they will not die. This is a perpetual statute for them, for him [Aharon] and his descendants for all their generations.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:22)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:23)


וְאַתְּ סַב לָךְ בּוּסְמִין רֵישָׁא מֵירָא דַכְיָא **מַתְקַל** חֲמֵשׁ מְאָה וְקִנְּמָן בֶּשֶׂם פַּלְגּוּתֵיהּ **מַתְקַל** מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין וּקְנֵה בוּסְמָא **מַתְקַל** מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין:


“You shall take the finest spices, [**the weight of**] five hundred [shekels] of pure myrrh, half portions of fragrant cinnamon, [consisting of] [**the weight of**] two hundred and fifty [shekels] each, and [**the weight of**] two hundred and fifty [shekels] of calamus spices.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:24)


וּקְצִיעֲתָא **מַתְקַל** חֲמֵשׁ מְאָה **בְּסִלְעֵי** קוּדְשָׁא וּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא **מְלֵא** הִינָא:


[Also take] [**the weight of**] five hundred [shekels] of cassia based on the shekel [**sela**] value of the Holy [Sanctuary], and a [**full**] hin of olive oil.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:25)


וְתַעְבֵּד יָתֵיהּ מְשַׁח רְבוּת קוּדְשָׁא בּוֹסֶם מְבַסָּם עוֹבַד בּוּסְמָנוּ מְשַׁח רְבוּת קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵא:


Make it into sacred anointing oil, a blend made by a [perfume] blender. It shall be a sacred anointing oil.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:26)


וּתְרַבִּי בֵיהּ יָת מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיָת אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא:


Anoint with it the Tent of Meeting, and the Ark of the Testimony,


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:27)


וְיָת פָּתוֹרָא וְיָת כָּל מָנוֹהִי וְיָת מְנַרְתָּא וְיָת מָנָהָא וְיָת מַדְבְּחָא דִקְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמַיָּא**:


And the Table and all its vessels, and the Menorah and its vessels, and the [**Spice**] Incense Altar,


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:28)


וְיָת מַדְבַּח דַּעֲלָתָא וְיָת כָּל מָנוֹהִי וְיָת כִּיּוֹרָא וְיָת בְּסִיסֵיהּ:


And the Burnt-offering Altar and all its vessels, and the wash basin and its base.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:29)


וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתְהוֹן וִיהוֹן קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָּא כָּל דְּיִקְרַב בְּהוֹן יִתְקַדָּשׁ:


Sanctify them and they will become holy of holies. Anything that touches them will become holy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:30)


וְיָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי תְּרַבִּי וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתְהוֹן לְשַׁמָּשָׁא **קֳדָמַי**:


You shall also anoint Aharon and his sons, and sanctify them to serve [**before**] Me as kohanim.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/30:31)


וְעִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּמַלֵּל לְמֵימָר מְשַׁח רְבוּת קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵי דֵין **קֳדָמַי** לְדָרֵיכוֹן:


Speak to the B’nei Yisrael, saying: “This shall be sacred anointing oil to [**before**] Me for all your generations.


###### Onkelos Exodus 30:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:30)


וַחֲזָא אַהֲרֹן וְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת משֶׁה וְהָא **סְגֵי זִיו יְקָרָא** דְאַפּוֹהִי וּדְחִילוּ מִלְּאִתְקְרָבָא לְוָתֵיהּ:


When Aharon and all the B’nei Yisrael saw Moshe, and beheld that the skin [**splendor of the glory**] of his face had become radiant [**increased**], they were afraid to come close to him.


###### Onkelos Exodus 34:31

וּקְרָא לְהוֹן משֶׁה וְתָבוּ לְוָתֵיהּ אַהֲרֹן וְכָל רַבְרְבַיָּא בִּכְנִשְׁתָּא וּמַלֵּל משֶׁה עִמְּהוֹן:


Moshe called to them and they returned to him—Aharon and all the leaders of the congregation—and Moshe spoke to them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 34:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:32)

After that, all the B’nei Yisrael came close [to him] and he commanded them [regarding] all that Adonoy had spoken with him on Mount Sinai.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:33)


וְשֵׁיצֵי משֶׁה מִלְמַלָּלָא עִמְּהוֹן וִיהַב עַל אַפּוֹהִי **בֵּית אַפֵּי**:


When Moshe finished speaking with them, he placed a [**face**] cover[**ing**] over his face.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/34:34)


וְכַד עָלֵל משֶׁה לָקֳדָם יְיָ לְמַלָּלָא עִמֵּיהּ אַעְדִּי יָת **בֵּית אַפּוֹהִי** עַד מִפְּקֵיהּ וְנָפֵק וּמְמַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת דִי מִתְפַּקָּד:


[But] when Moshe came before Adonoy, to speak to Him; he removed the [**face**] cover[**ing**] until he was ready to leave. and he would then go out and speak to the B’nei Yisrael, [telling them] what he had been commanded.
