וְכָל אִתְּתָא חַכִּימַת לִבָּא בִּידָהָא מְעַזְּלָא וּמַיְתַן כַּד עָזֵל יָת תִּכְלָא וְיָת אַרְגְּוָנָא יָת **צְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וְיָת בּוּצָא:


And every wise-hearted woman spun with her hands; and they brought the spun yarn of greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool and fine linen.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:26)


וְכָל נְשַׁיָּא דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבְּהֶן עִמְּהֶן בְּחָכְמָא עַזְּלַן יָת מֵעַזְיָא:


And all the women whose hearts inspired them with wisdom, spun the goat’s hair.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:27)


וְרַבְרְבַיָּא אַיְתִיאוּ יָת אַבְנֵי בוּרְלָא וְיָת אַבְנֵי אַשְׁלָמוּתָא לְשַׁקָּעָא בְּאֵפוֹדָא וּבְחוּשְׁנָא:


And the leaders [of the tribes] brought onyx stones, and stones for setting into the ephod and breastplate.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:28)


וְיָת בּוּסְמָא וְיָת מִשְׁחָא לְאַנְהָרוּתָא וּלְמִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְלִקְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָּא:


And the spices, and the oil for the light, and the anointing oil, and the incense of aromatic spices.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:29)


כָּל גְּבַר וְאִתְּתָא דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבְּהוֹן עִמְּהוֹן לְאַיְתָאָה לְכָל עִבִדְתָּא דִּי פַקֵּד יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה אַיְתִיאוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל נְדַבְתָּא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Every man and woman whose generous heart inspired them to bring something for all the work that Adonoy had commanded to be done through Moshe [did so]. The B’nei Yisrael brought a free-will gift to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:30)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲזוֹ **דְּרַבִּי** יְיָ בְּשׁוּם בְּצַלְאֵל בַּר אוּרִי בַר חוּר לְשִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָה:


Moshe said to the B’nei Yisrael, “See, Adonoy has designated [**exalted**] by name, Betzalel, son of Uri, son of Chur, of the tribe of Yehudah.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:31)


וְאַשְׁלֵם עִמֵּיהּ **רוּחַ נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם יְיָ** בְּחָכְמְתָא בְּסוּכְלְתָנוּ וּבְמַדַּע וּבְכָל עִבִידָא:


And He endowed him with Divine [**the**] spirit [**of prophecy from before Adonoy**], with wisdom, with understanding, with knowledge and with [the skill to perform] all types of crafting.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:32)


**וּלְאַלָּפָא אוּמַנְוָן** לְמֶעְבַּד בְּדַהֲבָא וּבְכַסְפָּא וּבִנְחָשָׁא:


And to devise [plans] [**teach craftsmanship**] with ingenuity, to execute [those plans] in gold, in silver and in copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:33)


**וּבְאוּמָנוּת אֶבֶן טָבָא** לְאַשְׁלָמָא וּבְנַגָּרוּת אָעָא לְמֶעְבַּד בְּכָל עִבִידַת **אוּמַנְוָן**:


And in masonary [**to cut precious stones**] for settings, and in carpentry, to execute all kinds of clever tasks [**of craftsmanship**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:34)


וּלְאַלָּפָא יְהַב בְּלִבֵּיהּ הוּא וְאָהֳלִיאָב בַּר אֲחִיסָמָךְ לְשִׁבְטָא דְדָן:


And the ability to instruct he instilled in his heart, he (Betzalel) and Oholiov, the son of Achisomoch of the tribe of Dan.


###### Onkelos Exodus 35:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/35:35)


אַשְׁלֵם עִמְּהוֹן חַכִּימוּת לִבָּא לְמֶעְבַּד כָּל עִבִידַת **נַגָּר** וְאָמָּן וְצַיָּר בְּתִכְלָא וּבְאַרְגְּוָנָא **בִּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבְבוּצָא וּמַחֵי עָבְדֵי כָּל עִבִידָא **וּמַלְפֵי אוּמַנְוָן**:


He filled them with wisdom of the heart, to execute all the tasks of the engraver [**carpenter**], the craftsman, and the embroiderer of greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, fine linen, and the [the tasks of] weaver, [also] that of laborers and planners [**those who teach craftsmanship**].



## Chapter 36

###### Onkelos Exodus 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:1)


וַעֲבַד בְּצַלְאֵל וְאָהֳלִיאָב וְכָל גְּבַר חַכִּים לִבָּא דִּיהַב יְיָ חָכְמָא וְסוּכְלְתָנוּתָא בְּהוֹן לְמִדַּע לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל עִבִידַת פָּלְחַן קוּדְשָׁא לְכֹל דִּי פַּקֵּד יְיָ:


And they shall execute—Betzalel and Oholiov—and every wise-hearted man whom Adonoy had endowed with wisdom and insight to know (how) to execute in all of the holy work—all that Adonoy had commanded.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:2)


וּקְרָא משֶׁה לִבְצַלְאֵל וּלְאָהֳלִיאָב וּלְכָל גְּבַר חַכִּים לִבָּא דִּיהַב יְיָ חָכְמְתָא בְּלִבֵּיהּ כָּל דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ לְמִקְרַב לְעִבִדְתָּא לְמֶעְבַּד יָתֵיהּ:


Moshe called to Betzalel and to Oholiov, and to every wise-hearted man whom Adonoy had endowed with wisdom in his heart, everyone whose heart moved him to approach the task—to execute it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:3)


וּנְסִיבוּ מִן קֳדָם משֶׁה יָת כָּל אַפְרָשׁוּתָא דִּי אַיְתִיאוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְעִבִידַת פָּלְחַן קוּדְשָׁא לְמֶעְבַּד יָתַהּ וְאִנּוּן מַיְתַן לֵיהּ עוֹד נְדַבְתָּא בִּצְפַר בִּצְפָר:


And they took from before Moshe, all the terumah-offering which the B’nei Yisrael brought for the tasks of the holy work, to execute it. And they continued to bring gifts each and every morning.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:4)


וַאֲתוֹ כָּל חַכִּימַיָּא דְּעָבְדִין יָת כָּל עִבִידַת קוּדְשָׁא גְּבַר גְּבַר מֵעִבִדְתֵּיהּ דְּאִנּוּן עָבְדִין:


All the wise men came—who were executing all the holy tasks—each and every man from the task in which he was engaged.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:5)


וַאֲמָרוּ לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימַר מַסְגַּן עַמָּא לְאַיְתָאָה מִסַּת פָּלְחָנָא לְעִבִדְתָּא דְּפַקֵּד יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד יָתַהּ:


They said the following to Moshe: the people are bringing too much—more than is needed for the tasks which Adonoy has commanded to execute.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:6)


וּפַקֵּד משֶׁה וְאַעֲבַרוּ כָרוֹזָא בְמַשְׁרִיתָא לְמֵימַר גְּבַר וְאִתְּתָא לָא יַעַבְדוּן עוֹד עִבִדְתָּא לְאַפְרָשׁוּת קוּדְשָׁא וּפְסַק עַמָּא מִלְּאַיְתָאָה:


Moshe commanded—and they proclaimed throughout the encampment saying, “Let no man or woman bring any more material for the sacred offering.” And the people stopped bringing.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:7)


וְעִבִדְתָּא הֲוַת מִסַּת לְכָל עִבִדְתָּא לְמֶעְבַּד יָתַהּ וִיתָרָת:


The material was enough for all the work that had to be done, and some was left over.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:8)


וַעֲבָדוּ כָל חַכִּימֵי לִבָּא בְּעָבְדֵי עִבִדְתָּא יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא עֲשַׂר יְרִיעָן דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי צוּרַת כְּרוּבִין עוֹבַד אָמָּן עֲבַד יָתְהוֹן:


All craftsmen with the spirit of wisdom made the mishkon out of ten drapes, (consisting of) twined fine linen, greenish blue wool, dark red wool and crimson [**colored**] wool, figures of Cherubim, the work of a craftsman.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:9)


אֻרְכָּא דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עַסְרִין וּתְמַנְיָא אַמִּין וּפוּתְיָא אַרְבַּע אַמִּין דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מְשַׁחְתָא חֲדָא לְכָל יְרִיעָתָא:


The length of each drape was twenty eight amohs, and each drape was four amohs wide. All drapes had the same measure.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:10)


וְלָפֵף יָת חָמֵשׁ יְרִיעָן חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא וְחָמֵשׁ יְרִיעָן לָפֵף חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא:


He joined (sewed) five of these drapes together, and the other five drapes he joined (sewed) together.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:11)


וַעֲבַד עֲנוּבִין דְּתִכְלָא עַל סִפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מִסִּטְרָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי כֵּן עֲבַד בְּסִפְתָא דִּירִיעֲתָא בְּסִטְרָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיֵתָא:


He made loops of greenish blue wool on the edge of the drape which was at the (innermost) end of the (first) group. He did the same on the edge of the drape which was the innermost end of the second group.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:12)


חַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עֲבַד בִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עֲבַד בְּסִטְרָא דִירִיעֲתָא דְבֵית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיֵתָא מְכַוְּנָן עֲנוּבַיָּא חֲדָא לָקֳבֵל חֲדָא:


He made fifty loops on (the edge of) one drape and he made fifty loops on the edge of the drape which was on the second group. The loops were opposite one another.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:13)


וַעֲבַד חַמְשִׁין פּוּרְפִין דִּדְהָב וְלָפֵף יָת יְרִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא בְּפוּרְפַיָּא וַהֲוָה מַשְׁכְּנָא חָד:


He made fifty golden clasps and he joined the drapes (of each group) together with the clasps. The mishkon was thus one unit.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:14)


וַעֲבַד יְרִיעָן דִּמְעַזֵּי לִפְרָסָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא חַד עֲשַׂר יְרִיעָן עֲבַד יָתְהֶן:


He made drapes of goats’ hair for a covering over the mishkon. He made eleven such drapes.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:15)


אֻרְכָּא דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא תְּלָתִין בְּאַמִּין וְאַרְבַּע אַמִּין פּוּתְיָא דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מְשַׁחְתָּא חֲדָא לְחַד עֲשַׂר יְרִיעָן:


The length of each drape was thirty amohs, and the width of each drape was four amohs. The eleven drapes were of the same measure.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:16)


וְלָפֵף יָת חֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעָן לְחוֹד וְיָת שֵׁת יְרִיעָן לְחוֹד:


And he joined together five drapes by themselves, and six drapes by themselves.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:17)


וַעֲבַד עֲנוּבִין חַמְשִׁין עַל סִפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא בְּסִטְרָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עֲבַד עַל סִפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא דְבֵית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיֵתָא:


He made fifty loops on the edge of one drape which was the innermost end of the (first) group and he made fifty loops on the edge of the drape which was on the second group.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:18)


וַעֲבַד פּוּרְפִין דִּנְחָשָׁא חַמְשִׁין לְלַפָּפָא יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא לְמֵהֱוֵי חָד:


He made fifty copper clasps to join the tent together making it one.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:19)


וַעֲבַד חוֹפָאָה לְמַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכֵי דְדִכְרֵי מְסַמְּקֵי וְחוֹפָאָה מַשְׁכֵי **דְסַסְגּוֹנָא** מִלְּעֵלָּא:


He made a covering for the tent out of red dyed rams’ skins, and a covering of tachash [**multicolored**] skins above that.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:20)


וַעֲבַד יָת דַּפַּיָּא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין קָיְמִין:


He made planks for the mishkon out of acacia wood; (these planks) stood upright (vertically).


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:21)


עֲשַׂר אַמִּין אֻרְכָּא דְדַפָּא וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגּוּת אַמְּתָא פּוּתְיָא דְּדַפָּא חָד:


Each plank was ten amohs long, and one and one half amohs was the width of each plank.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:22)


תַּרְתֵּין צִירִין לְדַפָּא חַד מְשַׁלְּבִין חֲדָא לָקֳבֵל חֲדָא כֵּן עֲבַד לְכֹל דַּפֵּי מַשְׁכְּנָא:


Each plank had two (square) pegs, exactly parallel to each other. In this manner he made all the planks of the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:23)


וַעֲבַד יָת דַּפַּיָּא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא עֶסְרִין דַּפִּין לְרוּחַ עֵבַר דָּרוֹמָא:


He made these planks for the mishkon, twenty planks for the southern side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:24)


וְאַרְבְּעִין סַמְכִין דִּכְסַף עֲבַד תְּחוֹת עֶסְרִין דַּפִּין תְּרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַפָּא חַד לִתְרֵין צִירוֹהִי וּתְרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חַד לִתְרֵין צִירוֹהִי:


He made forty silver sockets, (and placed them) under the twenty planks. There were two sockets under one plank (one) for each of the two pegs, and two sockets under the other plank, (one) for each of the two pegs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:25)


וְלִסְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא תִּנְיֵתָא לְרוּחַ צִפּוּנָא עֲבַד עֶסְרִין דַּפִּין:


For the second wall of the mishkon, to the northern side, he made twenty planks.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:26)


וְאַרְבְּעִין סַמְכֵיהוֹן דִּכְסָף תְּרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חַד וּתְרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חָד:


(He made) their forty silver sockets, two sockets under each plank, two sockets under each plank.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:27)


וְלִסְיָפֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא מַעַרְבָא עֲבַד שִׁתָּא דַפִּין:


For the western end of the mishkon he made six planks.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:28)


וּתְרֵין דַּפִּין עֲבַד לְזָוְיַת מַשְׁכְּנָא **בְּסוֹפֵיהוֹן**:


He made two planks for the corners of the mishkon at the rear side [**their ends**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:29)


וַהֲווֹ מְכַוְּנִין מִלְּרַע וְכַחֲדָא הֲווֹ מְכַוְּנִין עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ לְעִזְקָתָא חֲדָא כֵּן עֲבַד לְתַרְוֵיהוֹן לִתְרֵין זָוְיָן:


They were coupled together on the bottom, and they were coupled together evenly on the top with one (square) ring. In this manner he made the two (planks) which were at the two corners.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:30)


וַהֲווֹ תְּמַנְיָא דַפִּין וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן דִּכְסַף שִׁתָּא עֲשַׂר סַמְכִין תְּרֵין סַמְכִין תְּרֵין סַמְכִין תְּחוֹת דַּפָּא חָד:


There were eight planks, and their silver sockets, totaling sixteen sockets, two sockets, two sockets under each plank.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:31)


וַעֲבַד עַבְּרֵי דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין חַמְשָׁא לְדַפֵּי סְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא חָד:


He made bars out of acacia wood, five for the planks of one wall,


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:32)


וְחַמְשָׁא עַבְּרִין לְדַפֵּי סְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא תִּנְיֵתָא וְחַמְשָׁא עַבְּרִין לְדַפֵּי מַשְׁכְּנָא לְסוֹפֵיהוֹן מַעַרְבָא:


and five bars for the planks of the second wall of the mishkon, and five bars for the planks of the western (back) wall of the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:33)


וַעֲבַד יָת עַבְּרָא מְצִיעָאָה לְאַעְבָּרָא בְּגוֹ דַפַּיָּא מִן סְיָפֵי לִסְיָפֵי:


He made the middle (center) bar to go through the planks, [reinforcing the planks] from one end to the other.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:34)


וְיָת דַּפַּיָּא חֲפָא דַהֲבָא וְיָת עִזְקָתְהוֹן עֲבַד דַּהֲבָא אַתְרָא לְעַבְּרַיָּא וַחֲפָא יָת עַבְּרַיָּא דַּהֲבָא:


He overlaid the planks with gold, and made their rings out of gold to hold the bars. He (also) overlaid the bars with gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:35)


וַעֲבַד יָת פָּרֻכְתָּא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד אָמָּן עֲבַד יָתַהּ צוּרַת כְּרוּבִין:


He made the paroches (partition) out of greenish blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and fine, twined linen, the work of a craftsman, with figures of Cherubim (woven into it).


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:36)


וַעֲבַד לֵיהּ אַרְבְּעָא עַמּוּדֵי שִׁטִּין וַחֲפָנוּן דַּהֲבָא וָוֵיהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וְאַתִּיךְ לְהוֹן אַרְבְּעָא סַמְכִין דִּכְסָף:


He made for it (the paroches) four acacia-wood pillars and he overlaid them with gold. Their hooks were also of gold. He cast for them four silver sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:37)


וַעֲבַד פְּרָסָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד צַיָּר:


He made a screen for the entrance of the tent, of greenish blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and fine twined linen. It was the work of an embroiderer.


###### Onkelos Exodus 36:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/36:38)


וְיָת עַמּוּדוֹהִי חַמְשָׁא וְיָת וָוֵיהוֹן וַחֲפָא רֵישֵׁיהוֹן וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן חַמְשָׁא נְחָשָׁא:


(He made) five pillars (to hold the screen), along with their hooks and he overlaid their caps and bands with gold. Their five sockets (he made of) copper.



## Chapter 37

###### Onkelos Exodus 37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:1)


וַעֲבַד בְּצַלְאֵל יָת אֲרוֹנָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין וּפַלְגָּא אֻרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָא פְתָיֵהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָּא רוּמֵיהּ:


Betzalel made the ark of acacia wood, two and one-half amohs long, one and one-half amohs wide, and one and one-half amohs high.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:2)


וַחֲפָהִי דְּהַב דְּכֵי מִגָּו וּמִבָּרָא וַעֲבַד לֵיהּ דֵּיר דִּדְהַב סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He covered it with pure gold from inside and out. He made for it a golden crown-like rim around (its top).


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:3)


וְאַתִּיךְ לֵיהּ אַרְבַּע עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָתֵיהּ וְתַרְתֵּין עִזְקַן עַל סִטְרֵיהּ חַד וְתַרְתֵּין עִזְקַן עַל סִטְרֵיהּ תִּנְיֵתָא:


He cast four gold rings on its (the ark’s) four corners, two rings on one side and two rings on the other side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:4)


וַעֲבַד אֲרִיחֵי דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וַחֲפָא יָתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא:


He made two poles of acacia wood, and overlaid them with gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:5)


וְאָעֵל יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא בְּעִזְקָתָא עַל סִטְרֵי אֲרוֹנָא לְמִטַּל יָת אֲרוֹנָא:


He placed the poles in the rings on the sides of the ark, so that the ark can be carried with them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:6)


וַעֲבַד כַּפֻּרְתָּא דְּהַב דְּכֵי תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין וּפַלְגָּא אֻרְכַּהּ וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָּא פְּתָיַהּ:


He made a cover (for the ark) of pure gold, two and one-half amohs long, and one and one-half amohs wide.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:7)


וַעֲבַד תְּרֵין כְּרוּבִין דִּדְהָב נְגִיד עֲבַד יָתְהוֹן מִתְּרֵין סִטְרֵי כַפֻּרְתָּא:


He made two golden Cherubim. He hammered them out of the two ends of the (ark) cover.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:8)


כְּרוּבָא חַד **מִסִּטְרָא** מִכָּא וּכְרוּבָא חַד **מִסִּטְרָא** מִכָּא מִן כַּפֻּרְתָּא עֲבַד יָת כְּרוּבַיָּא מִתְּרֵין **סִטְרוֹהִי**:


One Cherub out of one end [**side**], and one Cherub out of the other end [**side**]. From the (same piece of gold as the) cover itself, he made the Cherubim on its two ends [**sides**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:9)


וַהֲווֹ כְרוּבַיָּא פְּרִיסָן גַּדְפֵיהוֹן לְעֵלָּא נָטְלִין בְּגַדְפֵיהוֹן עַל כַּפֻּרְתָּא וְאַפֵּיהוֹן חַד לָקֳבֵל חַד לָקֳבֵל כַּפֻּרְתָּא הֲווֹ אַפֵּי כְרוּבַיָּא:


The Cherubim had their wings spread upward, sheltering the cover with their wings, and they faced one another, the Cherubim’s faces were (inclined) toward the cover.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:10)


וַעֲבַד יָת פָּתוֹרָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין אֻרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְּתָא פְתָיֵהּ וְאַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָּא רוּמֵיהּ:


He made a table out of acacia wood, two amohs long, one amoh wide, and one and one-half amohs high.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:11)


וַחֲפָא יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי וַעֲבַד לֵיהּ דֵּיר דִּדְהַב סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He overlaid it with pure gold, and he made a crown-like rim around it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:12)


וַעֲבַד לֵיהּ גְּדַנְפָא **רוּמֵיהּ** פּוּשְׁכָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר וַעֲבַד דֵּיר דִּדְהַב לִגְדַנְפֵיהּ סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He made a ledge of a tefach [**high**] around (the table), and he made a gold crown-like rim around the ledge.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:13)


וְאַתִּיךְ לֵיהּ אַרְבַּע עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב וִיהַב יָת עִזְקָתָא עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָתָא דִּי לְאַרְבַּע רַגְלוֹהִי:


He cast for it (the table) four gold rings, and placed the rings on the four corners that are on its four legs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:14)


לָקֳבֵל גְּדַנְפָא הֲווֹ עִזְקָתָא אַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָּא לְמִטַּל יָת פָּתוֹרָא:


The rings were against the ledge as houses (receptacles) for the poles with which to carry the table.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:15)


וַעֲבַד יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וַחֲפָא יָתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא לְמִטַּל יָת פָּתוֹרָא:


He made the poles out of acacia wood, and overlaid them with gold with which to carry the table.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:16)


וַעֲבַד יָת מָנַיָּא דִּי עַל פָּתוֹרָא יָת מָגִיסוֹהִי וְיָת בָּזִיכוֹהִי וְיָת מְכִילָתֵיהּ וְיָת קַשְׂוָתָא דִּי יִתְנַסַּךְ בְּהוֹן דְּהַב דְּכֵי:


He made the vessels that were on the table, its dishes (bread shapes), its spoons (incense bowls), its supports, and its half-hollow tubes by which they (the breads) are covered, out of pure gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:17)


וַעֲבַד יָת מְנַרְתָּא דְּהַב דְּכֵי נְגִיד עֲבַד יָת מְנַרְתָּא שִׁידַהּ וּקְנַהּ כַּלִידָהָא חֵזוּרָהָא וְשׁוֹשַׁנָּהָא מִנַּהּ הֲווֹ:


He made the menorah (out) of pure gold. The menorah was made by hammering (its form out of a solid piece of gold). Its base, its shaft, its cups, its knobs, and its flowers were of it (the same piece of gold).


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:18)


וְשִׁתָּא קְנִין נָפְקִין מִסִּטְרָהָא תְּלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִסִּטְרַהּ חַד וּתְלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִסִּטְרַהּ תִּנְיָנָא:


Six branches extended from its sides, three branches of the menorah from one of its sides, and three branches of the menorah from the second side.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:19)


תְּלָתָא כַלִּידִין מְצַיְּרִין בְּקַנְיָא חַד חֵזוּר וְשׁוֹשַׁן וּתְלָתָא כַלִּידִין מְצַיְּרִין בְּקַנְיָא חַד חֵזוּר וְשׁוֹשָׁן כֵּן לְשִׁתָּא קְנִין דְּנָפְקִין מִן מְנַרְתָּא:


Three cups were engraved in almond-shaped patterns, on one branch with a knob and a flower, and three cups were engraved in almond-shaped patterns, on one branch with a knob and a flower. So was (the form) of each of the six branches that extended from (the shaft) of the menorah.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:20)


וּבִמְנַרְתָּא אַרְבְּעָא כַלִּידִין מְצַיְּרִין חֵזּוּרָהָא וְשׁוֹשַׁנָּהָא:


The (shaft of the) menorah had four cups, engraved in almond shaped patterns, with their knobs and their flowers.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:21)


וְחֵזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין מִנַּהּ וְחֵזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין מִנַּהּ וְחֵזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין מִנַּהּ לְשִׁתָּא קְנִין דְּנָפְקִין מִנַּהּ:


There was a knob under the two branches extending from it, and a (second) knob under the (second) two branches extending from it, and a (third) knob under the (third) two branches extending from it. (This held true) for the six branches that extended from it (the menorah’s shaft).


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:22)


חֵזוּרֵיהוֹן וּקְנֵיהוֹן מִנַּהּ הֲווֹ כֻּלַהּ נְגִידָא חֲדָא דְּהַב דְּכֵי:


Their knobs and their branches were made out of (the menorah) itself. It was all made of a single hammered work of pure gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:23)


וַעֲבַד יָת בּוֹצִינָהָא שִׁבְעָא וְצִבְיְתָהָא וּמַחִתְּיְתָהָא דְּהַב דְּכֵי:


He made seven lamps (for the menorah), and its tongs and its (ash) scoops, of pure gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:24)


כִּכְּרָא דְּהַב דְּכֵי עֲבַד יָתַהּ וְיָת כָּל מָנָהָא:


He made it out of a kikar of pure gold, together with all its vessels.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:25)


וַעֲבַד יָת מַדְבְּחָא דִקְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמַיָּא** דְאָעֵי שִׁטִּין אַמְּתָא אֻרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְּתָא פְתָיֵהּ מְרַבַּע וְתַרְתֵּין אַמִּין רוּמֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ הֲווֹ קַרְנוֹהִי:


He made the [**spice**] incense altar out of acacia wood, one amoh long and one amoh wide, it was square, and two amohs high. Its horn-like elevations were of one piece with it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:26)


וַחֲפָא יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי יָת אִגָּרֵיהּ וְיָת כָּתְלוֹהִי סְחוֹר סְחוֹר וְיָת קַרְנוֹהִי וַעֲבַד לֵיהּ דֵּיר דִּדְהַב סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He overlaid it with pure gold, on its top, its walls all around, and its horn-like elevations. He made a gold crown-like rim all around it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:27)


וְתַרְתֵּין עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב עֲבַד לֵיהּ מִלְּרַע לְדֵירֵיהּ עַל תַּרְתֵּין זִוְיָתֵיהּ עַל תְּרֵין סִטְרוֹהִי לְאַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָּא לְמִטַּל יָתֵיהּ בְּהוֹן:


He made two gold rings for it under its crown-like rim on two of its corners, on its two opposite sides, to house the poles with which to carry it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:28)


וַעֲבַד יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וַחֲפָא יָתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא:


He made the poles out of acacia wood and overlaid them with gold.


###### Onkelos Exodus 37:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/37:29)


וַעֲבַד יָת מִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא קוּדְשָׁא וְיָת קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָּא דְּכֵי עוֹבַד בּוּסְמָנוּ:


He made the sacred anointing oil and the pure perfumed incense, according to the work of the perfumer.



## Chapter 38

###### Onkelos Exodus 38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:1)


וַעֲבַד יָת מַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין חֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין אֻרְכֵּיהּ וַחֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין פְּתָיֵהּ מְרַבַּע וּתְלָתָא אַמִּין רוּמֵיהּ:


He made the burnt-offering altar out of acacia wood. It was five amohs long and five amohs wide, it was square, and three amohs high.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:2)


וַעֲבַד קַרְנוֹהִי עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָתֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ הֲווֹ קַרְנוֹהִי וַחֲפָא יָתֵיהּ נְחָשָׁא:


He made its raised projections on its four corners. Its raised projections were one piece with it, and he overlaid it with copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:3)


וַעֲבַד יָת כָּל מָנֵי מַדְבְּחָא יָת פְּסַכְתֵּרָוָתָא וְיָת מַגְרוֹפְיָתָא וְיָת מִזְרְקָתָא יָת צִנּוּרְיָתָא וְיָת מַחְתְּיָתָא כָּל מָנוֹהִי עֲבַד נְחָשָׁא:


He made all the vessels of the altar, its pots, its scoops, its basins, its flesh-hooks, and its fire pans. He made all of its vessels out of copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:4)


וַעֲבַד לְמַדְבְּחָא סְרָדָא עוֹבַד מְצַדְתָּא דִנְחָשָׁא תְּחוֹת סוֹבָבֵיהּ מִלְּרַע עַד פַּלְגֵּיהּ:


He made for the altar a grate of copper netting, under its rim extending downward, (so that the grate reached) to the middle of the altar.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:5)


וְאַתִּיךְ אַרְבַּע עִזְקָתָא בְּאַרְבַּע **סִטְרָתָא** לִסְרָדָא דִנְחָשָׁא אַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָּא:


He cast four rings on the four corners [**sides**]of the copper grating to house the poles.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:6)


וַעֲבַד יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא דְּאָעֵי שִׁטִּין וַחֲפָא יָתְהוֹן נְחָשָׁא:


He made the poles of acacia wood, and overlaid them with copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:7)


וְאָעֵיל יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא בְּעִזְקָתָא עַל סִטְרֵי מַדְבְּחָא לְמִטַּל יָתֵיהּ בְּהוֹן חֲלִיל לוּחִין עֲבַד יָתֵיהּ:


He placed the poles into the rings that were on the (two) sides of the altar to carry it with them. He made it a hollow structure constructed from boards.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:8)


וַעֲבַד יָת כִּיוֹרָא נְחָשָׁא וְיָת בְּסִיסֵיהּ נְחָשָׁא בְּמֶחְזְיַן נְשַׁיָּא **דְּאָתְיָן לְצַלָּאָה** בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


He made the basin out of copper and its base out of copper, from the mirrors of the women who had gathered [**come to pray**] at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:9)


וַעֲבַד יָת דַּרְתָּא לְרוּחַ עֵבַר דָּרוֹמָא סְרָדֵי דַרְתָּא דְבוּץ שְׁזִיר מְאָה בְאַמִּין:


He made the enclosure (for the mishkon). On the south side the curtains for the enclosure were made of fine, twined linen, one hundred amohs long.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:10)


עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עֶסְרִין וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן עֶסְרִין נְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָּא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן כְּסָף:


It had twenty pillars and twenty copper sockets. The hooks and bands of the pillars were of silver.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:11)


וּלְרוּחַ צִפּוּנָא מְאָה אַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עֶסְרִין וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן עֶסְרִין נְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָּא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף:


On the north side, (its length was) one hundred amohs. It had twenty pillars and twenty copper sockets. The hooks and bands of the pillars were of silver.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:12)


וּלְרוּחַ מַעַרְבָא סְרָדִין חַמְשִׁין בְּאַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עַסְרָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן עַסְרָא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָּא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף:


On the west side, the curtains were of fifty amohs. It had ten pillars and ten sockets. The hooks and bands of the pillars were of silver.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:13)


וּלְרוּחַ קִדּוּמָא מָדִינְחָא חַמְשִׁין אַמִּין:


(The width of the enclosure) on the east side was fifty amohs.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:14)


סְרָדִין חֲמֵשׁ עֶסְרֵי אַמִּין לְעִבְרָא עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא:


Curtains fifteen amohs long were on one flank (of the entrance). They had three pillars and three sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:15)


וּלְעִבְרָא תִנְיֵתָא מִכָּא וּמִכָּא לִתְרַע דַּרְתָּא סְרָדִין חֲמֵשׁ עֶסְרֵי אַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא


The other flank, on either side of the gate to the enclosure (there also were) curtains fifteen amohs long. They (too) had three pillars and three sockets.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:16)


כָּל סְרָדֵי דְדַרְתָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר:


All curtains around the enclosure were made of twined, fine linen.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:17)


וְסַמְכַיָּא לְעַמּוּדַיָּא נְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָּא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסַף וְחִפּוּי רֵישֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף וְאִנּוּן מְכַבְּשִׁין דִּכְסַף כָּל עַמּוּדֵי דַרְתָּא:


The sockets for the pillars were of copper, the hooks and bands of the pillars were of silver, and the covering of their tops was of silver. All pillars of the enclosure had silver bands.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:18)


וּפְרָסָא דִּתְרַע דַּרְתָּא עוֹבַד צַיָּר תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְעֶסְרִין אַמִּין אֻרְכָּא וְרוּמָא בְּפוּתְיָא חֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין לָקֳבֵל סְרָדֵי דַרְתָּא:


The screen at the entrance of the enclosure was the work of an embroiderer. It was made of greenish blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and twined, fine linen. It was twenty amohs long and the height in the width was five amohs, corresponding to the curtains of the enclosure.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:19)


וְעַמּוּדֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעָא וְסַמְכֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעָא נְחָשָׁא וָוֵיהוֹן דִּכְסַף וְחִפּוּי רֵישֵׁיהוֹן וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף:


(The screen) had four pillars and four copper sockets, silver hooks, and their tops and bands were overlaid with silver.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:20)


וְכָל סִכַּיָּא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא וּלְדַרְתָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר דִּנְחָשָׁא:


All the stakes of the mishkon and the surrounding enclosure were made of copper.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:21)


אִלֵּין מִנְיָנֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא דִּי אִתְמְנֵי עַל מֵימְרָא דְמשֶׁה פָּלְחַן לֵיוָאֵי בִּידָא דְּאִיתָמָר בַּר אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא:


These are the accounts of the mishkon, the mishkon of Testimony, that were drawn up by Moshe’s order; for the work of the Levites under the direction of Isamar, son of Aharon the kohein.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:22)


וּבְצַלְאֵל בַּר אוּרִי בַר חוּר לְשִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָה עֲבַד יָת כָּל דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


Betzalel, son of Uri, son of Chur, of the tribe of Yehudah, made all that Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:23)


וְעִמֵּיהּ אָהֳלִיאָב בַּר אֲחִיסָמָךְ לְשִׁבְטָא דְדָן **נַגָּר** וְאֳמָּן וְצַיָּר בְּתִכְלָא וּבְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּבִצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבְבוּצָא:


With him was Oholiav, son of Achisamach, of the tribe of Dan, sculptor [**carpenter**] and craftsman, and embroiderer in greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and fine linen.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:24)


כָּל דַּהֲבָא דְאִתְעֲבֵד לְעִבִידָא בְּכֹל עִבִידַת קוּדְשָׁא וַהֲוָה דְּהַב אֲרָמוּתָא עֶסְרִין וּתְשַׁע כִּכְּרִין וּשְׁבַע מְאָה וּתְלָתִין **סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי** קוּדְשָׁא:


All the gold that was used for the work in all the work of the Sanctuary. The gold donated as a wave offering amounted to twenty-nine kikar and seven hundred and thirty shekel [**selaim**], (measured) by the [**selaim of the**] Sanctuary standard.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:25)


וּכְסַף מִנְיָנֵי כְנִשְׁתָּא מְאָה כִכְּרִין וְאֶלֶף וּשְׁבַע מְאָה וְשִׁבְעִין וְחַמְשָׁא **סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי** קוּדְשָׁא:


The silver (collected) by the community census (amounted to) one hundred kikar, and one thousand seven hundred and seventy-five shekel [**selaim**], (measured) by the [**selaim of the**] Sanctuary standard,


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:26)


תִּקְלָא לְגֻלְגַלְתָּא פַּלְגּוּת **סִלְעָא בְּסִלְעֵי** קוּדְשָׁא לְכֹל דְּעָבַר עַל מִנְיָנַיָּא מִבַּר עֶסְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵלָּא לְשֵׁית מְאָה וּתְלָתָא אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה וְחַמְשִׁין:


one beka per person, (the equivalent of) a half-shekel[**sela**], (measured) by the [**selaim of the**] Sanctuary standard, from each one included in the census, from the age of twenty years and up, for six hundred and three thousand five hundred and fifty (men).


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:27)


וַהֲוָה מְאָה כִּכְּרִין דִּכְסַף לְאַתָּכָא יָת סַמְכֵי קוּדְשָׁא וְיָת סַמְכֵי דְפָרֻכְתָּא מְאָה סַמְכִין לִמְאָה כִכְּרִין כִּכְּרָא לְסַמְכָא:


One hundred kikar of this silver was (used) to cast the sockets for the mishkon and the sockets for the paroches (partition). One hundred sockets (were cast) from one hundred kikar, one kikar for each socket.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:28)


וְיָת אַלְפָא וּשְׁבַע מְאָה וְשִׁבְעִין וְחַמְשָׁא עֲבַד וָוִין לְעַמּוּדַיָּא וְחִפָּא רֵישֵׁיהוֹן וְכַבֵּשׁ יָתְהוֹן:


Of the (remaining) one thousand seven hundred and seventy-five (shekel) he made hooks for the pillars, overlaid their caps and made bands for them.


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:29)


וּנְחָשָׁא דַאֲרָמוּתָא שִׁבְעִין כִּכְּרִין וּתְרֵין אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה **סִלְעִין**:


The copper (donated as) a wave offering (amounted to) seventy kikar, and two thousand four hundred shekel [**selaim**],


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:30)


וַעֲבַד בַּהּ יָת סַמְכֵי תְּרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיָת מַדְבְּחָא דִנְחָשָׁא וְיָת סְרָדָא דִנְחָשָׁא דִּי לֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל מָנֵי מַדְבְּחָא:


from which he made sockets for the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, the copper altar with its copper grate, all the altar’s vessels,


###### Onkelos Exodus 38:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/38:31)


וְיָת סַמְכֵי דְדַרְתָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר וְיָת סַמְכֵי תְּרַע דַּרְתָּא וְיָת כָּל סִכֵּי מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיָת כָּל סִכֵּי דַרְתָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


the sockets around the enclosure, the sockets for the entrance of the enclosure, all the stakes for the mishkon, and all the stakes around the enclosure.



## Chapter 39

###### Onkelos Exodus 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:1)


וּמִן תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרִי עֲבָדוּ לְבוּשֵׁי שִׁמּוּשָׁא לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בְקוּדְשָׁא וַעֲבָדוּ יָת לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא דִּי לְאַהֲרֹן כְּמָא דִּי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


From the greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, and crimson [**colored**] wool, they made garments of distinction [**service**] for service in the Sanctuary, and they made sacred garments for Aharon, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:2)


וַעֲבַד יָת אֵפוֹדָא דַּהֲבָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר:


He made the eiphod out of gold, greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and twisted, fine linen.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:3)


וְרַדִּידוּ יָת טַסֵּי דַהֲבָא וְקַצִּיצוּ חוּטִין לְמֶעְבַּד בְּגוֹ תִכְלָא וּבְגוֹ אַרְגְּוָנָא וּבְגוֹ **צְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבְגוֹ בוּצָא עוֹבַד אָמָּן:


They beat the plates of gold and cut (them) into threads to work them into the greenish-blue wool, into the dark red wool, into the crimson [**colored**] wool, and into the fine linen, the work of a craftsman.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:4)


כַּתְפָּן עֲבָדוּ לֵיהּ מְלַפְּפָן עַל תְּרֵין **סִטְרוֹהִי** יִתְלָפֵף:


They made shoulder straps attached to it, attached at its two corners [**sides**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:5)


וְהֶמְיַן **תִּקּוּנֵיהּ** דִּי עֲלוֹהִי מִנֵּיהּ הוּא כְּעוֹבָדוֹהִי דַּהֲבָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


The eiphod’s belt [**that enhances it**] which was on it, was woven with it in the same manner (as the eiphod) (with) gold, greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool, and twined, fine linen. just as Adonoy commanded.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:6)


וַעֲבָדוּ יָת אַבְנֵי בוּרְלָא מְשַׁקְּעָן מְרַמְּצָן דִּדְהָב גְּלִיפָן **כְּתַב מְפָרַשׁ** עַל שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


They placed the shoham stones in gold settings, They (the stones) were engraved like the engraving of a signet (ring) [**with script that is clear**], with the names of the B’nei Yisrael.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:7)


וְשַׁוִּי יָתְהוֹן עַל כִּתְפָּא דְאֵפוֹדָא אַבְנֵי דוּכְרָנָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He set them on the shoulder straps of the eiphod, as remembrance stones for the B’nei Yisrael, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:8)


וַעֲבַד יָת חוּשְׁנָא עוֹבַד אָמָּן כְּעוֹבָדֵי אֵפוֹדָא דַּהֲבָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָנָא **וּצְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר:


He made the breastplate the work of a craftsman, in the same manner the eiphod was made, (out of) gold, greenish-blue wool, dark red wool, crimson [**colored**] wool and twined, fine linen.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:9)


מְרַבַּע הֲוָה עֵיף עֲבָדוּ יָת חוּשְׁנָא זֶרְתָּא אֻרְכֵּיהּ וְזֶרְתָּא פְתָיֵהּ עֵיף:


They made the breastplate a doubled square, a span in its length and a span in its width.


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:10)


וְאַשְׁלִימוּ בֵיהּ אַרְבְּעָא סִדְרֵי אֶבֶן **טָבָא** סִדְרָא קַדְמָאָה סַמְקָן יַרְקָן וּבַרְקָן סִדְרָא **חָד**:


They filled them (the breastplate settings) with four rows of [**precious**] stones: The first row: An odem (ruby), pitda (topaz), and barekes (emerald), (was placed on) the first row [**one**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:11)


וְסִדְרָא תִּנְיָנָא אִזְמָרַגְדִּין שַׁבְזֵיז וְסַבְהֲלוֹם:


The second row: nofech (carbuncle), sapphire, and yahalom (diamond).


###### Onkelos Exodus 39:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:12)


וְסִדְרָא תְּלִיתָאָה קַנְכֵּרֵי טַרְקְיָא וְעֵין עֶגְלָא:


The third row: leshem (opal), shevo (agate), and achlomo (amethyst).

They made twisted end-chains for the breastplate, braided work of pure gold.

They made two gold settings, and two gold rings. They set the two gold rings at the two (upper) corners [**sides**] of the breastplate.

They put (attached) the two gold-braided chains on the two rings, at the corners [**sides**] of the breastplate.

the ark of the Testimony, its poles, its cover,

the pure Menorah, its lamps, the lamps to be set in order, all its vessels, oil for light,

the golden altar, the anointing oil, the incense of spices, the screen for the entrance of the Tent,

###### Onkelos Exodus 39:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/39:43)


וַחֲזָא משֶׁה יָת כָּל עִבִדְתָּא וְהָא עֲבָדוּ יָתַהּ כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ כֵּן עֲבָדוּ וּבָרִיךְ יָתְהוֹן משֶׁה:


Moshe saw the entire work, and behold, they did it just as Adonoy commanded, so had they done, and Moshe blessed them.



## Chapter 40

###### Onkelos Exodus 40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:2)


בְּיוֹמָא יַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא תְּקִים יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


“On the first day of the first month, you should set up the mishkon of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:3)


וּתְשַׁוִּי תַמָּן יָת אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא **וְתַטֵּל** עַל אֲרוֹנָא יָת פָּרֻכְתָּא:


Place the ark of Testimony there, and spread [**cover**] the paroches (partition) over the ark.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:4)


וְתָעֵל יָת פָּתוֹרָא וְתַסְדַּר יָת סִדְרֵיהּ וְתָעֵל יָת מְנַרְתָּא וְתַדְלֵק יָת בּוֹצִינָהָא:


Bring in the table and set it in order. Bring in the menorah and light its lamps.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:5)


וְתִתֵּן יָת מַדְבְּחָא דְדַהֲבָא לִקְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמַיָּא** קֳדָם אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וּתְשַׁוִּי יָת פְּרָסָא דְתַרְעָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא:


Place the golden altar for burning incense [**of spices**], before the ark of Testimony. Place the screen at the mishkon’s entrance.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:6)


וְתִתֵּן יָת מַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא קֳדָם תְּרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Place the burnt-offering altar in front of the entrance of the mishkon of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:7)


וְתִתֵּן יָת כִּיּוֹרָא בֵּין מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּבֵין מַדְבְּחָא וְתִתֵּן תַּמָּן מַיָּא:


Place the laver between the Tent of Meeting and the altar, and put water into it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:8)


וּתְשַׁוֵּי יָת דַּרְתָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר וְתִתֵּן יָת פְּרָסָא דִּתְרַע דַּרְתָּא:


Put up the enclosure all around, and set up the screen for the enclosure’s entrance.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:9)


וְתִסַּב יָת מִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וּתְרַבֵּי יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיָת כָּל דִּי בֵיהּ וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל מָנוֹהִי וִיהֵי קוּדְשָׁא:


Take the anointing oil, and anoint the mishkon and everything in it. Sanctify it and all its vessels so that it will become holy.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:10)


וּתְרַבֵּי יָת מַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא וְיָת כָּל מָנוֹהִי וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָת מַדְבְּחָא וִיהֵי מַדְבְּחָא קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין:


Anoint the burnt-offering altar and all its vessels. Sanctify the altar so that the altar becomes holy of holies.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:11)


וּתְרַבֵּי יָת כִּיּוֹרָא וְיָת בְּסִיסֵיהּ וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ:


Anoint the laver and its base and sanctify it.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:12)


וּתְקָרֵב יָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתַסְחֵי יָתְהוֹן בְּמַיָּא:


Bring Aharon and his sons to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, and immerse them in a mikveh.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:13)


וְתַלְבֵּשׁ יָת אַהֲרֹן יָת לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא וּתְרַבֵּי יָתֵיהּ וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ וִישַׁמֵּשׁ **קֳדָמָי**:


Clothe Aharon with the sacred garments. Anoint him and sanctify him so that he may serve as a kohein to [**before**] Me.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:14)


וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי תְּקָרֵב וְתַלְבֵּשׁ יָתְהוֹן כִּתּוּנִין:


Bring his sons and clothe them with undershirts.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:15)


וּתְרַבֵּי יָתְהוֹן כְּמָא דִי רַבֵּיתָא יָת אֲבוּהוֹן וִישַׁמְּשׁוּן **קֳדָמָי** וּתְהֵי לְמֵהֶוֵי לְהוֹן רְבוּתְהוֹן לְכַהֲנוּת עָלָם לְדָרֵיהוֹן:


Anoint them as you anointed their father, so that they may serve as kohanim to [**before**] Me. This should be done so that their anointing will make them an eternal kehunah throughout their generations.”


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:16)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה כְּכֹל דִּי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ כֵּן עֲבָד:


Moshe did according to all that Adonoy commanded him—so he did.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:17)


וַהֲוָה בְּיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה בְּשַׁתָּא תִנְיֵתָא בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא אִתָּקַם מַשְׁכְּנָא:


It was in the first month of the second year, on the first day of the month, that the mishkon was set up.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:18)


וַאֲקֵם משֶׁה יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא וִיהַב יָת סַמְּכוֹהִי וְשַׁוִּי יָת דַּפּוֹהִי וִיהַב יָת עַבְרוֹהִי וַאֲקֵם יָת עַמּוּדוֹהִי:


Moshe set up the mishkon, placed its sockets, put up its boards, put in its bars, and set up its pillars.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:19)


וּפְרַס יָת פְּרָסָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא וְשַׁוִּי יָת חוֹפָאָה דְמַשְׁכְּנָא עֲלוֹהִי מִלְּעֵלָּא כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He spread the tent over the mishkon and placed the tent’s covering above it, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:20)


וּנְסִיב וִיהַב יָת סַהֲדוּתָא לַאֲרוֹנָא וְשַׁוִּי יָת אֲרִיחַיָּא עַל אֲרוֹנָא וִיהַב יָת כַּפֻּרְתָּא עַל אֲרוֹנָא מִלְעֵלָּא:


He took the Testimony and placed it into the ark, put the poles on the ark, and put the cover on top of the ark.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:21)


וְאָעֵל יָת אֲרוֹנָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא וְשַׁוִּי יָת פָּרֻכְתָּא דִפְרָסָא וְאַטֵּל עַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He brought the ark into the mishkon and fastened the paroches (partition)-screen so that it covered the ark of Testimony, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:22)


וִיהַב יָת פָּתוֹרָא בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא עַל צִדָּא דְמַשְׁכְּנָא צִפּוּנָא מִבָּרָא לְפָרֻכְתָּא:


He placed the table in the Tent of Meeting on the north side of the mishkon, outside the paroches (partition).


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:23)


וְסַדַּר עֲלוֹהִי סִדּוּר דִּלְחֵם קֳדָם יְיָ כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He set upon it an arrangement of bread before Adonoy, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:24)


וְשַׁוִּי יָת מְנַרְתָּא בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָקֳבֵל פָּתוֹרָא עַל צִדָּא דְמַשְׁכְּנָא לְדָרוֹמָא:


He placed the Menorah in the Tent of Meeting, opposite the table, on the south side of the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:25)


וְאַדְלֵק בּוֹצִינָהָא קֳדָם יְיָ כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He lit the lamps before Adonoy, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:26)


וְשַׁוִּי יָת מַדְבְּחָא דְדַהֲבָא בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא קֳדָם פָּרֻכְתָּא:


He placed the golden altar inside the Tent of Meeting, in front of the paroches.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:27)


וְאַקְטַר עֲלוֹהִי קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He burned the incense of spices upon it, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:28)


וְשַׁוִּי יָת פְּרָסָא דְתַרְעָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא:


He set up the screen at the mishkon’s entrance.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:29)


וְיָת מַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא שַׁוִּי בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְאַסֵּק עֲלוֹהִי יָת עֲלָתָא וְיָת מִנְחָתָא כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He placed the burnt-offering altar at the entrance of the mishkon of the Tent of Meeting. He sacrificed on it a burnt-offering and a meal-offering, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:30)


וְשַׁוִּי יָת כִּיוֹרָא בֵּין מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּבֵין מַדְבְּחָא וִיהַב תַּמָּן מַיָּא **לְקִדּוּשׁ**:


He set the laver between the Tent of Meeting and the altar, and put water into it for washing [**sanctification**].


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:31)


**וִיקַדְּשׁוּן** מִנֵּיהּ משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי יָת יְדֵיהוֹן וְיָת רַגְלֵיהוֹן:


Moshe, Aharon and his sons washed [**would sanctify**] their hands and feet from it,


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:32)


בְּמֵעַלְהוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּבְמִקְרָבְהוֹן לְמַדְבְּחָא **יְקַדְּשׁוּן** כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


when they came to the Tent of Meeting and when they approached the altar they would wash [**sanctify themselves**], just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:33)


וַאֲקֵם יָת דַּרְתָּא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לְמַשְׁכְּנָא וּלְמַדְבְּחָא וִיהַב יָת פְּרָסָא דִּתְרַע דַּרְתָּא וְשֵׁיצֵי משֶׁה יָת עִבִידָא:


He set up the enclosure around the mishkon and the altar, and put up the screen at the enclosure’s entrance, and thus Moshe completed the work.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:34)


וַחֲפָא עֲנָנָא יָת מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וִיקָרָא דַיְיָ אִתְמְלִי יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא:


The cloud covered the Tent of Meeting, and the glory of Adonoy filled the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:35)


וְלָא יָכִיל משֶׁה לְמֵעַל לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא אֲרֵי שְׁרָא עֲלוֹהִי עֲנָנָא וִיקָרָא דַיְיָ אִתְמְלִי יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא:


Moshe was unable to enter the Tent of Meeting because the cloud rested on it, and the glory of Adonoy filled the mishkon.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:36)


וּבְאִסְתַּלָּקוּת עֲנָנָא מֵעִלָּוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא נָטְלִין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכֹל מַטְּלָנֵיהוֹן:


When the cloud rose up from over the mishkon, the B’nei Yisrael would set out in all their travels.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:37)


וְאִם לָא יִסְתַּלַּק עֲנָנָא וְלָא נָטְלִין עַד יוֹמָא דְּאִסְתַּלָּקוּתֵיהּ:


If the cloud did not rise, they would not set out until the day that it rose.


###### Onkelos Exodus 40:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Exodus/r/40:38)


אֲרֵי עֲנָנָא **יְקָרָא דַ**יְיָ עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא בִּימָמָא **וְחֵיזוּ** אֶשָּׁתָא הֲוֵי בְלֵילְיָא בֵּיהּ לְעֵינֵי כָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכֹל מַטְּלָנֵיהוֹן:


For the cloud of [**the Glory of**] Adonoy was upon the mishkon by day, and there was a [**vision of**] fire in it by night, before the eyes of the entire Beis Yisrael, in all their travels.
